We therefore introduce skytopia, a policy built on an action-conditioned latent world model, and the 3D Gaussian Splatting platform on which it is trained. A forward objective predicts the representation of the next observation from the intended motion, and an inverse objective recovers that motion from the predicted transition. Because the prediction never reaches action generation, the predictor is discarded and one policy serves point-goal, image-goal, and goal-free navigation. Simulation experiments show that skytopia outperforms every baseline under all three specifications, attaining 57.8%, 66.0%, and 49.0% success rate, while discarding the predictor removes 59.4% of the inference cost. The same policy is subsequently deployed on a physical drone without fine-tuning and reaches goals in indoor, open outdoor, and woodland environments.

</details>


### [169] [Towards Intent-Aware Human-Robot Teaming: A Platform for Search-and-Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26051)
*Rohith Prem Maben,Ayesha Jena,Björn Olofsson,Stefan Reitmann,Jacek Malec,Rogier Woltjer,Elin Anna Topp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个人机交互平台，用于在复杂动态环境中研究操作员行为，并基于先进框架支持意图推断与决策；在搜救场景中展示了自主系统对操作员感知、决策和行动的支持程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态环境下，人类操作员与异构自主智能体之间的有效协作面临挑战，需要理解操作员行为并提升意图推断与决策支持能力。

Method: 构建一个交互平台，允许研究操作员行为，并采用当前先进框架支持意图推断和决策制定。

Result: 在搜救任务中验证了该平台，展示了自主系统能够在多大程度上支持操作员的感知、决策和行动。

Conclusion: 该交互平台有助于研究并实现人机协作，并为自主系统在搜救等复杂任务中辅助人类操作员提供了实证依据。

Abstract: We investigate the challenges of enabling effective collaboration between human operators and heterogeneous autonomous agents in complex, dynamic environments by developing an interaction platform that allows study of operator behavior and supports intent inference and decision-making using state-of-the-art frameworks. We demonstrate the extent to which the operator's perception, decisions, and actions could be supported by autonomous systems during search-and-rescue operations with our platform.

</details>


### [170] [StrataVLA: Hierarchical and Efficient 3D Geometric Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26071)
*Jin Cui,Zhaoyu Pu,Botao Cai,Jun Ye,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StrataVLA——一种即插即用的分层几何接地框架，通过冻结的几何基础模型与稀疏分层几何适配器（Geometry Adapters），让视觉-语言骨干网络在多层级持续获取任务相关几何证据，并借助任务感知路由与 LRU 特征缓存降低推理成本，在 LIBERO 等基准上取得 98.53% 平均成功率并最多减少 88% 几何模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽从大规模视觉-语言预训练中继承了强语义先验，但在机器人操作中受限于缺乏足够的 3D 空间感知能力。现有方案存在明显不足：或需要显式深度/点云输入，或把几何信息压缩为仅训练期的监督信号，或只在模型输入端/动作专家处注入几何，导致视觉-语言骨干网络始终无法持续获得与任务相关的空间信息。

Method: 核心是"分层几何接地"：（1）使用冻结的几何基础模型，从 RGB 观测中提取共享几何特征；（2）在骨干网络的若干选定深度插入稀疏的、层级专属的 Geometry Adapters，通过交叉注意力让视觉表示检索相关几何证据；（3）为提升推理实用性，结合任务感知路由与基于 LRU 的特征缓存，利用操作过程中的时间冗余来减少几何模型调用。整体为即插即用设计，无需显式深度输入。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv 及真实世界操作任务上，相较强 VLA 基线均取得一致提升。在 LIBERO 套件上达到 98.53% 的平均成功率，同时将几何模型调用次数最多减少 88%。

Conclusion: 实验证明"分层几何注入"是一种有效且高效的方式，可在不引入显式深度输入的前提下实现具备空间接地能力的机器人控制，兼顾性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit strong semantic priors from large-scale vision-language pretraining, yet remain limited in robotic manipulation by insufficient 3D spatial awareness. Existing approaches either require explicit depth or point-cloud inputs, compress geometry into training-time supervision, or inject it only at the model input or action expert, leaving the vision-language backbone without persistent access to task-relevant spatial information. We introduce StrataVLA, a plug-and-play framework for hierarchical geometric grounding. A frozen geometry foundation model extracts shared geometric features from RGB observations, while sparse, layer-specific Geometry Adapters allow visual representations at selected backbone depths to retrieve relevant geometric evidence through cross-attention. To make inference-time geometry practical, StrataVLA further combines task-aware routing with an LRU feature cache that exploits temporal redundancy during task manipulation. Experiments on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over strong VLA baselines. StrataVLA achieves 98.53% average success on LIBERO suites while reducing geometry-model invocations by up to 88%, establishing hierarchical geometry injection as an effective and efficient way to achieve spatially grounded robotic control.

</details>


### [171] [Situation Aware Locomotion for Dual Mobile Cobots in Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26083)
*William Moraes,Igor Nunes,Ahilen Mazondo,Sebastian Barcelona,Hiago Sodre,Pablo Moraes,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向双移动协作机器人的情境感知运动框架，通过感知—理解—预测选择安全运动动作；仿真结果显示任务成功率100%，显著优于独立与固定优先级基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享工业环境中，两台移动协作机器人需兼顾负载、机械臂状态、共享区域占用与潜在冲突。独立策略或固定优先级策略难以适应动态场景，易导致冲突、安全停机与任务失败，因此需引入情境感知来提升运动决策的安全性与效率。

Method: 将情境感知建模为感知、理解、预测三个层次，并用于运动决策。输入包括机器人位姿、负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区域占用、障碍物状态及短期机器人间冲突预测，据此选择安全运动动作。在仿真中实现，场景设计匹配4 m×4 m物理测试区域，并与独立基线、固定优先级基线在多次试验和随机种子下进行对比。

Result: 情境感知方法在所有场景中达到100%任务成功率；独立与固定优先级基线各自总体成功率为33.3%。该方法消除了共享区域冲突与安全停机，保持最大的平均最小机器人间距，并实现最低的平均任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 融合负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区域推理与短时冲突预测的情境感知，能够有效改善双机器人运动的安全性与效率，并具备向实际物理测试区域迁移的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a situation aware locomotion framework for two mobile collaborative robots operating in shared industrial environments. The proposed method models situation awareness through perception, comprehension, and projection to support locomotion decisions. Robot pose, load state, manipulator state, shared zone occupancy, obstacle state, and predicted inter robot conflict were used to select safe locomotion actions. The framework was implemented in simulation and evaluated in simulated industrial scenarios designed to match a feasible 4 m by 4 m physical test area. The proposed method was compared with two other baselines over multiple trials and randomized seeds. The results show that the situation aware method achieved 100% task success across all scenarios, while the independent and fixed priority baselines each achieved 33.3% overall success. The proposed method eliminated shared zone conflicts and safety stops, maintained the largest average minimum inter robot distance, and completed the tasks with the lowest average completion time. These results indicate that situational awareness can improve the locomotion of dual robots by combining load state, manipulator state, reasoning about the shared zone, and prediction of short-horizon conflicts.

</details>


### [172] [Vision-Language Models as copilots for Autonomous UAV Navigation: Analysis of Latency and Reliability in Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26084)
*Hiago Sodre,Sebastian Barcelona,Vincent Sandin,Pablo Moraes,Ahilen Mazondo,Igor Nunes,William Moraes,André Kelbouscas,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于无GPS环境下无人机的混合FSM-VLM控制架构，将确定性有限状态机（FSM）负责底层物理控制与异步VLM副驾驶负责高层语义寻路相结合，通过SITL仿真评估三种不同参数规模的模型，发现参数规模而非纯延迟是VLM安全集成到自主飞行的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉语言模型（VLM）集成到自主无人机（UAV）中可带来前所未有的语义推理能力，但实时闭环导航不仅要求低推理延迟，还要求遵从结构化飞行指令。现有方案难以兼顾低延迟与指令合规性，因此需要新的控制架构来解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出混合FSM-VLM控制架构：用确定性有限状态机（FSM）执行低层物理控制，用异步VLM副驾驶完成高层语义寻路，二者解耦。在软件在环（SITL）仿真中评估三种不同参数规模的模型，并在正常与退化两种场景下，分别隔离并度量格式层的语法错误与逻辑层的语义幻觉。

Result: 在SITL仿真中对三种不同参数规模的模型进行了评估，框架成功隔离并测量了格式层语法错误与逻辑层语义幻觉；结果表明参数规模（而非纯延迟）是VLM安全、兼容地集成到自主飞行中的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 混合FSM-VLM架构可在无GPS环境下实现语义寻路与低层控制的解耦；参数规模是制约VLM安全集成的关键因素，未来应重点关注模型能力而非单纯优化延迟。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offers unprecedented semantic reasoning capabilities. However, real-time closed-loop navigation requires not only low inference latency but also obedience to structured flight commands. This paper proposes a hybrid FSM-VLM control architecture for UAVs in GPS-free environments. The system combines a deterministic Finite State Machine (FSM) for low-level physical control with an asynchronous VLM copilot for high-level semantic pathfinding. We evaluate three models with different parameter scales in a Software-In-The-Loop (SITL) simulation. The framework isolates and measures syntax errors at the format level versus semantic hallucinations at the logic level in a normal and degraded scenario. This study demonstrates that parameter scaling, and not pure latency, remains the primary bottleneck for the safe and compatible integration of VLM into autonomous flights.

</details>


### [173] [Acoustic Ellipses: Bio-Inspired Omnidirectional Echolocation in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems using Frequency Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26085)
*Petras Swissler,Lindsay Burke,Julia Hyland Bruno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文受鸟类飞行与鸣叫启发，提出一种协作式声学环境障碍物识别与建图方法：静止源代理发射频率调制啁啾信号，移动监听代理接收直达与反射信号，利用二者之间的“频率间隙”生成候选反射椭圆，并通过二维累积滤波定位高密度反射点。作者先以定制音频模拟器验证单监听配置下障碍物数量、采样率和路径曲率的影响，再研究双监听协作运动策略，最后在户外公园用现成硬件开展实地实验，证明低成本的群体机器人环境建图可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向群体机器人系统，现有环境建图方案往往依赖昂贵传感器或复杂计算。作者受鸟类飞行与鸣叫行为启发，希望利用简单的声学啁啾信号和协作代理，在低成本硬件条件下实现有效的障碍物识别与环境映射。

Method: 方法核心是：由静止源代理发射频率调制啁啾，一个或多个移动监听代理观测直达信号与经障碍物反射的信号；利用直达与反射啁啾之间的“频率间隙”定义候选反射椭圆，再将这些椭圆输入二维累积滤波器，以识别潜在反射密度最高的位置。验证分三阶段：1）使用定制的高效音频模拟器，在单监听场景下考察障碍物数量、采样率和运动路径曲率对识别精度的影响；2）研究双监听配置下的不同协作运动策略；3）在户外公园环境中使用现成硬件进行实地实验，验证频率间隙识别的实际可行性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，障碍物数量、采样率和路径曲率会影响单监听代理准确识别环境障碍物的能力；双监听配置下不同协作运动策略表现存在差异。户外公园实地实验证明，利用现成硬件可以识别直达与反射啁啾之间的频率间隙，验证了该方法在真实环境中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为群体机器人系统提供了一种低成本环境建图的基础方案，说明基于频率调制啁啾与协作监听代理的声学建图在仿真和真实场景中均具潜力，并为后续多代理协作策略与硬件部署研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Inspired by the flight and song of birds, we propose an approach that enables cooperating agents to effectively identify obstacles in their environment by having a stationary source agent emit a frequency-modulated chirp while one or more listener agents observe the direct and reflected signals while in motion. We present a novel mapping approach that exploits the ``frequency gap'' between direct and reflected chirp signals to define candidate reflection ellipses, which are then fed into a 2D accumulation filter to identify locations with the highest density of potential reflections.
  We demonstrate this work first with simulation results derived from an efficient, bespoke audio simulator, examining the effect of obstacle count, sampling rate, and path curvature on the ability to accurately identify environmental obstacles for a one-listener scenario. We then examine different cooperative motion strategies for two-listener configurations. Finally, we validate the real-world viability of this approach through field experiments in an outdoor park setting to demonstrate the ability to identify frequency gaps using off-the-shelf hardware. Our results provide a foundation for a low-cost approach to environmental mapping in swarm robotic systems.

</details>


### [174] [GDLAM: Group-Disentangled Latent Action Model for Highly Disentangled Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26118)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiawei Li,Jiale Zhang,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Maowei Hu,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GDLAM：将潜动作编码按构造分解为 N 个独立变分瓶颈与空间门控路由组，并用互斥性、组/门稀疏性、静态-动态正交等信息几何目标训练，从而在无动作视频中学习可解耦、可控且可迁移的潜动作表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜动作模型（LAM）通过无动作视频的自监督未来预测学习动作相关表示，但把相机运动、物体动力学和交互事件等异质视觉变化源压缩进单一潜向量，导致表示纠缠、语义结构不足，进而限制世界模型可控性与 VLA 策略泛化。

Method: GDLAM 在结构上将潜动作码分解为 N 个组，每组具备独立变分瓶颈和空间门控路由通路；训练采用信息几何目标：互斥性、组稀疏性、门稀疏性与静态-动态正交性，使各组在因果上相互区分，而非仅统计去相关。

Result: 干预任一组仅改变该组且不影响其他组；在无标签解耦指标 Modularity、MIG、DCI 上显著优于强非结构化 LAM。分组编码未牺牲动作信息：在三种互信息估计器和线性探针下，分布内与分布外均比单体基线更具信息量。迁移到下游：(1) 世界建模：基于 GDLAM 预训练的世界模型在 rollout 保真度和动作跟随能力上优于 SOTA；(2) VLA 策略：在多个仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务中，相较先前方法显著提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: GDLAM 通过分组解耦的潜动作表示，在保持甚至提升动作信息量的同时增强语义结构与可控性，并可作为可复用的具身智能预训练表示，有效提升世界模型与 VLA 策略的下游性能。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action-related representations from action-free videos via self-supervised future prediction, offering a scalable paradigm for embodied intelligence pretraining. However, existing LAMs collapse heterogeneous sources of visual change, including camera motion, object dynamics, and interaction events, into a single latent vector, resulting in entangled representations with limited semantic structure and consequently restricting world model controllability and VLA policy generalization. We introduce the Group-Disentangled Latent Action Model (GDLAM), a latent action model whose code is factorized by construction into N groups, each with an independent variational bottleneck and a spatially gated routing pathway, and trained with a set of information-geometric objectives: mutual exclusivity, group and gate sparsity, and static-dynamic orthogonality, that make the groups mutually causally distinct rather than merely decorrelated. Quantitatively, intervening on any single group changes only that group and leaves the others intact, and GDLAM improves label-free disentanglement metrics, including Modularity, MIG, and DCI, by wide margins over a strong unstructured LAM. Notably, this factorization is not at the expense of action information: across three mutual-information estimators and a linear probe, the grouped code is more informative than monolithic baselines both in- and out-of-distribution. As supporting evidence that the disentangled code is a reusable pretraining currency, we further transfer it to two downstream regimes: (1) World Modeling: World models pretrained with GDLAM achieve superior rollout fidelity and action-following capability compared with SOTA baselines. (2) VLA Policies: Pretraining with GDLAM substantially improves task success rates over previous methods across multiple simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks

</details>


### [175] [Design and Implementation of an Ultra-Low-Cost Wall-Climbing Robot for Infrastructure Crack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26130)
*Mrinmoy Modak,Supreyo Chakravorty Pretom,Shourv Tarafder,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本自动化裂缝检测机器人，结合YOLOv8区域检测与CNN/EfficientNet-B0裂缝分类，并采用风扇负压吸附、四轮滑移转向与ESP32-CAM，实现墙面/天花板巡检，总成本约25美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑与基础设施裂缝检测对安全性和寿命至关重要，但传统人工巡检受预算、劳动强度和安全风险限制，尤其发展中国家基础设施检测资源有限，因此需要低成本、可扩展、易获取的自动化方案。

Method: 系统分两阶段：第一阶段用YOLOv8从视频帧中检测并定位墙体区域，裁剪边界框；第二阶段对裁剪区域使用CNN、EfficientNet-B0或YOLOv8之一进行裂缝检测。硬件采用风扇负压吸附、四轮滑移转向驱动、ESP32-CAM实时图像采集，轻量化3D打印底盘；并提出漏斗形机身以增强负压生成。还构建了精选裂缝图像数据集用于训练与评估，并提供轻量Web应用支持手机控制。

Result: YOLOv8增强裂缝定位实时性，CNN与EfficientNet-B0提供二分类并保证较高精确率和召回率；漏斗形机身使占空比降低44%，显著降低功耗；机器人可在墙面和天花板稳定移动并采集图像；系统总成本约25美元。

Conclusion: 低成本硬件与深度学习流水线结合，为实时基础设施巡检提供了可扩展、高效且易获取的解决方案，适合预算受限的发展中地区，有助于降低检测成本、劳动强度与安全风险。

Abstract: Crack detection is a crucial process to ensure the safety and longevity of buildings and other infrastructure. In this paper, we developed a low-cost, automated crack detection robot that leverages CNN, EfficientNet-B0, and YOLOv8 for efficient identification of cracks in concrete surfaces with a curated crack image dataset introduced to support training and evaluation. YOLOv8's real-time object detection enhances crack localization, while CNN and EfficientNet-B0 provide binary classification, ensuring high precision and recall. The system consists of two stages. In the first stage, YOLOv8 detects and localizes wall regions from the video frame, and the bounding boxes are cropped. The second stage performs crack detection using one of three models by analyzing the cropped regions. Cost-effective approaches are also taken for robot design. The robot features a fan-based negative pressure adhesion system, a 4-wheeled skid-steering drive, and an ESP32-CAM for real-time image capture. Its lightweight 3D-printed chassis ensures stability, allowing it to navigate both walls and ceilings while capturing images for crack analysis. Unlike conventional wall-climbing robot designs, this robot incorporates a funnel-shaped body that enhances negative pressure generation and achieves a 44% reduction in duty cycle, significantly lowering power consumption. By combining low-cost hardware with a deep learning pipeline, our system provides a scalable, efficient, and accessible solution for real-time infrastructure inspection at an approximate total cost of $25, with a lightweight web application enabling smartphone-based control. This affordability makes the system more suitable for the developing world, where infrastructure inspection is often limited by budget constraints, labor intensity, and safety risks.

</details>


### [176] [StepTrigger: Contact-State-Triggered Backdoor Attacks on VLM-Powered Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26131)
*Jiageng Zhang,Doniyorkhon Obidov,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 StepTrigger，一种面向 VLM 驱动机器人规划器的接触状态触发后门攻击：触发器不是文本提示或视觉标记，而是四足机器人行走于密集地形时产生的足-地压力与接触模式。方法从多模态机器人状态中学习选择性后门策略，将偶发压力事件作为良性样本、密集地形接触作为投毒样本，离线评估显示其能较好保持干净行为并区分真假触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与视觉语言模型日益被用作机器人高层规划器，根据任务目标和传感器摘要选择导航或操作动作。现有针对 LLM/具身智能体的后门攻击主要依赖语言、可见物体、场景语义或动作历史序列等触发器，尚未充分关注本体感觉与接触通道这一潜在攻击面。

Method: StepTrigger 利用 Unitree Go1 四足机器人行走于密集地形时产生的足-地压力与接触模式作为触发器。由于接触信号本身含噪且良性运动中也可能出现压力异常，方法不将全部压力异常视为触发，而是从多模态机器人状态中学习选择性后门策略：以偶发压力事件作为良性样本，以密集地形接触作为投毒样本，训练被污染的规划器。

Result: 在分层离线评估中，训练后的规划器实现 98.75% 干净行为保持率、92.50% 假触发拒绝率、76.25% 真触发激活率，以及 89.17% 总体解析行为准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，本体感觉与接触通道中存在一种未被仅关注语言、视觉或动作历史的防御方法覆盖的后门攻击面。

Abstract: Large language models and vision-language models are increasingly used as high-level planners in robotic systems, using task goals and sensor summaries to select navigation or manipulation actions. This creates a new backdoor surface: a compromised planner can behave normally in most runs, yet change its target selection when a hidden trigger is present. Prior attacks on LLM-powered or embodied agents mainly rely on triggers that appear in language, camera-visible objects, scene semantics, or specific sequences of past actions. This paper presents StepTrigger, a contact-state-triggered backdoor attack for VLM-powered legged robots. The trigger is not a prompt token or a visible marker. It is produced by pressure and foot-ground contact patterns that arise when a Unitree Go1 quadruped walks across a dense terrain patch.
  Unlike conventional visual or textual triggers, contact signals are inherently noisy and may also arise during benign locomotion. To avoid treating every pressure anomaly as a trigger, StepTrigger learns a selective backdoor policy from multimodal robot state, using incidental pressure events as benign examples and dense-patch contacts as poisoned examples. In a stratified offline evaluation, the trained planner achieved 98.75% clean behavior preservation, 92.50% false-trigger rejection, 76.25% true-trigger activation, and 89.17% overall parsed behavior accuracy. These results reveal a backdoor surface in proprioceptive and contact channels that is not captured by defenses focused only on language, vision, or action history.

</details>


### [177] [Toward Self-Repairing Ubiquitous Robots Using Goal-Oriented Agentic AI in Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26155)
*Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向目标的智能体AI架构，通过情境对话使非专家用户完成技术维修任务，并在物理硬件维修任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泛在机器人系统通常缺乏传统可视化界面，自然语言交互对维护与维修任务至关重要。因此需要一种面向目标的智能体架构，支持非专家用户通过情境对话完成技术维修。

Method: 架构将交互前目标分解、持久状态跟踪、策略性目标管理和实时对话执行相分离。在物理硬件维修任务中对20名参与者进行系统评估。

Result: 19名参与者完成任务，完成率为95%。参与者认为系统有帮助且有能力，智能体对元查询和语码转换等对话干扰保持鲁棒。与先前在线基线相比，物理交互显著降低了感知社会存在感（p=.0005）以及信任与能力感（p=.037），但感知帮助性仍然较高。

Conclusion: 该目标导向智能体AI架构可有效支持非专家用户完成物理维修任务；在物理交互场景下，尽管社会存在感、信任与能力感相比在线基线显著下降，但系统仍保持较高的帮助性评价。

Abstract: Ubiquitous robotic systems often lack traditional visual interfaces, making natural language interaction important for maintenance and repair. This paper presents a goal-oriented agentic AI architecture that enables non-expert users to complete technical repair tasks through situated dialogue. The architecture separates pre-interaction goal decomposition, persistent state tracking, strategic goal management, and real-time conversational execution. We evaluated the system in a physical hardware repair task with twenty participants. Nineteen participants completed the task, corresponding to a 95% completion rate. Participants rated the system as helpful and competent, and the agent remained robust to conversational diversions such as meta-queries and code-switching. A comparison with a prior online baseline showed that physical interaction significantly reduced perceived social presence, (p=.0005), and trust and competence, (p=.037), while perceived helpfulness remained high.

</details>


### [178] [Silent Sabotage: Internal State Triggered Backdoor Attacks on LLM-Powered Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26184)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Shivayogi Akki,Tan Chen,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次系统研究LLM驱动机器人系统中"基于历史"的后门攻击：攻击者通过操纵控制器指令植入后门，其触发器为机器人自身过去动作的稀有序列；该后门正常时休眠、保持效用，激活后引发完全停止或碰撞等恶意行为；模拟实验中对多种机器人与多种LLM均实现近乎完美的攻击成功率，且极难被检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与机器人控制的融合带来具备复杂推理与规划能力的新一代自主智能体，同时也引入了尚未被充分探索的新型安全风险。现有LLM后门研究主要聚焦于由外部刺激（特定词语、视觉物体、环境状态）触发的攻击，忽略了触发信号内生于智能体自身操作逻辑这类更隐蔽、更危险的漏洞类别。

Method: 提出并研究"基于历史的后门攻击"：通过操纵LLM机器人控制器的指令嵌入隐蔽后门，其触发条件为机器人自身过去动作的特定稀有序列，而非外部线索。在含多种机器人和多种LLM的模拟环境中开展实验验证。

Result: 该基于历史的攻击具有很高有效性：攻击成功率接近完美，同时检测难度极高。后门在正常运行期间保持休眠，不损害机器人效用，仅在触发序列出现时激活，诱发完全停止或碰撞等恶意行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自主系统中一个关键且此前未被处理的漏洞，表明安全防护必须考虑智能体的内部状态（动作历史），对LLM驱动机器人系统的后门防御提出了迫切需求。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into robotic control systems is enabling a new generation of autonomous agents capable of complex reasoning and planning. While this paradigm shift accelerates progress, it also introduces novel security risks that remain largely unexplored. Current research into LLM backdoors has focused on attacks triggered by external stimuli, such as specific words, visual objects, or environmental states. These attacks, while potent, overlook a more insidious class of vulnerability where the trigger is internal to the agent's own operational logic. This paper presents the first comprehensive study of history-based backdoor attacks on LLM-powered robotic systems. We demonstrate that an attacker can embed a stealthy backdoor into an LLM-based robot controller by manipulating its instructions. This backdoor is triggered not by an external cue, but by a specific, rare sequence of the robot's own past actions. It remains dormant during normal operation, preserving the robot's utility, but can be activated to induce a malicious behavior, such as a complete stop or a collision. Our experiments, conducted in a simulated environment with a variety of robots and LLMs, show that this history-based attack is highly effective, achieving a near-perfect attack success rate while remaining exceptionally difficult to detect. These findings reveal a critical and previously unaddressed vulnerability in autonomous systems and underscore the urgent need for security measures that account for an agent's internal state.

</details>


### [179] [Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects Using Model Predictive Path Integral Control with Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26238)
*Lukas Zeh,Johannes Meiwaldt,Zexu Zhou,Armin Lechler,Alexander Verl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于机器学习的DLO操作最优控制方法：用biLSTM学习线缆动力学，结合MPPI模型预测控制，仿真与实验验证其有效性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（DLO，如线缆）具有无限自由度与非线性动力学，传统建模与控制困难，给自动化操作带来显著挑战。

Method: 将方法分为建模与控制两部分：建模采用双向长短期记忆网络（biLSTM），使用MuJoCo物理引擎生成的合成数据进行训练；控制采用基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的模型预测控制策略来操作DLO。

Result: 通过仿真与实验进行评估，结果表明该方法能够实现准确且高效的DLO操作。

Conclusion: 所提出的“biLSTM建模 + MPPI控制”机器学习最优控制框架对DLO操作有效，具备较好的准确性与效率。

Abstract: The manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as cables poses a significant challenge for automation due to their infinite degrees of freedom and non-linear dynamics. In this paper we present a machine learning based optimal control approach for the manipulation of DLOs. This approach is divided into two main components: modeling and control. For modeling the dynamics of the DLO, we propose a learning based approach using a bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (biLSTM) network. The biLSTM network is trained on synthetic data generated by the MuJoCo physics engine. For manipulating the DLO, a model predictive control strategy that employs Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is selected. The proposed approach is evaluated through simulation and experiments. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in achieving accurate and efficient manipulation of DLOs.

</details>


### [180] [TriWorldBench: A Tri-View Consistency Perspective on Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26314)
*Xuanyi Liu,Haofeng Wang,Ruiqi Li,Danni Yu,Rui Wan,Ruixu Zhang,Siyu Tao,Xue Yang,Shaofeng Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TRIWORLDBENCH：一个用同步“头部+左腕+右腕”三视角视频评估具身世界模型的基准，包含 50 个双臂操作任务、500 条 episode 和 19 项指标，并给出 TWB-Score 以汇总整体表现、保留分视角结果定位失败来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型需预测机器人动作结果以支持学习与规划。对配备头部与腕部相机的机器人而言，头部视角提供全局任务信息，腕部视角揭示夹爪—物体局部交互，二者互补；但独立评估各视角无法判断它们是否描述了同一动作与同一物体状态，因此缺少面向多视角一致性的统一评测方法。

Method: 构建 TRIWORLDBENCH 基准，使用同步的头部、左腕、右腕视频进行评估；数据集涵盖 50 个双臂操作任务、500 条 episode；设计 19 项指标，覆盖三视角一致性、任务对齐、物理与 3D 一致性、运动质量、时序一致性和视觉质量；将跨视角检查与针对各相机定制的度量结合，并用 TWB-Score 汇总整体性能，同时保留每视角结果以定位预测失败位置。

Result: 该基准能够评估“单个视角看似合理的视频”是否同时构成对目标任务的连贯、一致的预测，并可识别预测在哪些视角或维度上失败；评估范围由此从单视角视觉质量扩展到跨视角任务一致性。代码、数据与指标定义已在 GitHub 公开。

Conclusion: TRIWORLDBENCH 为具身世界模型提供了多视角、任务导向的评测框架，推动评估从单一视觉质量转向同步三视角下的一致性、物理合理性与任务对齐，为后续世界模型学习与规划提供更可靠的衡量标准。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the outcomes of robot actions to support learning and planning. For robots equipped with head and wrist cameras, this requires complementary views: the head view captures the overall task, while wrist views reveal local gripper-object interactions. However, evaluating these views independently cannot determine whether they describe the same action and object state. We introduce TRIWORLDBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating embodied world models through synchronized head, left-wrist, and right-wrist videos. It contains 500 episodes across 50 bimanual manipulation tasks and uses 19 metrics to assess tri-view consistency, task alignment, physical and 3D coherence, motion quality, temporal consistency, and visual quality. By combining cross-view checks with measurements tailored to each camera, the benchmark evaluates whether plausible individual videos also form a consistent prediction of the intended task. We summarize overall performance with TWB-Score and retain per-view results to identify where predictions fail. This extends world-model evaluation beyond single-view visual quality. Code, data, and metric definitions are available at https://github.com/TriWorldBench/TriWorldBench.

</details>


### [181] [ArborSplat: Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Orchards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26315)
*Alessandro Masini,Matteo Frosi,Mirko Usuelli,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArborSplat：面向果园机器人的在线语义3DGS SLAM系统，通过LiDAR里程计跟踪，在高度带与地平面约束下直接在高斯地图上优化语义，实现高精度、高效率的语义建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园机器人需要保留树干、棚架、果实等细小但语义重要的结构的地图。现有3DGS SLAM虽具高光度保真度，但其优化以表观驱动为主，将图像语义迁移到3D点对细结构不可靠——细结构的像素可能从背景表面获得深度，导致语义标注错误。

Method: 提出ArborSplat在线语义3DGS SLAM：1）用LiDAR里程计进行跟踪；2）直接在高斯地图上优化语义，受每个关键帧立体点云拟合的地平面之上的类别特定高度带约束；3）在线将多视角证据融合为语义点云，同时剔除以局部地表面或单目深度不一致的标签；4）类别约束细化将高斯容量保留给欠表达结构。

Result: 在苹果与梨园（休眠期、花期、采收期）评估：全程路线上，12条轨迹的ATE均低于0.5 m；在共享301帧片段上，相比SGS-SLAM和GS3LAM，训练视角mIoU提升0.23–0.50、留出集mIoU提升0.15–0.36，速度快1.7–7.5倍；而SemGauss-SLAM在全部6个片段上均耗尽GPU显存。

Conclusion: ArborSplat通过地平面高度带约束与多视角语义融合，实现了对果园细结构的高精度在线语义建图，在精度与效率上均显著优于现有语义3DGS SLAM方法，且显存占用更具优势。

Abstract: Orchard robots need maps that preserve small but semantically important structures such as trunks, trellises, and fruit. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM achieves high photometric fidelity. However, its optimization remains appearance-driven, and transferring image semantics to 3D points is unreliable for thin structures, whose pixels may receive depth from background surfaces. We present ArborSplat, an online semantic 3DGS SLAM system that tracks with LiDAR odometry and optimizes semantics directly on the Gaussian map, constrained by class-specific height bands above a ground plane fitted to each keyframe's stereo point cloud, and fuses multi-view evidence into a semantic point cloud online while rejecting labels inconsistent with the local ground surface or with monocular depth. Class-constrained refinement reserves Gaussian capacity for underrepresented structures and, under reduced budgets, increases training-view accuracy on tree classes. We evaluate the approach on apple and pear orchards during dormancy, flowering, and harvesting. On full routes, it keeps ATE below 0.5 m on all 12 traversals. On shared 301-frame segments, it exceeds SGS-SLAM and GS3LAM by 0.23 to 0.50 training-view and 0.15 to 0.36 held-out mIoU while running 1.7 to 7.5 times faster, whereas SemGauss-SLAM runs out of GPU memory on all six.

</details>


### [182] [Hierarchical Floorplan-Guided Vision-Language Exploration for Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26360)
*Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HFLEX-EQA 分层具身问答框架，将在线场景图构建、VLM 规划、语义前沿探索与户型图先验相结合，在 OpenEQA、ExploreEQA 基准及真实四足机器人上验证了结构化先验的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身问答（EQA）需智能体在未知环境中探索、收集信息并回答场景相关问题。现有基于 VLM 配合语义地图或场景图的方法，其探索过程通常仅由局部观测驱动，环境的结构先验（如空间布局）大多未被利用，导致探索效率和相关性不足。

Method: HFLEX-EQA 是一个分层 EQA 框架。系统从 RGB-D 观测中增量构建分层场景图与开放词汇占据地图；VLM 联合推理场景图、任务相关视觉观测、探索历史以及估计得到的拓扑户型图进行规划。此外引入房间发现策略，利用户型图与开放词汇前沿语义，引导探索朝向语义相关但尚未观测的房间类型；并结合语义前沿探索。

Result: 在 OpenEQA 与 ExploreEQA 两个基准上进行评估，并在真实室内环境中的四足机器人上完成部署验证。结果表明，将基于 VLM 的分层规划与结构化户型图先验相结合，对 EQA 任务具有明显收益。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 分层规划与环境结构先验（户型图）融合，能有效提升具身问答的探索与问答性能；分层场景图、开放词汇地图与房间发现策略共同支撑了该框架的有效性，并在仿真基准与真实机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Embodied Question Answering (EQA) requires an agent to explore a previously unseen environment, gather relevant information, and answer questions about the scene. Recent approaches leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with semantic maps or scene graphs to guide exploration. However, exploration is typically driven only by local observations, while structural priors about the environment remain largely unused. We propose HFLEX-EQA, a hierarchical EQA framework that combines online scene graph construction, VLM- based planning, semantic frontier exploration, and floorplan priors. The system incrementally builds a hierarchical scene graph and an open-vocabulary occupancy map from RGB-D observations, enabling a VLM to jointly reason over the scene graph, task-relevant visual observations, exploration history, and an estimated topological floorplan. Furthermore, we introduce a room-discovery strategy that leverages the floorplan and open-vocabulary frontier semantics to guide exploration toward semantically relevant yet currently unobserved room types. We evaluate HFLEX-EQA on the OpenEQA and ExploreEQA benchmarks and demonstrate deployment on a quadruped robot in real indoor environments. Our results demonstrate the benefit of combining VLM-based hierarchical planning with structural floorplan priors for the EQA task.

</details>


### [183] [MAVP: Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26378)
*Jinhe Tang,Ruixiao Dai,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAVP 通过预测显式基座位姿目标并用定位反馈跟踪，提升移动操作策略的执行可靠性，在六项真实任务中全面超越速度控制基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需要底盘与机械臂协同运动并保持精确的空间定位，但仅由示教数据训练的策略往往难以可靠实现预期底盘运动，导致空间错位进而引发操作失败。因此需要一种能提供一致空间监督、并能在执行中纠偏的框架。

Method: MAVP 先从遥操作示教中重建静态地图，将示教的底盘轨迹表达在统一的地图坐标系中，从而获得跨示教一致的空间监督。执行时策略输入 RGB 观测、关节状态以及当前地图系底盘位姿，联合预测目标底盘位姿、手臂动作与夹爪动作；底层控制器以“前馈运动+位姿误差反馈”跟踪预测的底盘目标以纠正执行偏差。训练阶段还引入位姿噪声增强，提升策略对位姿输入误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在六项真实世界操作任务、三种策略族上，MAVP 在所有任务中的任务成功率均高于未锚定的速度控制基线。

Conclusion: 以地图坐标系为锚、显式预测并反馈跟踪底盘位姿目标，可有效缓解示教策略的底盘执行偏差，是提升移动操作可靠性的通用有效方案。

Abstract: Successful mobile manipulation requires coordinated base and arm motion while maintaining accurate spatial positioning. However, demonstration-trained policies can struggle to realise the intended base motion reliably, leading to spatial misalignment and subsequent manipulation failures. We present MAVP (Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies), a framework that improves execution reliability by predicting explicit base-pose targets and tracking them using localisation feedback. MAVP reconstructs a static map from teleoperated demonstrations and expresses demonstrated base trajectories in a shared map frame, providing consistent spatial supervision across demonstrations. At execution time, the policy receives RGB observations, joint states, and the robot's current map-frame base pose, and jointly predicts target base poses, arm actions, and gripper actions. A low-level controller tracks the predicted base targets using feedforward motion and pose error feedback, enabling correction of execution deviations. We additionally use pose-noise augmentation during training to improve robustness to errors in the policy's pose input. Across six real-world manipulation tasks and three policy families, MAVP achieves higher task success rates than unanchored velocity control in all tasks. Videos and additional results are available at https://123qwedsa123.github.io/mavp/.

</details>


### [184] [SparseNav: Instruction-conditioned Sparse Semantic Perception for Training-Free Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26408)
*Quanhua Chen,Juhan Kang,Runfeng Lin,ZiFei Zhang,Enquang Feng,Chunran Zheng,Xiwang Dong,Jiarong Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SparseNav：免训练、按需感知的稀疏语义地图VLN框架，在R2R-CE/RxR-CE Val-Unseen达42.8%/40.7%成功率，并部署于Unitree Go2四足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于地图的视觉语言导航依赖持久空间表征，但超出当前指令需求的语义获取会带来多余感知开销与无关标注；持续累积无关物体既浪费计算，又会干扰VLM规划器所消费的视觉-空间表征。

Method: 提出训练-free的SparseNav，遵循"少即是多"原则：持久维护轻量几何BEV地图与稀疏地标记忆；指令管理器跟踪导航进度并识别当前活跃地标查询；指令条件感知机制仅在查询地标可见且其度量位置能辅助下一步决策时调用开放词汇分割；地标记忆支持VLM在混合前沿与局部方向航点候选间选择。

Result: 无需额外训练，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE的Val-Unseen划分上成功率分别为42.8%和40.7%；通过控制消融检验语义感知策略与各组件贡献；在Unitree Go2四足机器人上部署，配备Intel RealSense D455 RGB-D用于几何建图与地标定位、Livox MID-360 LiDAR用于定位，无需预建地图，并在多个室内环境中验证指令条件航点导航的有效性。

Conclusion: SparseNav表明按需稀疏语义感知可降低感知与标注成本并保持有效导航性能，且具备真实机器人零预建地图部署能力。

Abstract: Map-based vision-language navigation (VLN) relies on persistent spatial representations to connect language understanding with geometric planning. However, acquiring semantics beyond the needs of the current instruction can introduce unnecessary perception cost and irrelevant annotations. Continuously accumulating unrelated objects may not only waste computation, but also clutter the visual-spatial representation consumed by the vision-language model (VLM) planner. To address this problem, we present SparseNav, a training-free framework that follows a less-is-more principle for semantic navigation. SparseNav persistently maintains a lightweight geometric bird's-eye-view (BEV) map and sparse landmark memory, acquiring new semantics on demand using the active sub-instruction to decide what is worth grounding. An instruction manager first tracks navigation progress and identifies the active landmark query. An instruction-conditioned perception mechanism then invokes open-vocabulary segmentation when the queried landmark is visible and its metric location can inform the next decision. The resulting landmark memory supports VLM selection among hybrid frontier and local directional waypoint candidates. Without any additional training, SparseNav achieves success rates of 42.8% on R2R-CE and 40.7% on RxR-CE, both on the Val-Unseen splits. Controlled ablations examine semantic perception strategies and the contributions of individual framework components. Furthermore, we successfully deployed SparseNav on a Unitree Go2 quadruped equipped with an Intel RealSense D455 RGB-D camera for geometric mapping and landmark grounding and a Livox MID-360 LiDAR for localization, without a prebuilt map. We validated its effectiveness across multiple indoor environments using instruction-conditioned waypoint navigation.

</details>


### [185] [Sample, Simulate, Select: Physics-in-the-Loop Text-to-Motion for Humanoids Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26420)
*Raphael Memmesheimer,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 S^3（sample-simulate-select）框架：用冻结的文本到动作模型采样多个候选动作，经方向匹配逆运动学重定向到 Unitree G1，再由预训练 SONIC 全身跟踪策略在完整刚体动力学下仿真执行并择优，从而在部署控制器在环的条件下缩小文本生成动作与机器人动力学之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到动作模型能生成看似合理的人体动作，但不建模机器人动力学；全身跟踪控制器能可靠执行给定参考，却无法对不可行参考重新规划。既有语言到人形机器人系统依赖训练来弥合该差距，本文希望量化：完全不做训练、仅把部署控制器本身放入回路，能弥合多少差距。

Method: S^3 对每个 prompt 从冻结文本到动作模型采样 N 个动作；用 direction-matching 逆运动学分别重定向到 Unitree G1；在完整刚体动力学下用预训练 SONIC 跟踪策略对全部候选进行 rollout；保留策略执行效果最好的候选。由于验证器就是确定性仿真器本身，S^3 在构造上达到 any-of-N 上限，因此研究的重点是该上限位置及未达上限的原因。另用运动学验证器预测摔倒，并与 GMR 重定向器做消融。

Result: 在 200 个分层 HumanML3D 测试 prompt、N=8 时，直立执行率从 83.5% 升至 89.5%，硬件门控通过数从 33 升至 85；完整测试集 4,184 prompts 上从 80.5% 升至 89.5%。运动学验证器预测摔倒 AUROC 0.90，但仅恢复约四分之一增益，说明对同一 prompt 内部候选排序比总体分类更难。选择无法修复的是“压低骨盆”这一类 prompt，而用重定向机器人数据训练的生成器可以执行。语义保真度用标准 text-motion 评估器打分，并用真实 mocap 对照将损失归因于机器人投影；与 GMR 消融显示互补失败模式，两者合并 any-of-8 上限升至 95.0%；177 个门控选中片段在真实 G1 上全部完成站立，硬件跟踪误差与仿真一致（r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 免训练地在回路中使用部署控制器作为验证器，可显著提升文本驱动人形动作的可执行性（尤其硬件门控通过率），并在真实机器人上保持与仿真一致的跟踪误差；但候选选择存在固有局限，无法修复特定失败类别，需结合重定向器多样性与针对性生成器训练。

Abstract: Text-to-motion models generate plausible human motion but do not model a robot's dynamics; whole-body tracking controllers execute robot references reliably but cannot replan an infeasible one. Recent language-to-humanoid systems bridge this gap by training. We measure how much of the gap closes with no training at all, by putting the deployment controller itself in the loop. Sample-simulate-select (S$^3$) draws $N$ motions per prompt from a frozen text-to-motion model, retargets each to a Unitree G1 by direction-matching inverse kinematics, rolls all of them out under full rigid-body dynamics with the pretrained SONIC tracking policy, and keeps the candidate the policy executed best. Because the verifier is the deterministic simulator itself, S$^3$ attains the any-of-$N$ ceiling by construction; what we measure is where that ceiling lies and what falls short of it. On 200 stratified HumanML3D test prompts with $N=8$, upright execution rises from 83.5% to 89.5% and hardware-gate passes from 33 to 85; on the complete test split (4,184 prompts) it rises from 80.5% to 89.5%. A kinematic verifier that predicts falls well (AUROC 0.90) recovers only a quarter of this gain: ranking a prompt's own candidates is harder than classifying the population. What selection cannot fix is one class, prompts that lower the pelvis, which a generator trained on retargeted robot data does execute. We further score the semantic fidelity of the executed motion with the standard text-motion evaluator, with a real-mocap control that attributes the loss to the robot projection, ablate the retargeter against GMR (complementary failures: the any-of-8 ceiling rises to 95.0% over both), and execute all 177 gate-selected clips on the real G1: every one completes standing, with hardware tracking error matching simulation ($r=0.94$).

</details>


### [186] [Dr-LiSA: Direct Radar-Lidar Scan Alignment for $SE(3)$ Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26423)
*Alex Zhang,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dr-LiSA——首个在SE(3)下将2D旋转雷达强度测量直接定位到3D激光雷达地图的方法。其核心是用学习的前向模型从候选位姿处的激光子图预测雷达测量，从而在SE(3)中直接对齐预测与观测雷达扫描。在超过90 km道路数据上，其SE(2)定位优于已有雷达-激光方法，平面精度可与最先进雷达-雷达定位竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达-激光定位可融合两类传感器优势：雷达对恶劣天气和降水稳健，激光在有利条件下可提供高保真3D地图。但现有雷达-激光定位方法局限于平面SE(2)，精度普遍不及激光-激光甚至雷达-雷达系统。关键挑战在于雷达与激光之间存在显著的传感模态差异，二者对场景结构的观测与表征方式根本不同。

Method: Dr-LiSA采用学习的前向模型桥接雷达-激光模态差异：给定候选位姿，由激光子图预测对应雷达测量；进而对预测雷达扫描与观测雷达扫描进行直接光度对齐，在SE(3)中完成定位。

Result: 在超过90 km的实车道路数据上，Dr-LiSA在SE(2)定位中优于先前雷达-激光方法，同时其平面定位精度与当前最先进的雷达-雷达定位方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Dr-LiSA首次实现SE(3)下的雷达-激光直接定位，证明学习前向模型可有效桥接雷达与激光的传感模态差异，将雷达-激光定位从平面SE(2)拓展到三维位姿估计，并保持与先进雷达-雷达方法相当的平面精度。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-LiSA, a first-of-its-kind direct method for localizing 2D spinning radar intensity measurements in $SE(3)$ against 3D lidar maps. Radar-lidar localization combines the complementary strengths of the two sensing modalities: radar is robust to adverse weather and precipitation, while lidar provides high-fidelity 3D maps in favourable conditions. However, existing radar-lidar localization methods are restricted to planar $SE(2)$ localization and have generally fallen short of the accuracy achieved by lidar-lidar and even radar-radar systems. A key challenge is the substantial sensing-modality gap between radar and lidar, which observe and represent scene structure in fundamentally different ways. Dr-LiSA bridges this gap using a learned forward model that predicts radar measurements from a lidar submap at a candidate pose, enabling direct photometric alignment of predicted and observed radar scans in $SE(3)$. Dr-LiSA outperforms prior radar-lidar approaches in $SE(2)$ while achieving planar accuracy competitive with state-of-the-art radar-radar localization across more than 90 km of on-road data.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond End-Task Success: How to Audit Visual Experience Retrieval in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26567)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套"审计"方法：在每个查询场景中穷尽执行所有已存经验，从而把复用策略的得分拆解为"逐场景选择质量"与"经验库质量"两部分，并在两个操作任务、三种复用机制及 K=3/10/50 的经验库上对五种视觉嵌入（从原始像素到 CLIP）的最近邻选择规则进行评测。结论是：视觉相似度选经验的能力被高估——一个事后选出的固定经验即可占满大部分性能差距，而逐场景选择贡献甚微，且视觉距离在场景内排序候选时几近随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通过复用过往经验而非重新训练来适应新任务，因此"选择哪条经验复用"至关重要。然而现有系统多以视觉相似度进行选择，评估时又只报告"所选经验"的成功率。该数值无法反映选择规则本身的好坏：规则可能因反复使用一条广泛可迁移的经验而获得高分，也可能因其偏好的经验本身很弱而得分低。当得分描述的是经验库而非选择规则时，会误导后续研究方向的判断，故需要一个能区分二者的评估框架。

Method: 提出一种审计式评估方法：在每一个查询场景中穷尽执行全部已存经验，覆盖两个操作任务、三种复用机制，经验库规模 K=3、10、50。由于每个备选经验的结果都已知，得分可被追溯归因到逐场景的选择质量或经验库整体质量。被审计的选择规则采用五种视觉嵌入（从原始像素到 CLIP）中的最近邻距离进行选择，并与随机选择、oracle、保持选择率但随机配对场景的 shuffle 基线对比。

Result: (1) 一条事后选定的固定经验即可弥合随机选择与 oracle 之间 30-58% 的性能差距，而逐场景选择仅竞争剩余 0.07-0.15 的成功率空间。(2) 当 K≥10 时，视觉规则对单一经验的集中程度是 oracle 的 1.5-3 倍，其得分随之由该经验的质量决定。(3) 只要规则显著偏离 shuffle 基线，规则就更差，且对所有学习到的图像策略都成立。(4) 视觉距离能较好地预测给定经验-场景对是否会成功（AUROC 最高 0.96），但在单个场景内对候选排序却不优于随机——五种嵌入中有四种在 K=50 时 AUROC 仅 0.45-0.52。

Conclusion: 穷尽执行在实践中通常不可行，因此该审计可简化为任何研究都能提供的两份低成本报告：被选经验的分布，以及事后最优单一经验的成功率。二者结合即可揭示选择规则的优劣，避免用描述经验库的分数误导复用型机器人的研究。

Abstract: Robots that store past experiences must select which one to reuse in a new scene. Most systems select by visual similarity, and most evaluations report only the success of the selected experience. That number does not show whether the selection was good: a rule can score well by repeatedly using one broadly transferable experience, or poorly because its preferred experience is weak. Since robots increasingly adapt by reuse rather than retraining, a score that describes the library rather than the rule misleads what the field builds next. We contribute an audit methodology: execute every stored experience in every query scene, over two manipulation tasks, three reuse mechanisms, and libraries of $K=3$, $10$, and $50$. Because every alternative's outcome is known, a score can be traced to per-scene selection or to library quality. The audited rules select by nearest-neighbor distance in five visual embeddings, from raw pixels to CLIP. (1) One fixed experience, chosen with hindsight, captures 30-58% of the gap between random selection and an oracle; per-scene selection competes for the remaining 0.07-0.15 in success rate. (2) At $K\ge10$, visual rules concentrate on one experience 1.5-3 times more than the oracle does, and their scores then follow that experience's quality. (3) Wherever a rule differs significantly from a shuffle that keeps its selection rates but pairs them with scenes at random, the rule is worse, for every learned image policy. (4) Visual distance predicts well whether a given pair will succeed (AUROC up to 0.96), yet ranks the candidates within one scene no better than chance for four of five embeddings at $K=50$ (AUROC 0.45-0.52). Exhaustive execution is usually infeasible, so the audit reduces to two cheap reports any study can give: the distribution of selected experiences, and the success of the best single experience in hindsight.

</details>


### [188] [Wheel-loader V-Cycle Automation with Deep Koopman MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26580)
*Armin Abdolmohammadi,Navid Mojahed,Dinesh Kumar,Bahram Ravani,Shima Nazari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架：长时域几何规划 + 数据驱动预测控制，实现轮式装载机自主V型作业循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式装载机在土方作业中反复前进-倒车，适合自动化；但铰接车辆非线性动力学与复杂车辆-地形交互使传统基于模型的方法效果受限。

Method: 采用降阶铰接运动学模型生成操作几何，通过共享中间状态联合优化前进与倒车轨迹；基于Algoryx Dynamics高保真仿真数据，分别学习前进与倒车的两个数据驱动深度双线性Koopman模型，并将其嵌入计算高效的MPC中进行轨迹跟踪。

Result: 控制器可在50 ms执行循环内实时运行；高保真仿真表明，端到端框架能够准确且计算高效地完成轮式装载机V型循环作业。

Conclusion: 该分层、数据驱动框架为铰接重型机械自主作业提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: The repeated forward-reverse maneuvers performed by wheel loaders during earthmoving operations make them well suited for automation. However, the nonlinear dynamics of articulated vehicles and complex vehicle-terrain interactions limit the effectiveness of conventional model-based approaches. This paper presents a hierarchical framework that combines long-horizon geometric planning with data-driven predictive control for autonomous wheel-loader operation. A reduced-order articulated kinematic model is used to generate the maneuver geometry, where the forward and reverse trajectories are jointly optimized through a shared intermediate state. To capture the vehicle dynamics, two data-driven deep bilinear Koopman models are learned for the forward and reverse motions using data generated from high-fidelity simulations in Algoryx Dynamics. The learned Koopman representations are subsequently incorporated into a computationally efficient model predictive control (MPC) formulation for trajectory tracking. The resulting controller operates in real time within a 50-ms execution loop. High-fidelity simulation results demonstrate that the proposed end-to-end framework enables accurate and computationally efficient execution of wheel-loader V-cycle maneuvers, providing a promising approach toward autonomous operation of articulated heavy-duty machinery.

</details>


### [189] [DreamStream: Towards Policy-Oriented Generative Simulation for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26792)
*Ziyang Leng,Sicheng Mo,Seth Z. Zhao,Haoyuan Cai,Yu Zeng,Rowan McAllister,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamStream：面向策略保真度的生成式闭环驾驶模拟器，用交通布局引导的自回归视频模型蒸馏大规模预训练视频模型，并引入FD$π$度量来评估仿真实真差距，进而构建Navhard-CL交互式基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略的闭环评估要求仿真观测不仅照片级真实，还要保留策略决策所依赖的场景特征。现有仿真平台存在仿真到真实的视觉差距，会破坏策略感知，从而无法可靠评估闭环决策能力。此外，FID等感知度量无法正确排序策略相关特征的保留程度。

Method: 提出DreamStream生成式闭环模拟器，基于"模拟器接地"的自回归视频模型实现面向策略的保真度；通过交通布局引导，从大规模预训练视频模型蒸馏，在改变视觉外观的同时保留场景布局与动态物体时序一致性等策略相关特征。提出FD$π$多表征度量，以公开E2E策略的场景上下文特征上的Fréchet距离衡量仿真到真实差距。基于DreamStream构建Navhard-CL基准，将非反应式真实基准NAVSIM转为含对抗驾驶行为与天气变化的交互测试环境。

Result: 在FD$π$度量下，DreamStream相较最强先前闭环模拟器在nuScenes提升1.6倍、在NAVSIM提升4.7倍，并对策略感知可观测性造成最小扰动。Navhard-CL暴露出先前闭环基准忽略的多种策略失效模式，如评分器偏差与缺乏恢复行为。

Conclusion: DreamStream通过策略导向的生成式闭环仿真，缩小了仿真到真实的视觉与感知差距，并提供更有效的闭环评估基准，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Faithfully evaluating end-to-end driving policies in simulation requires observations that are not merely photo-realistic, but preserve the scene features a policy relies on to make decisions. Existing platforms, however, exhibit a sim-to-real visual gap that corrupts policy perception, undermining their ability to assess a policy's closed-loop decision-making. To this end, we propose DreamStream, a generative, closed-loop simulator that achieves policy-oriented fidelity using a simulator-grounded autoregressive video model. Our video model is distilled from a large pretrained video model via traffic layout guidance, varying visual appearance while preserving policy-relevant features such as scenario layout and the temporal consistency of dynamic objects. We further observe that perceptual metrics like FID misrank how well these features are preserved. To tackle this, we introduce FD$π$, a new multi-representation metric that measures the sim-to-real gap as the Fréchet distance over scene-context features from public E2E policies. Under FD$π$, DreamStream improves over the strongest prior closed-loop simulator by $1.6\times$ on nuScenes and $4.7\times$ on NAVSIM, and induces the least perturbation to policy's perceptual observability. Based on DreamStream, we construct Navhard-CL benchmark, which turns non-reactive real-world benchmark NAVSIM into interactive testing environments with adversarial driving behaviors and weather variations. This benchmark exposes many failure modes of driving policies, such as scorer bias and lack of recovery behaviors, that prior closed-loop benchmarks overlook. Code and data are available at https://github.com/VAIL-UCLA/DreamStream.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用 Upworthy 研究档案（数千组共享真实流量的标题 A/B 测试）作为留出真值，开展「仿真到现实」效度研究，发现无角色设定的零样本 LLM 基线在预测点击率时显著优于十人合成角色（persona）面板；即对聚合互动量的预测，直接询问模型优于让模型进行角色扮演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销者日益将大语言模型（LLM）当作「合成角色」来预测受众对文案的反应，其依据是「经受众画像条件化的 LLM 能模拟人类样本」这一证据。但作者质疑：这种预测对真实行为是否真的有效？角色扮演机制是否真的有帮助？因此需要针对合成角色预测与真实行为之间的一致性进行效度检验。

Method: 采用「仿真到现实」效度研究范式，以 Upworthy 研究档案为留出真值（含真实流量的标题 A/B 测试与实测点击率）。对比两个条件：①基于真实受众人口统计信息构建的十人角色面板；②无角色的零样本基线（直接询问模型一般读者点击的可能性）。评估指标为排序一致性（Kendall τ）与 top-1 准确率。并做稳健性与复现检验：三个独立 Upworthy 数据划分、另一领域新闻数据集、随机种子、提示措辞、模型选择（三个 Gemini 档位及 OpenAI gpt-4.1）。

Result: ①真值可靠性是核心约束：多数 A/B 测试没有统计上可区分的优胜者，故效度只能在可靠子集上度量（n=399）。②与角色模拟前提相反，角色条件化会降低预测效度：无角色基线排序更好（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1 准确率 49.2%），明显优于角色面板（τ=0.084；top-1 34.6%），且置信区间不重叠。③该结果在三个独立划分上可复现，在另一领域新闻数据集上方向一致，并对种子、提示措辞与模型选择（三档 Gemini 及 gpt-4.1，配对差距显著）均稳健。

Conclusion: 对于预测聚合互动量，普通的 LLM 排序器优于角色模拟；合成角色不仅是弱预测器，甚至比不使用角色更差。直接询问模型可获取准确的人群级先验，而强制其扮演特定角色会引入偏差与噪声。所有数值均可通过公开的、以工件为先的复现包重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [191] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了TabPFN v2.5在59个生物医学表格数据集上微调时的五种AdamW预条件策略，发现原始AdamW在预测性能与统计排名上最优，现有曲率感知预条件器未带来可靠提升，需开发生物医学专用预条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN等表格基础模型在低数据表格分类中展现潜力，但优化与预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响尚不明确；现有通用预条件方法能否适配医疗表格学习缺乏系统实证评估。

Method: 在涵盖阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、SMC、KEEL及UCI生物医学基准的59个数据集上，对TabPFN v2.5进行微调，比较五种基于AdamW的预条件策略，并从预测性能、计算效率和统计显著性三方面评估。

Result: 原始AdamW优化器在整体性能和统计排名上持续最优；现有曲率感知预条件器在多样化生物医学学习场景中未能提供可靠改进。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法可能无法充分捕捉生物医学表格学习的优化特性，未来需针对医疗导向的表格基础模型设计生物医学感知的预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [192] [VideoX-Qwen: Data-Centric Instruction-Based Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26015)
*JJiahang Li,Dingbao Shao,Xinyu Chen,Song Wu,Jiang Lin,Duo Li,Yuhang Liu,Jiaxin Hu,Shengrong Gu,Ying Tai,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VideoX-Qwen：一个面向通用指令视频编辑的数据构建与模型训练一体化框架，构建超120万条视频编辑数据并训练统一 Qwen-Wan 编辑器，在多项指标上优于 UniVideo 与 Kling O1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视频编辑进展依赖大规模成对监督数据，以及将视频生成骨干有效适配到指令驱动编辑。与视频生成不同，视频编辑不仅要执行指定变换，还必须保持无关主体、场景结构、运动与时间连续性。

Method: 数据侧：可扩展生产管线组织专用生成与理解模型，覆盖 addition、removal、replacement、attribute editing 的互补路线，并进行质量筛选与指令增强；产出超过120万条 directional video-editing records，每个主要任务组超过40万条，自动接受率89%，统一为 source-instruction-target 接口。模型侧：统一 Qwen-Wan editor，结合多模态语义条件与密集源视频 latent guidance；采用渐进式 image-video 训练策略，对齐多模态指令接口、使视频生成器适配源条件编辑，并用精选高分辨率数据精修输出质量。

Result: 在100例与 UniVideo、Kling O1 的对比中，VideoX-Qwen 在11项报告指标中的9项取得最佳均值，包括指令遵循、编辑质量、内容保持、结构与感知相似度以及视频分布质量。

Conclusion: 大规模数据生产系统与统一训练框架为更强大的指令驱动视频编辑提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Progress in general-purpose video editing depends on constructing large-scale paired supervision and effectively adapting video-generation backbones to instruction-driven editing. Unlike video generation, video editing must execute a requested transformation while preserving unrelated subjects, scene structure, motion, and temporal continuity. We present VideoX-Qwen, an integrated data-construction and model-training framework for general instruction-based video editing. Our scalable production pipeline organizes specialized generation and understanding models into complementary routes for addition, removal, replacement, and attribute editing, followed by quality screening and instruction enrichment. It produces more than 1.2 million directional video-editing records, including over 400,000 records in each major task group, with an automatic acceptance rate of 89%. The resulting corpus provides broad and structured coverage of common editing operations through a unified source-instruction-target interface. We further develop a unified Qwen-Wan editor that combines multimodal semantic conditioning with dense source-video latent guidance. A progressive image-video training strategy aligns the multimodal instruction interface, adapts the video generator to source-conditioned editing, and refines output quality with selected high-resolution data. In a 100-example comparison with UniVideo and Kling O1, VideoX-Qwen achieves the best mean result on nine of eleven reported metrics, including instruction following, editing quality, content preservation, structural and perceptual similarity, and video-distribution quality. Together, the large-scale data-production system and unified training framework provide a practical foundation for more capable instruction-driven video editing.

</details>


### [193] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出4DGS-JEPA，一种面向动态高斯场景的Gaussian-native联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），用于因果多视界（multi-horizon）预测，以“时间组合”为核心原则，在不做完整未来外观重建的前提下学习可复用的预测性动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅（Dynamic Gaussian Splatting）虽能显式表示演化中的3D场景，但主要面向重建、未来状态生成或渲染优化，而非学习可复用的预测动力学；因此需要一种与高斯表示原生兼容、强调预测性与时间一致性的建模框架。

Method: 1）采用场景级、运动组级、高斯级的层次化表示，并设计视界条件转移算子，同时支持直接预测与递归展开（recursive rollout）。2）核心原则为时间组合：不同时序转移路径若到达同一未来端点，应产生兼容的预测状态；通过端点监督与多视界路径监督将预测锚定到未来目标嵌入。3）引入选择性几何解码器与几何级组合，用一致的组运动与高斯几何约束学习到的动力学，无需完整未来外观重建。4）提出混合对应机制：结合持久规范身份（canonical identity）与残差最优传输匹配（optimal-transport matching），以应对重排序与拓扑变化。5）从理论上刻画零损失下的路径一致性与有限误差下的展开累积。

Result: 三项受控实验提供机制级证据：时间组合在保持预测精度的同时降低潜空间路径依赖；几何级组合提升解码运动的一致性；混合对应机制在保持可靠身份识别的同时，在对应关系模糊时仍具稳健性。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA为动态高斯世界提供了一种预测性、时间组合式的建报表述，将动态高斯建模从重建/渲染导向推进到可复用预测动力学的学习，并结合理论分析与受控实验验证了各项机制的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [194] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个多模态可解释性GBM生存预测框架，将sheaf超图卷积、概念瓶颈层与扩展充分性检验（EST）正则统一起来，在UPenn-GBM 593例患者5折交叉验证中取得C-index 0.643，且折间标准差最低（0.015）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）生存预测模型普遍将准确性与可解释性视为相互竞争的目标：高性能模型牺牲透明度，可解释模型则损失预测能力；同时，多数方法仅依赖单一影像模态，未充分利用多模态MRI与临床元数据中的互补信息。作者主张这一权衡并非固有，并试图同时实现高判别力与可解释性。

Method: 提出多模态框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) sheaf超图神经网络，通过有向、非对称消息传递建模组织patch间的高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将学习到的表示压缩为具有临床依据的概念，实现事前（ante-hoc）可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性检验（EST）正则项，在训练中惩罚不忠实解释，确保解释真实反映内部决策过程。临床与基因组特征通过门控融合引入，在保留分子标志物主导预后信号的同时维持概念级可追溯性。

Result: 在UPenn-GBM数据集593例患者上进行5折交叉验证，框架取得0.643的一致性指数（C-index），并在所有对比模型中具有最低的折级方差（std = 0.015）。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个将sheaf超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则统一用于脑MRI可解释生存预测的工作，表明可解释性与预测性能之间并非必然存在权衡。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [195] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Ovis-Embedding 全模态嵌入模型系列，基于 Qwen-Omni 共享多模态骨干，在文本、图像、视频、音频统一表示空间中训练，在 MMEB-v3/v2、MVEB、MAEB、RTEB 等基准取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型通常为不同模态搭建独立编码塔，导致模态割裂、表示空间不统一，难以支持任意模态间的检索。作者希望用统一的全模态训练范式构建通用嵌入模型，实现 any-to-any 检索。

Method: 核心设计为共享多模态骨干而非独立模态塔，包含三项关键改进：(1) 原生全模态初始化：以预训练 Qwen-Omni 为嵌入骨干，通过低秩初始化进行对比训练适配；(2) 数据中心全模态训练：构建覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频及交错多模态数据的高质量语料，并提出同源采样构建任务一致批次以提供有效批内负样本，提高数据效率；(3) 面向嵌入的训练与推理优化：采用 focal loss 强化困难样本，使用基于相似度的 Embedding Distillation 从互补专家迁移细粒度相似结构；推理阶段通过低秩特征分解获得紧凑嵌入，支持灵活维度且性能损失极小。

Result: Ovis-Embedding 系列在 MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB、RTEB 上均达到 state-of-the-art，覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频模态，验证了全模态统一训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明统一全模态训练能够缓解模态碎片化问题，推动通用嵌入模型发展，为任意模态到任意模态的检索提供可行路径。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [196] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner 是一个面向长程操作任务的具身规划前端：用多源分层数据与接管时/失败标注增强监督，并以共享 VLM 主干提供离散事件状态与连续潜变量 CoT 两种事件结构化计划形式；离线二步规划评估中在四个模型中位列第二，真机实验优于所评估基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）系统虽需任务规划来连接高层指令与可执行行为，但常将中间规划结构隐式化；现有 chain-of-thought（CoT）规划器又依赖粗粒度任务级标注，或逐 token 序列化长推理轨迹，导致具身推理在监督信号与表示形式两方面均不充分。

Method: 数据侧：融合 Ego、UMI 与遥操作数据，采用层次化粒度与源相关标注深度；利用接管时（takeover-time）标注和人工设计失败样本，监督进行中的错误识别。模型侧：共享 VLM 主干暴露两种事件结构化计划形式——离散接口输出可解释的事件状态；潜变量接口通过 Staircase Decoding 在交错 Transformer 深度间传递连续 CoT 状态；并以冻结的 latent-to-text 重建目标为潜表示提供语义锚点。

Result: 离线二步规划评估中，X-Planner 在 BERTScore-F1 与基于 judge 的 Overall 分数上，于四个被评估模型中排名第二；在真机实验中分别优于所评估的基线。摘要同时指出，这些结果用于刻画规划文本质量与下游执行表现。

Conclusion: X-Planner 作为规划前端，同时改进具身推理的监督方式与表示结构，在离线规划文本质量和真实机器人下游执行上取得有竞争力的表现；其核心贡献在于用事件结构化、双接口与潜变量语义锚定来显式化长程操作中的任务规划。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [197] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool 是一个由 AI 智能体（AI agents）培育、维护并优化的形式化数学代码库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学（formalized mathematics）的积累与维护需要大量人工投入，构建规模化、持续演化的数学知识库成本高昂，因此需要借助自动化手段来降低维护负担并提升库的质量与规模。

Method: 构建名为 Lean Pool 的形式化数学代码库，并采用 AI 智能体作为核心驱动力，由其在库的"生长（grown）""维护（maintained）"与"优化（optimized）"三个环节上自主执行相关任务。

Result: 形成一个可持续扩展、自动维护并不断优化的形式化数学仓库 Lean Pool。

Conclusion: 通过 AI 智能体对形式化数学库进行全生命周期管理，可实现对形式化数学资源的自动化构建与持续改进。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [198] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"AI Neuroscientist"语言智能体，将LLM与神经影像工具集结合，支持用自然语言进行数据质控、建模与可视化；以fNIRS数据演示并在自定义基准上对比通用LLM智能体，未来扩展至fMRI等模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据分析需要专业编程与统计能力，缺乏计算背景的研究者难以直接开展分析；传统脚本化流程门槛高、交互性差，小规模数据探索缺少透明便捷的工具。

Method: 构建面向交互式数据探索的语言智能体：将大语言模型（LLM）与神经影像工具集集成，使其能够执行质量控制、建模和可视化；研究者可用自然语言查询数据质量并指定分析参数。系统以功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）数据为示例，并在自定义fNIRS基准测试套件上，与带代码沙箱的通用LLM智能体进行比较评估。

Result: 系统可在fNIRS场景下实现自然语言驱动的质控、建模与可视化，提供透明、可交互的分析方式；在自定义fNIRS基准上完成与通用LLM智能体的对比评估，展示其针对神经影像任务的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经影像分析提供了低门槛、可交互的智能体范式，适合小规模数据探索；未来将把架构推广到更多模态（如功能性磁共振成像fMRI），并扩展基准套件以覆盖更多fNIRS任务。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [199] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedGate-Fusion多模态门控模型，融合首诊叙事Transformer嵌入与10项常规风险标志物，用于初级保健前瞻性卒中风险分层；基于CPCSSN构建102,736例首诊患者队列，并在语义编码前对卒中相关术语进行词典脱敏以降低目标泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层具有挑战性，因为早期风险信号分散在常规生物标志物和非结构化临床叙事中，单一模态难以充分利用信息。

Method: 采用MedGate-Fusion多模态门控架构，整合首诊临床叙事的Transformer嵌入与10项常规记录风险标志物。数据来自加拿大初级保健哨兵监测网络（CPCSSN）电子病历；从808,921条就诊级观察中构建首诊队列，保留102,736条具有非空叙事且数据足以评估5年卒中结局的独特患者记录。为减少诊断提及导致的显式目标泄漏，在语义编码前使用基于词典的方法对卒中相关术语进行脱敏。

Result: 摘要仅报告队列构建与筛选规模：808,921条就诊级观察→102,736例独特患者记录；未提供模型判别力、校准度、AUC等性能结果。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出明确结论。研究意图是通過多模态融合与目标泄漏控制，提升初级保健前瞻性卒中风险分层的可行性；具体有效性需参考正文实验结果。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [200] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出关系推理基准与 ReAdapt 方法，让 LLM 社会智能体显式维护并更新关系状态，从而在内容线索与关系线索冲突时修正初始选择，提升反应选择与引荐任务准确率并降低 oracle 遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能体的基础决策（是否回应帖子、该联系谁）并非纯内容问题，而依赖人际关系中的关系强度、互惠性、共同连接等潜在关系。标准 LLM 智能体循环未显式建模新关系证据如何修正当前社会假设，当关系线索与内容线索冲突时，容易选择表面显眼的候选。

Method: 构建关系推理基准：500 个合成社会世界，包含好友、关注、互动历史与信息流，生成 1,000 条查询，覆盖反应选择与温暖引荐（寻找通往目标人的最佳桥梁）两个任务；约 53% 查询中表面显眼候选与关系 grounded oracle 不同，构成需修正初选的反转子集。提出 ReAdapt（Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate），在 ReAct 循环中加入显式结构化社会状态 z=(G, B, R, N, D)，分别表示目标、信念、关系、规范与披露。每次工具观察后执行类型化 Adapt 步骤，更新状态并输出策略操作（继续、切换、放弃、澄清），再选择下一步动作。

Result: 在 Gemini-3-Flash 与每任务 n=150 的分层子集上，ReAdapt 将温暖引荐准确率从 37% 提升至 51%（+14 点），反应选择准确率从 69% 提升至 77%（+8 点）；Oracle regret 分别从 0.260 降至 0.152、从 0.095 降至 0.053。

Conclusion: 在固定模型、工具与环境条件下，结果表明显式的关系状态自适应有助于 LLM 智能体将检索到的社会证据转化为修正后的决策。

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [201] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种低成本电路发现方法：将注意力视为单次前向传播中的路由图，保留指向答案的少量路由，并通过消融验证其因果重要性。在归纳头和IOI等已知电路任务上，提取边消融对模型的伤害显著大于同规模随机边消融，成本仅一次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型电路发现通常需要大量精心设计的干预实验，成本高、流程复杂。作者希望探索一种更简单、更廉价的替代方案，即从单次前向传播中直接提取可能重要的注意力路由，并验证这些路由是否具有真实因果作用。

Method: 将注意力解释为路由图，从一次前向传播中提取指向答案的一小组路由（边），然后对这些提取边进行消融，并与同规模随机边消融进行对比。在GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium和Pythia-410M上，每个实验单元使用n=100条提示，采用配对gap检验和bootstrap置信区间评估。

Result: 在归纳（induction）和IOI等已有已知正确电路的任务上，消融提取边对模型性能的损害显著大于消融同规模随机边。提取步骤仅需一次前向传播，而逐头patch扫描的成本约高出两个数量级。

Conclusion: 作者不声称构建了完整的电路图谱，而是提出一种廉价的“草图”方法：在已知任务上能携带真实因果信号，同时存在明确的失效模式。代码与评估工件已公开。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [202] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出潜在等价学习框架，将持久化任务身份与数据集相关实现解耦，通过原型与查询条件路由组织证据，在Data Agent Benchmark上以94.67%数据集宏平均分层Pass@1取得榜首。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业结构化数据智能体需在跨schema、关系、策略与业务角色的复杂环境中推理。现有基于可复用Markdown记忆/技能文件的方法虽能缓存已发现结构，却掩盖了自然分工：智能体擅长语义推理，学习系统擅长预测与组织重复结构。

Method: 提出潜在等价学习，分离持久化任务相关身份与其数据集相对实现。用支持与反对证据塑造“支持实现的高斯原型”以学习身份在特定数据环境中的表达，并用软隶属画像保留硬分配丢失的区分；另设可学习查询原型系统表示重复证据需求，经兼容性函数映射到同一持久身份结构。该身份因子化、查询条件化路由为下游推理物化相关数据集特定证据，使智能体基于已组织证据状态操作。

Result: 在Data Agent Benchmark（54个查询、12个异构数据集）上，完整实现五次完整试验取得94.67%数据集宏平均分层Pass@1，原始查询尝试258/270成功；基准Claude Opus 4.6参考智能体为55.51%；提交时在40个排行榜条目中排名第一。

Conclusion: 身份因子化与查询条件化路由能有效桥接结构化数据智能体的语义推理与学习系统结构组织能力，减少每次查询重建跨schema结构的需求，在异构数据集基准上显著优于参考智能体并达到领先水平。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [203] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*
