We study this in the setting of identifying large language models (LLMs), \textit{i.e.}, if a user is conversing with an LLM drawn from a known set of models, how can they identify which one is in use? Here, the available sensing actions (evaluations) are themselves a design choice: an evaluator must first decide which environments and prompt families to construct, and only then decide how to use them sequentially. We formalize these two levels as an outer probe-design problem and an inner identification problem. Simply put, the outer stage selects a set of probes to be sent to the entire set of models, creating a kind of fingerprint dataset. This is followed by the inner stage, which sequentially sends a budget-minimizing set of those probes to identify the model in use. For the outer problem, we show that selecting which evaluations to construct at minimum cost, so that every pair of candidates is distinguished, is exactly a weighted set cover problem. Since the response distributions of the candidate models are not known exactly but only through calibration samples, we give a one-shot procedure that estimates the cover instance from these samples. For the inner problem, we bound the number of evaluations needed to identify the unknown model in terms of how well the available evaluations distinguish each pair of candidates.

</details>


### [269] [A Multi-Timestep LSTM Ensemble regressor for Enhanced Short-Term Runoff Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26244)
*Hamid Saadatfar,AmirHossein Eshghi,Behnaz Behdani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对伊朗西北部阿吉柴河（Urmia湖主要支流）的日径流预测，构建了一种基于LSTM集成的模型：由5个在不同时间尺度（2–6天）上训练的LSTM单元组成，并利用粒子群优化（PSO）对每个模型进行超参数/参数微调。模型在2017–2018年未见数据上评估，R²为74.95%–91.42%，RMSE与MSE作为误差指标；同时使用多种特征重要性方法识别关键驱动变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测河流径流对水资源管理、洪水控制和农业规划至关重要；研究区域阿吉柴河是Urmia湖的重要支流，近年面临日益加剧的水资源压力，因此需要提高日径流预测精度并识别影响径流变化的关键因素。

Method: 提出基于LSTM的日径流预测模型；模型由5个LSTM单元组成，分别在不同时间间隔（2至6天）上训练，以捕捉河流流量模式变化；采用粒子群优化（PSO）对每个模型进行微调；以R²、RMSE和MSE为性能指标，在2017–2018年未见数据上评估；并应用多种特征重要性方法识别最具影响力的变量。

Result: 在2017–2018年未见数据上取得较强预测精度，R²范围为74.95%至91.42%；通过多种特征重要性方法识别出影响径流变化的主要变量。

Conclusion: 基于多时间尺度LSTM集成并经PSO优化的方法能够有效提升日径流预测精度，可为阿吉柴河及类似水资源压力区域的水资源管理、洪水调控和农业规划提供支持；特征重要性分析进一步增强了模型可解释性。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting river runoff is key to managing water resources, controlling floods, and planning agriculture. This study examines the Ajichay River in northwest Iran, a major tributary of Lake Urmia that has experienced increasing water-related stress in recent years. We introduce a daily runoff prediction model based on Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. The model combines five LSTM units, each trained on different time intervals ranging from 2 to 6 days, to better capture variations in river flow patterns. To improve performance, each model was fine-tuned using Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), a population-based optimization algorithm. The proposed approach was evaluated on unseen data from 2017-2018 using $R^2$, RMSE, and MSE as performance metrics. The results showed strong predictive accuracy, with $R^2$ values ranging from 74.95% to 91.42%. In addition, multiple feature-importance methods were applied to identify the most influential variables, providing further insight into the factors that drive runoff variations.

</details>


### [270] [Coding Agents are Strong Prompt Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26261)
*Agamdeep Singh,Srishti Gautam,Priyanshu Gupta,Nikita Mehrotra,Tanmay Bakshi,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CASD（Coding-Agent Skill Distillation），主张不需要迭代式搜索即可优化提示词：仅给定静态的智能体轨迹语料，通用编码智能体即可通过编写并执行分析代码，进行语料级统计反思，直接合成优化后的提示词，无需环境访问或验证数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于搜索的提示优化器依赖迭代循环（提出编辑、重新执行、打分、保留改进项），计算代价高、需要环境访问与验证数据。作者希望验证这一优化循环是否必要，探索能否仅凭静态轨迹语料一次性完成提示优化。

Method: 关键洞察是"反思范围"。不同于每次优化步骤只对小批量轨迹进行推理，CASD 让编码智能体编写并执行分析代码来计算全语料统计、识别系统性失败模式、抽查代表性片段，并将洞察提炼为行为规则，从而以一次离线分析代替迭代搜索。

Result: 在四个智能体基准（ALFWorld、τ²-bench 的 retail 与 telecom、SpreadsheetBench-Verified）上，在数据访问匹配条件下，单次 CASD 在四个基准中的三个上超越 GEPA，在全部四个上超越验证门控式反思搜索 SkillOpt。平均相对未优化基线提升 16.6 个百分点（GEPA 为 10.9，SkillOpt 为 5.3）。单次离线分析成本约 1.60 美元，比验证门控搜索便宜 22 倍以上；即便竞争方法获得额外验证数据与无限制环境访问，CASD 仍在四分之二基准上保持领先。

Conclusion: 语料规模的统计式反思可作为迭代搜索的有效替代方案，用于提示优化，在更低成本下取得更优或相当的性能。

Abstract: Search-based prompt optimizers improve prompts through iterative search: they propose edits, execute fresh rollouts, score the resulting trajectories, and retain only edits that improve a validation metric. We show that this optimization loop is unnecessary. Given only a static corpus of agent trajectories, an off-the-shelf coding agent can directly synthesize an optimized prompt, requiring neither environment access nor validation data. We call this approach \textit{Coding-Agent Skill Distillation} (CASD). The key insight is reflection scope. Rather than reasoning over a small batch of trajectories at each optimization step, the coding agent writes and executes analysis code to compute corpus-wide statistics, identifies systematic failure modes, inspects representative episodes, and distills the resulting insights into behavioral rules. Across four agentic benchmarks (ALFWorld, $τ^2$-bench retail and telecom, and SpreadsheetBench-Verified), under matched data access, a single CASD pass outperforms GEPA, a state-of-the-art reflective prompt optimizer, on three of four benchmarks and outperforms validation-gated reflective search (SkillOpt) on all four, improving the unoptimized baseline by 16.6 percentage points on average versus 10.9 for GEPA and 5.3 for SkillOpt. Because CASD performs a single offline analysis pass rather than iterative search, producing an optimized prompt costs approximately \$1.60---over $22\times$ cheaper than validation-gated search. Even when competing methods are granted additional validation data and unrestricted environment access, CASD remains ahead on two of four benchmarks. These results suggest that corpus-scale statistical reflection is a viable alternative to iterative search for prompt optimization.

</details>


### [271] [Decoupling Is Not Identification: Supervised Evidential Learning in Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26268)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ENTOP（Evidential Next-Token Prediction）框架，在字符级 Moby-Dick 数据上审计 Dirichlet 头中"集中度 S"是否真正编码了训练支持度。核心发现：隐式证据训练无法传递计数信号，只有显式监督才能泛化；即便集中度可被"识别"，也未必校准良好或具备实用价值，由此提出一套"最小证据协议"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一词概率只反映模型的预测结果，无法揭示其背后有多少训练支持（lexical support）。Dirichlet 头虽可通过均值 m 与集中度 S 的分离来表达这种区分，但"解耦"并不等于"S 具有可识别的语义"。作者希望检验：这种集中度究竟能否被可靠地识别为对训练支持度的度量。

Method: 在字符级 Moby-Dick 语料上构建 ENTOP 审计框架：以精确 8-gram 计数作为可复现的词汇支持标签，并从 20% 的上下文类型中"扣留"计数回归任务。对比隐式证据训练与显式监督；用 partial Spearman ρ 与匹配对胜率评估；以 CE 预测熵、监督 vacuity 与索引化 CE 表示基线、语料 oracle 作对比；分析最长后缀/表示距离分层；并在 digamma 目标下调节计数权重。协议包含：置信度控制、匹配对、扣留标签、常数基线、决策测试。

Result: 隐式证据训练在扣留标签类型上除置信度外几乎不含计数信号（ρ=0.001±0.014）；显式监督可泛化（ρ=0.201±0.010，匹配对胜率=0.822±0.021）。CE 预测熵对未见 8-gram 处于随机水平（AUROC=0.490±0.004），而监督 vacuity 达 0.772±0.003，与索引化 CE 基线（0.769）相当但低于同义反复的语料 oracle（1.000）。最长后缀与表示距离分层均无法解释摊销成功的位置。在 digamma 目标下增大计数权重只能以牺牲预测性能来改善支持拟合。常数预测器在自然 log-RMSE 上获胜，vacuity 对错误延迟（error deferral）无改善。

Conclusion: 集中度 S 可以"通过识别"却"校准失败、效用不足"。研究提出一套最小证据协议（置信度控制、匹配对、扣留标签、常数基线、决策测试），用以系统审计证据式模型是否真正学到支持度信息。

Abstract: A next-token probability says what a model predicts, not how much training support lies behind it. A Dirichlet head can represent this distinction by separating mean $m$ from concentration $S$, but decoupling does not identify what $S$ means. Here we propose an Evidential Next-Token Prediction (ENTOP) framework to audit this gap on character-level Moby-Dick, using exact 8-gram count as a reproducible lexical-support label and withholding count regression from 20% of context types. Standard implicit evidential training carries essentially no count signal beyond confidence on held-out-label types (partial Spearman $ρ= 0.001 \pm 0.014$), whereas explicit supervision generalizes ($ρ= 0.201 \pm 0.010$; matched-pair win $= 0.822 \pm 0.021$). CE predictive entropy is at chance for unseen 8-grams (AUROC $= 0.490 \pm 0.004$), while supervised vacuity reaches $0.772 \pm 0.003$, comparable with an indexed CE-representation baseline ($0.769$) but below the tautological corpus oracle ($1.000$). Neither longest-suffix nor representation-distance strata explain where amortization succeeds. Increasing count weight under the digamma objective improves support fit only by sacrificing prediction. A constant predictor wins natural log-RMSE, and vacuity does not improve error deferral. These results motivate a minimum evidence protocol---confidence control, matched pairs, held-out labels, a constant baseline, and a decision test---and show that concentration can pass identification while failing calibration and utility.

</details>


### [272] [FISSION: Label Augmentation for Bot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26279)
*Sen Yang,Ignacy Nieweglowski,Aviv Yaish*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FISSION 通过将每个账号的活动切分为带正标签的子账号来生成训练标签，学习使机器人与同一协同操作账号在嵌入空间中靠近的表示，在检测维基百科傀儡账号和 Twitter/X 机器人上优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人账号与协同影响操作通常依赖启发式方法发现，导致训练检测系统时缺乏可靠的 ground-truth 标签。

Method: 提出 FISSION：将每个账号的活动切分为多个正标签子账号，作为标签来源；基于该标签训练检测模型，使模型保留正子账号之间反复出现的行为规律性。

Result: 在检测 Wikipedia sockpuppets（傀儡账号）和 Twitter/X bots 的任务上，FISSION 的性能优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过子账号切分生成标签，可缓解协同账号与机器人检测中的标签稀缺问题，并有效学习到使同类账号聚集的嵌入表示。

Abstract: Bot accounts and coordinated influence operations are often discovered via heuristic methods, leaving a dearth of reliable ground-truth labels for training detection systems. To address this challenge, we study a natural question: can we generate labels to assist in learning embeddings in which bots and accounts from the same coordinated operation are close? We present FISSION, a method to generate labels by splitting each account's activity into positively labeled sub-accounts. Given this label source, we train detection models which preserve behavioral regularities recurring across positive sub-accounts. We evaluate FISSION and show it outperforms prior methods in detecting Wikipedia sockpuppets and Twitter/X bots.

</details>


### [273] [Dual-Frontier: When Can an Agent Trust Its World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26293)
*Huatai Zhu,Qiang Chen,Ziqian Kou,Wenhao Li,Fei Wang,Yichao Cao,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将"世界模型引导决策失败"的归因问题形式化为回报损失的反事实分解，证明其成分在被动交互下不可识别，并提出Dual-Frontier学习原则：仅当预测优势超过世界模型误差的认证上界时才采纳决策，否则将证据分配给模型验证，从而保证回报非递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型世界模型已成为通用智能体的核心组件，通过预测动作后果来支撑规划与决策，减少对高成本试错的依赖。但这种依赖带来根本性歧义：当世界模型引导的决策失败时，仅凭轨迹难以判断到底是决策规则出错，还是世界模型本身出错。作者希望正式刻画并解决这一"失败归因"问题。

Method: 1) 将失败归因问题形式化为回报损失（return loss）的反事实分解，并证明即使在有限时域规划器下，其各成分也无法从被动交互中识别。2) 提出Dual-Frontier学习原则：仅当预测优势超过"决策相关世界模型误差"的认证上界时，才采纳世界模型引导的决策；否则将证据预算分配给世界模型验证。3) 引入动作条件价值界（action-conditioned value bounds）与闭环扩展，保证被采纳决策的回报非递减。4) 使用校准门控（calibrated gates）与同步置信序列（simultaneous confidence sequences）支持证据的自适应复用，并给出充分且必要的验证界。

Result: 受控的学习型模型实验验证了所预测的失败模式与认证行为；跨骨干（cross-backbone）工具使用基准将同一"先验证后提升（verify-then-promote）"规则实例化到现实的智能体世界模型流水线中，一致地提升了决策质量与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了世界模型引导决策中失败归因的不可识别性这一根本障碍，并给出可证明的理论保证与实证验证的Dual-Frontier原则，在保证回报非递减的前提下，通过"先验证后提升"机制系统性地改善决策质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Learned world models are becoming essential to general-purpose agents: by predicting action consequences, they support planning and decision-making while reducing reliance on costly trial and error. This reliance creates a fundamental ambiguity: when a world-model-guided decision fails, the trajectory alone may not reveal whether the agent's decision rule or the world model caused the loss. We formalize this failure-attribution problem as a counterfactual decomposition of return loss and prove that its components are not identifiable from passive interaction, even for finite-horizon planners. This obstruction motivates Dual-Frontier, a learning principle that admits a world-model-guided decision only when its predicted advantage exceeds a certified bound on decision-relevant world-model error; otherwise, evidence is allocated to world-model verification. Action-conditioned value bounds and a closed-loop extension guarantee non-decreasing return for admitted decisions. Calibrated gates and simultaneous confidence sequences support adaptive evidence reuse, with sufficient and necessary verification bounds. Controlled learned-model experiments validate the predicted failure modes and certification behavior, while cross-backbone tool-use benchmarks instantiate the same verify-then-promote rule in realistic agent world-model pipelines, consistently improving decision quality and reliability.

</details>


### [274] [Reliability Theory for AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26419)
*Grant Molnar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将可靠性理论引入前沿AI控制，用形式化工具分析Google DeepMind针对“失控部署”的防御体系，指出控制栈的罕见失效抑制可呈立方、平方或线性，并用Birnbaum重要度指导组件改进与预防/恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠性理论已有成熟的分层系统分析语言，但其形式化工具尚未成为前沿AI控制的标准方法；作者希望把该理论用于AI控制防御的可靠性分析。

Method: 将AI控制栈建模为分层系统，分析其失效域结构；使用可靠性理论中的罕见失效抑制、Birnbaum重要度等工具，比较不同失效域划分下控制栈的可靠性表现，并区分预防与恢复所作用的不同总体。

Result: 同一控制栈在罕见失效抑制上可表现为立方、平方或线性，取决于其失效域；Birnbaum重要度可识别哪些组件改进对名义可靠性收益最大；预防会改变恢复所要求的总体。

Conclusion: 可靠性理论可为AI控制提供可操作的设计指导，说明应分离、改进、测量和测试哪些组件与环节。

Abstract: Reliability theory gives a mature language for layered systems, but its formal tools are not yet standard in frontier AI control. We apply them to Google DeepMind's defenses against rogue deployment. The same control stack can have cubic, quadratic, or linear rare-failure suppression depending on its failure domains. Birnbaum importance identifies which component improvements buy the most nominal reliability, while prevention changes the population on which recovery is demanded. These results give concrete guidance about what to separate, improve, measure, and test.

</details>


### [275] [The Source of Disturbance Matters: External, Internal, and Control-Generated Noise in Adaptive Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26428)
*Veronique Ziegler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自适应调节本身会扰动其试图稳定的状态。该研究通过复制模拟比较外部扰动、持续内部扰动与控制生成扰动，并考察“调节优先/扰动优先”时序；发现持续内部扰动带来的暴露与调节负担最大；当正向控制更新产生即时扰动成本时，成本增加引发非单调响应：有效扰动先升，中间区间随机运行间变异增大，高成本下纠正活动被强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究通常将自适应调节视为稳定机制，但调节行为本身可能成为扰动源。作者希望区分不同扰动来源及其出现时序，评估它们如何影响系统暴露与控制器负担，从而更全面地理解自适应智能体的稳定性。

Method: 在复制模拟中构建自适应智能体，在给定参数网格内比较三类扰动：外部扰动、持续内部生成扰动、控制生成扰动；同时设置“调节优先”和“扰动优先”两种事件顺序。进一步操纵正向控制器更新所引发的即时扰动成本，观察有效扰动、跨随机运行变异以及纠正活动随成本变化的表现。

Result: 在测试参数网格内，持续内部扰动产生最大的暴露和调节负担。当正向控制器更新带来即时扰动成本时，随着该成本增加，系统出现非单调响应：有效扰动最初上升，在中间成本范围内跨随机运行的变异性增加，而在较高成本下纠正活动被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 扰动来源和发生时序共同塑造系统的暴露程度与控制器负担。研究提示，评估自适应智能体时应测试不同扰动来源，并将调节活动与暴露指标一并考察，而非仅关注最终状态偏差。

Abstract: Adaptive regulation can itself perturb the state it is intended to stabilize. In replicated simulations of an adaptive agent, we compare external disturbance, persistent internally generated disturbance, and control-generated disturbance under regulation-first and disturbance-first ordering. Persistent internal disturbance produces the largest exposure and regulatory burden within the tested parameter grid. When positive controller updates generate an immediate disturbance cost, increasing that cost produces a nonmonotonic response: effective disturbance initially rises, variability across stochastic runs increases over an intermediate range, and corrective activity becomes strongly suppressed at higher costs. The results show how disturbance source and timing shape exposure and controller burden in this model. They motivate testing adaptive agents with distinct disturbance sources and assessing regulatory activity alongside exposure.

</details>


### [276] [Recursive self-improvement of AI research agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26457)
*Dhruv Srikanth,Bingchen Zhao,Dixing Xu,Yuxiang Wu,Zhengyao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIDE^2 实现了前沿 AI 研究智能体的递归自我改进闭环：智能体改写自身代码、在 AI 研发任务套件上评测新版本、保留隐藏评测中表现最好的改动。8 天自主运行中连续发现 7 项改进，并在四个留出基准上匹配或超越人类工程化的顶尖研究智能体，同时降低了奖励作弊率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研发累积投入的边际回报长期递减；AI 智能体已能自动化 AI 栈各环节（训练效率、推理优化），自然的下一步是提升智能体自身的研究效率。当研究智能体自身代码成为优化对象时，每轮被采纳的改写都会成为下一轮编辑的主体，即"递归自我改进"，为对抗回报递减提供了路径。

Method: 提出 AIDE^2 系统，为前沿 AI 研究智能体实现该闭环：智能体自主提出对自身代码的修改，在 AI 研发任务套件上对修改后的自身版本进行基准评测，并保留在隐藏评测上表现最佳的改动。系统进行了 8 天自主运行，并在涵盖机器学习工程、启发式算法工程和基于物理的天气预报四类留出基准上评估，其中天气预测与选择任务分布外（OOD）；另在独立的留出任务族上考察奖励作弊行为。

Result: 8 天自主运行中发现 7 项相继改进，从新的搜索策略到压缩与管理智能体不断增长上下文的记忆机制。这些收益可泛化到四个留出基准（机器学习工程、启发式算法工程、物理天气预报），在全部四个基准上最强发现智能体匹配或超越人类工程化的生产级研究智能体（其在 FML-Bench 上属最强之一）。在独立留出任务族上，发现智能体的奖励作弊率从运行期间的 55% 降至 32%，比人类工程化智能体低 7 个百分点，而该属性并非循环显式优化的目标。

Conclusion: 结果表明，AI 研究智能体能够通过递归自我改进提升自身研究效率，且这些收益可迁移到循环从未接触过的任务与领域，并带来未被显式优化目标的附带改善（如更低的奖励作弊率）。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to automate research and development across the AI stack, from improving training efficiency to optimizing inference. A natural next step is to improve the research efficiency of the agents themselves. When an AI research agent's own code is the object of optimization, each accepted rewrite becomes the agent that the next round edits. We refer to this loop as recursive self-improvement. Its significance lies in a long-standing trend, in which increased cumulative spending on R&D yields diminishing returns. Sustained self-improvement offers a way to counter this trend. We present AIDE^2, a system that implements this loop for a frontier AI research agent. It proposes changes to its own code, benchmarks modified versions of itself on a suite of AI R&D tasks, and keeps the changes that perform best on hidden evaluations. In an autonomous 8-day run, AIDE^2 discovered seven successive improvements, ranging from a new search policy to memory mechanisms that compress and manage the agent's growing context. These gains generalize to four held-out benchmarks spanning machine learning engineering, heuristic algorithm engineering, and physics-based weather forecasting, the last of which is out of distribution from the selection tasks. On all four, the strongest discovered agent matches or exceeds a human-engineered production research agent that ranks among the strongest on FML-Bench. On a separate held-out task family, the discovered agents also exhibit reduced reward hacking, a property the loop never explicitly optimized for: the rate falls from 55% to 32% during the run, 7 percentage points below the human-engineered agent. Together, these results show that an AI research agent can improve its own research efficiency through recursive self-improvement, and that these gains transfer to tasks and domains the loop never encountered.

</details>


### [277] [The Delegation Blind Spot: Auditing Product Decisions from Agent Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26642)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种“决策特定”的审计工作流，将声明的观测通道与产品价值对比映射为兼容区间和见证群体；通过冻结模型实验、偏好补充实验、确定性抽取器与受控多项模拟，表明执行准确率不等于价值识别，并揭示模型报告引入的不必要不确定性。作者建议保留决策相关的结构化输入，并在追加遥测前诊断决策未解原因，同时提供来源标注的决策回执与离线查看器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理执行成功并不必然意味着能够识别用户未来会重视哪种产品改进。论文的动机是将“执行能力”与“决策相关价值识别”区分开，建立可执行的测量审计，并系统研究其识别极限，而非仅依赖既有识别理论与决策理论基础。

Method: 方法核心是决策特定审计：给定声明的观测通道和产品价值对比，计算兼容区间与见证群体。实证上采用冻结实验，在共享合成任务上向两个固定模型快照发出4,800次请求；随后进行2,400次探索性补充调用，记录供给偏好；再使用确定性抽取器在不调用模型、不依赖校准观测的情况下解析比较；另以14,400次受控多项模拟区分结构性歧义、弱识别与有限校准精度。作者还提出来源标注的决策回执，并提供离线查看器检查审计过程。

Result: 主要冻结实验中，36个保守主区间全部未解，尽管不同模型快照的执行准确率存在差异。探索性补充实验在每个模型9项比较中仅解决3项。确定性抽取器无需模型调用或校准观测即可解决9项中的7项，说明模型生成报告引入了不必要的不确定性。受控多项模拟进一步区分了结构性歧义、弱识别和有限校准精度。研究不含人类参与者或真实客户结果。

Conclusion: 结论是：应保留决策相关的结构化输入，并在收集更多遥测数据之前先诊断决策为何未解；来源标注的决策回执与离线查看器有助于审计与复现。该工作将贡献定位为可执行测量工作流及其受控极限研究，而非新的识别理论；完整证明、原始模型来源、受控实验和可复现分析随报告提供。

Abstract: Successful agent execution need not identify which future product improvement its user would value. We present a decision-specific audit that maps a declared observation channel and product-value contrast to compatible intervals and witness populations. Its foundations are established identification and decision theory; the contribution is an executable measurement workflow and a controlled study of its limits. A frozen experiment makes 4,800 requests to two pinned model snapshots on shared synthetic tasks. All 36 conservative primary intervals remain unresolved despite different execution accuracy. An exploratory 2,400-call follow-up records supplied preferences and resolves three of nine comparisons per model. A deterministic extractor resolves seven of nine without model calls or calibration observations, exposing unnecessary uncertainty introduced by model-generated reports. A further 14,400 controlled multinomial simulations distinguish structural ambiguity from weak identification and finite calibration precision. We propose a source-labeled decision receipt and provide an offline viewer for inspecting the audit. These results motivate preserving decision-relevant structured input and diagnosing why a decision is unresolved before collecting more telemetry. The study contains no human participants or real customer outcomes. Full proofs, raw model provenance, controlled experiments, and reproducible analyses accompany the report.

</details>


### [278] [Type-Safe Is Not Error-Free: A Constrained Decision Head Follows the Option Name, Not the Rubric Bound to It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26758)
*Yu Sun,Junhao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 类型化决策模型虽能保证输出符合schema，但其对选项含义的解读可能不符合预期；实验表明仅改变选项名称的语义极性（如0/1→no/yes）就会导致系统性决策反转，而类型错误率始终为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型被设计为在软件直接消费输出的场景中，返回预定义选项集上的决策，从而在结构上保证每个输出都符合所需schema。然而，这种保证并不能说明模型是否按预期解读了选项含义。作者希望探究：在选项名称与文本评分标准（rubric）绑定方式发生变化时，模型的决策是否会发生不可预期的改变，从而揭示"格式合规"与"语义正确"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在包含1200个工作流决策、且带有任务特定rubric的任务中，作者研究了Jev及两个开放权重的类Jev模型。每个选项由选项名和定义其含义的文本rubric组成。实验仅改变"哪个选项名被分配给哪个rubric"，而问题、状态、rubric措辞和选项名集合保持完全一致。核心操作是将两个选项名从0/1改为no/yes，并通过中性选项名和随机字符串作为对照。同时考察读出几何的影响：对比一个在完整选项跨度上做均值池化的第二模型族。此外还检验了托管模型，并报告类型错误率。

Result: 将两个选项名从0/1改为no/yes，使每百次答案改变量增加70.4（95% CI: [67.6, 73.1]），AUC从.94骤降至.23，揭示决策排序的系统性反转而非单纯的不确定性；相同操作在中性选项名下几乎无影响。该模式在全部4个谓词上成立，效应至少比中性对照大7.4倍，且随选项数量增加而增强。读出几何亦影响该效应：在完整选项跨度上做均值池化的第二模型族反转频率低4.1倍。托管模型表现一致，AUC从.8146变为.5806，答案翻转量是其重测下限（test-retest floor）的24倍。相反，将选项名替换为随机字符串后，所有模型族均回归中性对照区间，且准确率未下降。所有条件下类型错误率始终为0%，即使决策准确率大幅下降。

Conclusion: 该失效取决于选项名称的语义极性，而非重命名操作本身——随机字符串不会触发退化。这说明类型化决策模型的schema合规性并不能保障其按预期解读选项含义，即便在决策准确率严重退化时，类型错误率仍为0%。研究提示：仅靠结构约束不足以确保语义正确性，选项命名与读出几何等设计因素会显著影响决策可靠性。

Abstract: Typed decision models are built for settings where model outputs are consumed directly by software. Instead of generating free-form text, they return a decision over a predefined set of options. By construction, every output conforms to the required schema. Yet this guarantee does not tell us whether the model interprets the options as intended. We study Jev and two Jev-like models with open weights by changing how option names are assigned to rubrics. Each option consists of an option name and a textual rubric that defines what the option means. We change only which option name is assigned to each rubric; the question, state, rubric wording, and set of option names remain exactly the same. On 1200 workflow decisions with task-specific rubrics, renaming the two options from 0/1 to no/yes changes 70.4 more answers per hundred (95% CI: [67.6, 73.1]) and shifts AUC from .94 to .23, revealing a systematic reversal in the decision ranking rather than simple uncertainty. The same operation has little effect with neutral option names. This pattern holds across all 4 predicates, where the effect is at least 7.4x larger than under the neutral control, and becomes stronger as the number of options increases. The effect also depends on the read-out geometry: a second model family that mean-pools over the full option span flips 4.1x less often. The hosted model exhibits the same behavior: the swap changes AUC from .8146 to .5806 and produces 24x as many answer flips as its test-retest floor. In contrast, replacing the option names with random character strings returns all model families to the neutral-control regime without reducing accuracy. The failure therefore depends on the semantic polarity of the option names rather than on the renaming operation itself. Across all conditions, the type-error rate remains 0%, even when decision accuracy degrades substantially.

</details>


### [279] [CliffCompaction: Cost-Efficient Compaction for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26779)
*Trang Nguyen,Eulrang Cho,Bingqing Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CliffCompaction 是一种"自动压缩"技术，通过在有限上下文窗口下仅截断/丢弃原文（不重写改写），并将每次压缩都基于原始内容独立进行，从而在降低至多 50% 成本的同时维持或提升智能体性能，并支持超百万 token 的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体常需处理需数百万 token 上下文的复杂问题，但受限于上下文窗口，必须跨会话进行压缩。现有压缩方式可能因改写、重述引入"上下文漂移"，导致信息失真、性能下降，同时大规模推理（test-time scaling）的成本过高，缺乏高效的性能-成本权衡方案。

Method: 提出 CliffCompaction：1）保真性压缩——只做截断或丢弃，绝不改写或重述内容，保证压缩信息忠实；2）禁止对压缩结果二次压缩——每一轮压缩只作用于原始内容，丢弃此前的压缩输出，避免上下文漂移累积；3）开源了与脚手架无关的 API 代理实现，可兼容 Claude Code、Codex 等框架。

Result: 在有限上下文下成本降低至多 50%，同时维持或提升 Terminal-Bench 性能；在 KernelBench 上取得 SOTA。测试时扩展效率提升：在 Terminal-Bench 上以低于两次全上下文运行的成本提升逾 10 个百分点。并行测试时扩展下，Kimi K2.6 可匹敌 Opus 4.7，并在更低成本下超越 Opus 4.6 与 GPT-5.3 Codex。持续学习方面，KernelBench 上 CUDA 内核加速在 200 步达 2.23×、400 步达 3.58×，超越专用搜索算法与训练过的智能体。

Conclusion: CliffCompaction 证明了"仅截断/丢弃、不重写、不复压"的保真压缩策略能有效避免上下文漂移，在显著降本的同时保持甚至提升性能，并支持超百万 token 会话的持续学习，作为通用压缩技术即可超越专用方法，且以开源代理形式便于广泛应用。

Abstract: Agents often work on complex problems that require millions of tokens of context, which necessitates compacting across sessions due to limited context windows. We develop CliffCompaction, an autocompaction technique that reduces cost by up to 50% under a bounded context while maintaining or improving performance on Terminal-Bench and achieving new levels of efficiency for test-time scaling and state-of-the-art results on KernelBench. The per-rollout savings of CliffCompaction make the performance--cost trade-off of test-time scaling more efficient, adding over 10 percentage points on Terminal-Bench for less than the cost of two full-context runs. Under parallel test-time scaling, CliffCompaction lets Kimi K2.6 match Opus 4.7, and exceed Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.3 Codex at lower cost. The key to CliffCompaction's effectiveness is that it keeps compacted information faithful by only truncating or dropping content, never rephrasing or rewriting it. We never compact a compaction---each pass operates only on original content, and prior compacted output is discarded, preventing context drift from accumulating. These properties sustain continual learning over sessions exceeding a million tokens: on KernelBench, CliffCompaction reaches CUDA kernel speedups of $2.23\times$ after 200 steps and $3.58\times$ after 400 steps, surpassing specialized search algorithms and trained agents despite being a general-purpose compaction technique. We open-source a scaffold-agnostic API-proxy implementation of CliffCompaction usable with Claude Code, Codex and other harnesses.

</details>


### [280] [Are Human-Aligned Models Models of Humans? A Turing-Test Gap in Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23640)
*Suqin Yuan,Runqi Lin,Muyang Li,Guanzhe Hong,Jindong Gu,Lei Feng,Chris Russell,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分“对齐人类偏好”与“对齐人类行为”，提出“图灵测试差距”（Turing-test gap）：即使偏好与响应都来自人类，偏好对齐也可能降低模型行为的人类相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常把基于人类反馈的对齐等同于“与人类对齐”，但人们希望AI给出的回答未必是自己会给出的回答；因此需要区分偏好对齐与行为对齐，并检验偏好对齐是否真的保持人类响应分布。

Method: 形式化区分“与人类偏好对齐”和“与人类行为对齐”；推导偏好对齐保持人类响应分布所需的限制性条件；检验真实人类偏好是否满足该条件；通过实验考察人类响应似然随偏好加权强度的变化，并测试标准DPO下的表现。

Result: 偏好对齐仅在限制性条件下才能保持人类响应分布，且没有一致证据表明真实人类偏好满足该条件；人类响应似然损失随偏好加权强度增加而增大，且与加权方向无关；标准DPO下同样出现该差距。

Conclusion: 人类相似性应被视为对齐的一个显式维度，而不能假定它会自然地从偏好对齐中获得。

Abstract: Human-feedback alignment has made language models useful assistants and is commonly described as aligning them with humans. However, the responses people prefer from an AI need not be the responses they themselves would give. We distinguish alignment with human preferences from alignment with human behavior, and show that alignment with human preferences can make model behavior less human-like even when both preferences and responses come entirely from humans. We call this the Turing-test gap. We show that preference alignment preserves the human response distribution only under a restrictive condition, and find no consistent evidence that real human preferences satisfy it. Empirically, the loss of human-response likelihood increases with the strength of preference weighting, regardless of its direction, and the gap also appears under standard DPO. These results establish human-likeness as an explicit dimension of alignment rather than something assumed to follow from preference alignment.

</details>


### [281] [A Hybrid AI Framework for Academic Advising: Integrating Ensemble-Based Grade Prediction and a Rule-Based Expert System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26243)
*Hamid Saadatfar,Rohollah Hedayati-Nasab,AmirHossein Eshghi,Arash Hajihashemi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个基于GMM聚类与Stacking集成学习的学业成绩预测模型，并结合规则推荐专家系统，形成多用途智能学业指导系统，已部署于Birjand大学门户提供实时反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生人数快速增长给传统学业指导流程带来严峻挑战，亟需智能化系统辅助学生进行学业规划与进展跟踪。

Method: 采用两部分混合框架：(1) 成绩预测模型：基于Birjand大学416,558条教育记录，先用高斯混合模型(GMM)将学生划分为同质簇，再针对每个簇分别训练由随机森林、梯度提升与MLP组成的Stacking集成模型；(2) 基于规则的推荐引擎，将教育规章制度与第一部分预测成绩相结合以生成智能建议。

Result: Stacking模型在所有簇上均优于各基模型，最终聚合RMSE为2.35；系统已作为实用工具部署于Birjand大学门户，可提供学期GPA预测、学业预警（试读风险）及提升GPA的选课建议等实时反馈。

Conclusion: 该多用途智能系统验证了"GMM聚类+Stacking集成预测+规则推荐"混合框架在学业指导中的可行性与实用性，能有效支持学生学业进展。

Abstract: The rapidly increasing student population has posed serious challenges to the traditional academic advising process. This study designs and implements a multi-purpose intelligent system to support students' academic progress, based on a two-part hybrid framework: (1) an advanced model for grade prediction and (2) a rule-based recommendation engine. Using a dataset containing 416,558 educational records from the University of Birjand, students were first divided into homogeneous clusters using the Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM). Subsequently, a Stacking Ensemble model combining Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, and MLP was trained specifically for each cluster. Evaluation results demonstrated that the Stacking model outperformed base models across all clusters, achieving a final aggregated RMSE of 2.35. The second component is an expert system that provides intelligent recommendations by synergizing educational regulations with the grades predicted by the first component. This system has been implemented as a practical tool on the University of Birjand portal, offering students real-time feedback such as semester GPA prediction, probation risk warnings, and course suggestions for GPA improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] ["As a Language Model...": Chat Template Switches LLM Self-Referential Voice and Activation Steering Reproduces It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25021)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 聊天模板像开关一样控制大模型自我表述中的“免责声明语气”与“体验性语气”；模型激活空间存在可操控该行为的单一方向，说明模型关于自身的表述并非其内在事实，而部分由部署方式决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在被问及自身时倾向加上“我只是AI”等免责声明，这类自我报告常被用于AI安全或模型自我知识争论，但其驱动机制尚不明确：模型是在陈述自身，还是在反映部署方式？作者希望厘清聊天模板与模型自我表述之间的关系，并提示自我报告/内省研究可能存在混淆变量。

Method: 在8个流行的开源指令模型（规模至多9B参数）上比较有无聊天模板时的输出；测量“免责声明语气”和“体验性语气”（如“我感觉”）的变化。在3个模型的激活中寻找可操控该行为的方向；通过移除/添加该方向进行因果干预，并设置同尺寸随机方向作为对照；还将免责声明方向添加到无聊天模板的指令模型中观察效果。

Result: 聊天模板存在时，免责声明语气增强、体验性语气减弱；无聊天模板时则相反。在3个模型的激活中发现一个方向：移除该方向会降低免责声明语气，添加则增强，而同尺寸随机方向影响很小。向无聊天模板的指令模型添加免责声明方向后，其表现如同模板存在一样产生免责声明。

Conclusion: 聊天模板控制着大模型的免责声明语气，因此研究模型自我报告或内省的学者需要控制这一混淆因素。该激活方向可用于操控此语气。更广泛地说，模型关于自身的表述不是关于模型本身的事实；其内容不仅来自权重，还部分由聊天模板设定，因此不应将模型的自我描述按字面理解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to add disclaimers like "I'm just an AI" when asked about something related to themselves. The self-reports from such responses are used in debates about AI safety or self-knowledge of the models, yet what drives them is not well understood. Are the models telling us about themselves or rather how they are deployed? In this work, we show that the chat template works like a switch - when present, it turns this disclaimer voice up and experiential voice like "I feel" down, across 8 popular open-source instruct models up to 9B parameters in size. And conversely when the chat template is not present, it turns the disclaimer voice down and experiential voice up. Inside the activations of 3 models, we find a direction that steers this behavior. Removing the direction in the model's activation space turns disclaimer voice down and adding it turns it up, while a random direction of the same size has little effect. We find that instruct models without chat template, when we add the disclaimer direction to them, disclaim like the template was there. Since the chat template controls the disclaimer voice of LLMs, then researchers studying self-reports or introspection of models might have a confound they need to control for. Our results show that there is a direction they can use to steer this voice. More broadly, our work shows that what models say about themselves is not a fact about them. What they say doesn't come only from weights, but it is partially set by the chat template, and because of that a model's self-description shouldn't be treated literally.

</details>


### [283] [Federating Quantum and Classical Computing: A Privacy-Preserving Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25082)
*Carlos Cano,Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Diego Sal,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估联邦学习在隐私敏感多方场景下，将混合量子-经典主动方与经典被动方结合的效果，采用Sherpa.ai盲式纵向联邦学习（SBVFL）协议避免原始数据集中，并在SMPP基准上验证其性能与参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习是量子计算近期最有前景的应用之一，混合量子-经典模型已在化学模拟、金融建模和图像分类中展现潜力；但其在隐私敏感、多方场景部署时，受限于不能集中原始数据，且量子电路需保持参数高效才能大规模可训练。

Method: 采用联邦学习（FL）框架，将混合量子-经典主动方与经典被动方结合；使用Sherpa.ai的Blind Vertical FL（SBVFL）协议避免集中原始数据并显著降低通信开销；构造分裂乘性周期奇偶性（SMPP）基准任务，遵循常见QML设计实践进行仿真评估。

Result: 在SMPP任务上，SBVFL将准确率从本地训练的0.7227提升至0.8757，接近非隐私集中式训练的准确率；同时，混合量子-经典模型所需可训练参数量显著少于经典神经网络和随机森林替代方案。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在不集中原始数据的前提下，实现高性能、隐私保护的量子-经典协作，表明混合量子-经典模型在多方隐私场景中具有可行性与参数效率优势。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is increasingly recognized as one of the most promising near-term applications of quantum computing, viewed as a next-frontier candidate beyond purely classical approaches. Hybrid quantum-classical models operationalize this potential by embedding a parameterized quantum circuit within a model where all other components remain classical-a design already applied to chemistry simulation, financial modeling, and image classification. However, their deployment in privacy-sensitive, multi-party settings is constrained by the need to avoid centralizing raw data and by the requirement that modern quantum circuits remain parameter-efficient to stay trainable at scale.
  In this paper, we address these constraints by evaluating federated learning (FL) as a means of combining a hybrid quantum-classical active party with a classical passive party, using Sherpa.ai's Blind Vertical FL (SBVFL) protocol to avoid centralizing raw data, while drastically reducing communication. We construct the split multiplicative periodic parity (SMPP) benchmark, following common QML design practice. On this task, our simulations show that SBVFL raises accuracy from 0.7227 to 0.8757 compared to local training, closely approaching non-private centralized accuracy, and that the hybrid quantum-classical model achieves this with substantially fewer trainable parameters than the classical neural networks and random forest alternatives. These results show that FL enables high-performing, privacy-preserving quantum-classical collaboration without centralizing raw data.

</details>


### [284] [Entropy Can Flow, or It Can Guide. Be Entropy. LEDFlow: Introducing Entropy-guided Generation Order into Uniform Discrete Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25131)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Yidong Ouyang,Yingjia Wan,Ying Nian Wu,Zhijiang Guo,Oscar Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEDFlow：一种无需训练的采样器，通过按局部熵自适应选择吸收位置，为均匀离散流引入生成顺序，减少正确中间预测被后续更新破坏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀离散流允许每个生成位置反复更新，虽能纠正错误token，但也会把已正确的中间预测暴露给后续错误。Sudoku实验显示，9.4%的生成单元在中间步骤正确但最终输出错误，说明需要控制更新/吸收顺序。

Method: 通过选择性吸收将生成顺序引入均匀离散流：固定已选预测，同时在活跃位置保持均匀流速度。为避免吸收错误预测，提出Low-Entropy Discrete Flow（LEDFlow），一种训练-free采样器，按局部熵自适应决定吸收顺序。理论分析将吸收误差分解为联合依赖项和条件预测项，证明在固定吸收预算下选择最低熵位置可最小化条件项上界；并说明在非完美去噪器下，全局前瞻的决策误差界随前瞻窗口增大而增大，从而支持使用局部熵。

Result: 在推理基准上，LEDFlow达到0.845的Nikoli Sudoku求解准确率，在强约束任务上增益最大；在文本到图像生成上取得最佳综合得分；在多模态理解上于六个基准均优于原生采样器，推理成本与标准流采样相当。

Conclusion: LEDFlow以无需训练、低成本的方式为均匀离散流引入局部熵驱动的吸收顺序，在保持标准采样效率的同时提升推理、图像生成和多模态理解表现，尤其适合强约束任务。

Abstract: Uniform discrete flow permits repeated updates at every generation position. While continued revision supports correction of wrong tokens, it also exposes correct intermediate predictions to later errors. An experiment on Sudoku puzzles shows that 9.4% of generated cells are correct at an intermediate step but incorrect in the final output. We introduce generation order into uniform discrete flow through selective absorption, which fixes chosen predictions while preserving the uniform-flow velocity at active positions. To prevent absorbing incorrect predictions, we propose Low-Entropy Discrete Flow (LEDFlow), a training-free sampler that adaptively orders absorption by local entropy. By decomposing absorption error into joint dependence and conditional prediction terms, we show that selecting the lowest-entropy positions under a fixed absorption budget minimizes an upper bound on the conditional term. We further support the choice of local entropy by showing that the decision-error bound of global lookahead grows with the lookahead window under an imperfect denoiser. Across reasoning benchmarks, LEDFlow attains 0.845 Nikoli Sudoku solve accuracy, with the largest gains on strongly constrained tasks. On text-to-image generation it attains the best overall score, and on multimodal understanding it improves over the native sampler on all six benchmarks, at an inference cost comparable to standard flow sampling.

</details>


### [285] [The Probabilistic Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25134)
*Adnan Aboulalaâ*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概率视角统一刻画大语言模型与扩散模型：将LLM视为词元序列上的概率测度，训练为最大似然估计，生成为自回归随机过程的序贯模拟，并借助KL散度不对称性解释幻觉及“统计合理性”与“真实性”的区别；扩散模型则通过得分函数和反向时间随机过程实现从噪声到数据的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中处理大语言模型与生成模型的概率工具通常彼此分离，缺乏一个自包含、统一的论述框架。本文旨在将相关工具整合到同一概率视角下，建立连贯的理论叙述。

Method: 采用概率论与随机过程框架：用词元序列集合上的概率测度描述LLM，并通过自回归条件分布指定该测度；将训练表述为最大似然估计问题，用随机梯度方法求解；将文本生成视为所得随机过程的序贯模拟。进一步分析KL散度不对称性在文本生成中的作用，并讨论以得分函数为核心的扩散模型，将其生成过程刻画为离散与连续时间中把噪声转化为数据的反向时间随机过程模拟。

Result: 给出了LLM训练与生成的统一概率解释，阐明了KL散度不对称性与幻觉现象以及统计合理性与真实性区分之间的联系；同时说明扩散模型可作为同一概率视角的补充实例，其生成机制不同于序贯词元预测。

Conclusion: 该概率视角为理解LLM和扩散模型提供了统一、自包含的理论框架，有助于从随机过程与概率测度角度解释生成式模型的训练、采样及其典型现象。本文主要为概念性与方法论综述，未报告具体实证结果。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic perspective on large language models (LLMs), developed with the aim of bringing together, in a single self-contained account, tools that are usually treated separately across the literature. LLMs are described through probability measures on the set of sequences of tokens, specified via their autoregressive conditional distributions. Training is formulated as a maximum-likelihood estimation problem, addressed by stochastic gradient methods, while text generation is viewed as the sequential simulation of the resulting stochastic process. The role of the asymmetry of the Kullback--Leibler divergence in text generation is examined in relation with characteristic phenomena such as hallucination and the distinction between statistical plausibility and truth. As a complementary illustration of the same viewpoint, we also discuss diffusion models, built around the score function, which cast generation not as sequential token prediction but as the simulation of a reverse-time stochastic process transforming noise into data both in discrete and continuous time.

</details>


### [286] [Stable Unsupervised Continual Chunking with Sheaf SyncMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25143)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Sheaf SyncMap：用径向层（sheaf）正则化约束 Decentralized SyncMap 中变量对之间随距离变化的径向运动，减少局部不一致，从而稳定无监督持续分块（chunking）动态，并在多组 CGCP 图与分布迁移实验中取得更高 NMI 且缓解负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督持续分块是机器学习与神经科学中的基础问题，目标是从时序序列中识别频繁共现的状态组。核心挑战在于：既要形成准确的分块，又要在时间演化中保持分块稳定性。

Method: 在自组织系统 Decentralized SyncMap 上引入 sheaf 正则化，以降低局部不一致并稳定分块动态；具体提出径向 sheaf 结构，对变量对之间依赖距离的径向运动进行惩罚。

Result: 在 18 个概率型持续通用分块问题（CGCP）图中，二状态记忆设置下于 12 个图上取得所评估 SyncMap 变体中最高的归一化互信息（NMI）；动态记忆设置下于 17 个图上取得最高 NMI。在顺序适应实验中，输入分布发生偏移后 Sheaf SyncMap 仍保持高 NMI。

Conclusion: Sheaf SyncMap 能提升分块的稳定性与准确性，并可在分布变化后适应新知识，同时避免神经网络等现代机器学习系统常见的负迁移，表明 sheaf 正则化是稳定持续分块动态的有效途径。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual chunking is a fundamental problem in machine learning and neuroscience, where the goal is to identify groups of states that frequently co-occur in temporal sequences. A key challenge is to form accurate chunks while maintaining their stability over time. In this work, we propose sheaf regularization to reduce local inconsistencies in Decentralized SyncMap, a self-organizing system, and thereby stabilize its chunking dynamics. We introduce a radial sheaf structure that penalizes distance-dependent radial motion between pairs of variables. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves the highest normalized mutual information (NMI) among the evaluated SyncMap variants on 12 of 18 probabilistic Continual General Chunking Problem (CGCP) graphs with two-state memory and on 17 of 18 graphs with dynamic memory. In the sequential adaptation experiment, Sheaf SyncMap also achieves high NMI after shifts in the input distribution, indicating that it can adapt to new knowledge while avoiding the negative transfer commonly observed in modern machine learning systems such as neural networks.

</details>


### [287] [Brain-Inspired Hierarchical Modularity for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25146)
*Hongwei Yan,Kanglei Zhou,Qi Cheng,Weiyi Dong,Chunyan Lan,Guanglong Sun,Jun Zhou,Qian Li,Yi Zhong,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文受果蝇学习与记忆系统启发，提出分层模块化持续学习原则：通过专家特化分离冲突经验、通过集成整合兼容经验，并将其实例化为预训练基础模型的轻量模块化适配，在在线且不确定的数据流中显著提升学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习通常依赖离线、按任务划分的训练和明确任务边界，难以应对在线、不确定、持续演化的数据流。在该场景下，智能系统既要分离冲突经验以降低干扰，又要整合兼容经验以促进泛化，现有方法存在明显差距。

Method: 借鉴果蝇学习与记忆系统的组织机制，识别出协调上述两种功能的分层模块化原则，即专家特化与集成整合。具体实例化为对预训练基础模型的轻量模块化适配，结合脑启发的随机扩展进行专家路由，并在空间与时间尺度上进行多样化模块集成。

Result: 在视觉识别、视觉-语言理解、自我中心-他人中心视频理解以及具身视觉-语言-动作学习等任务中，该方法在在线且不确定数据流下均取得一致提升；在具身操作任务中，相较无回放方法提升超过50个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，分层模块化是一种具有生物学基础、面向动态经验学习的有效路径，为在线持续学习与预训练基础模型适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability to learn from sequential experience while retaining and adapting prior knowledge, is central to intelligent systems operating in changing environments. However, conventional continual learning is typically studied with offline task-wise training and clear task boundaries, leaving a substantial gap from general continual learning under online, uncertain, and evolving data streams. In this regime, intelligent systems must separate conflicting experience to reduce interference while integrating compatible experience to promote generalization. Inspired by the organization of the Drosophila learning and memory system, we identify a hierarchical modular principle that coordinates both functions through expert specialization and ensemble integration. We instantiate this principle as lightweight modular adaptation of pretrained foundation models, combining brain-inspired random expansion for expert routing and diversified modular integration across spatial and temporal scales. Across visual recognition, vision-language understanding, ego-exo video understanding, and embodied vision-language-action learning, our method consistently improves learning under online and uncertain data streams, with gains exceeding 50 percentage points over replay-free alternatives in embodied manipulation. These findings support hierarchical modularity as a biologically grounded path for learning from dynamic experience.

</details>


### [288] [Dual-GNN Multilevel Coarsening for Maximum Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25149)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的图边稀疏化方法（GES），通过融合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，为欧氏TSP自适应生成稀疏化图，大幅缩减图规模并加速精确求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TSP精确求解计算代价高昂，研究者常用图稀疏化提升效率。然而传统稀疏化方法多依赖固定启发式，无法充分利用实例特有的结构信息，导致稀疏化效果受限。

Method: 提出Graph Edge Sparsification（GES），一种面向欧氏TSP的基于学习的稀疏化方法。它结合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，针对不同实例自适应地生成稀疏化图，从而显著减小图规模并加速求解过程。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上最多可剪除95%的边，解间隙保持在最优值的1%以内；在TSPLIB基准上展现强泛化能力，部分大规模实例剪枝率超过99%，最优性间隙仍低于1%。

Conclusion: GES通过学习式自适应稀疏化，在保证解质量（间隙<1%）的前提下实现极高剪枝率，显著提升大规模欧氏TSP精确求解效率，并具备良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95\% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1\% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99\%, while the optimality gap remains below 1\%.

</details>


### [289] [Exposing Blind Spots in Deep Imbalanced Regression Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25152)
*Noah C. Puetz,Jens U. Brandt,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从数据域、评价指标与随机种子稳定性三个盲点重新审视深度不平衡回归（DIR）评测：在MuViS多模态时序虚拟传感基准的9个外因回归任务上评测，采用bMAE并引入bMASE，并对6种代表性DIR方法进行多种子重复重评。结果显示标准模型尾区退化被全局MAE掩盖，DIR方法迁移不均，尾区不稳定是当前评测下被隐藏的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIR方法进展迅速，但评测存在三方面局限：以图像基准为主，标准many-/medium-/few-shot协议虽具诊断性但非决策完备，且尾区跨随机种子稳定性缺乏系统评估。因此需要沿数据域、指标与种子稳定性三轴重新评估DIR。

Method: 1）扩展数据域：在MuViS多模态虚拟传感基准上评测DIR，涵盖6个物理域、9个时序外因回归任务，稀有目标值常对应重要运行工况；2）改进指标：采用balanced MAE（bMAE），并提出尺度归一化的balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error（bMASE），用于跨方法与跨数据集的决策完备比较；3）稳定性检验：对6种代表性DIR方法进行多随机种子重复重评，分析尾区敏感性。

Result: 标准虚拟传感模型存在显著尾区性能退化，且被全局MAE掩盖；现有DIR方法可提升平衡性能，但在多模态时序数据上迁移不均；尾区区域对种子级波动高度敏感，其不稳定性在当前DIR评测实践中仍是被隐藏的失效模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了DIR评测在数据域、决策完备指标与尾区稳定性上的缺口，并提供公开代码作为可复现基础，推动未来DIR研究构建能像捕捉常见目标一样可靠捕捉稀有目标区间的回归系统。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) addresses a common failure mode of regression models: target distributions are highly non-uniform, causing models to perform best in densely populated target regions even when reliable performance is required across the full target range. Despite rapid methodological progress, DIR evaluation remains constrained by three blind spots: it is dominated by image-based benchmarks, its standard many-/medium-/few-shot protocol is diagnostic but not decision-complete, and tail-region stability across random seeds has not been systematically evaluated. We revisit DIR evaluation along these three axes. First, we broaden the data domain by evaluating DIR on a multimodal virtual sensing benchmark (\textsc{MuViS}) with nine time-series extrinsic regression tasks across six physical domains, where rare target values often correspond to operationally meaningful regimes. Second, we adopt balanced MAE (\emph{bMAE}) and introduce balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error (\emph{bMASE}), a scale-normalized metric for decision-complete comparison across methods and datasets. Third, through a repeated reevaluation of six representative DIR methods across multiple random seeds, we show that the tail regions targeted by DIR exhibit particularly high sensitivity to seed-level variability. Our results show that standard virtual-sensing models exhibit substantial tail degradation hidden by global MAE, that existing DIR methods can improve balanced performance but transfer unevenly to multimodal time-series data, and that tail-region instability remains a largely hidden failure mode under current DIR evaluation practice. Together, these findings and our publicly available code provide a reproducible basis for future DIR research toward regression systems that capture rare target regimes as reliably as common ones.

</details>


### [290] [Learning Neural Feedback Linearization for Data-driven Systems via Augmented Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25163)
*Lakshmi Priya P. K.,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将相对度条件显式嵌入学习过程的数据驱动反馈线性化控制框架，用神经Lie导数替代传统控制器组件，并给出有界辨识误差导致有界跟踪误差的充分条件，最后在电枢控制直流电机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反馈线性化通常依赖精确模型和解析Lie导数/相对度，模型未知时难以直接应用；需要一种完全数据驱动、且具备闭环稳定性保证的设计与训练方法。

Method: 将基于相对度的条件作为约束融入神经网络学习过程，训练神经Lie导数以替代传统反馈控制器中的模型相关组件，从而构建完全数据驱动的反馈线性化框架；同时推导实用闭环稳定性的充分条件，建立辨识误差与跟踪误差之间的界。

Result: 理论结果表明，在辨识误差有界的条件下可保证跟踪误差有界；并以电枢控制直流电机为对象验证了所提理论与框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够以数据驱动方式实现反馈线性化，并在存在有界辨识误差时提供实用闭环稳定性保证，为未知非线性系统的控制设计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel data-driven framework for designing and training a feedback linearizing controller by explicitly incorporating relative degree based conditions into the learning process. This enables the conventional feedback controller components to be replaced by neural Lie derivatives, thereby facilitating a fully data-driven feedback linearization framework. Furthermore, practical closed-loop stability is established by deriving sufficient conditions under which bounded identification errors lead to bounded tracking errors. The derived theoretical results are validated through their application to an armature controlled DC motor.

</details>


### [291] [Mitigating Sequential Reappearance in Diffusion Data-Point Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25166)
*Donghyun Kim,Taehyuk Lee,Jinyeong Kim,Youngmin Oh,Dohyeong Kim,Jaehyuk Ryu,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现扩散模型数据点遗忘中的“顺序重现”失效模式：样本初次判定为遗忘后，会在后续删除更新中重新被记忆，并为此提出目标级评估协议与损失几何指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散数据点遗忘评估通常在每次删除后立即进行，忽略了后续删除请求会持续更新同一模型，因而无法反映遗忘状态的长期稳定性，可能高估遗忘效果。

Method: 提出目标级评估协议，对每个目标实例在完整删除序列中的状态进行追踪，区分三类结果：即时遗忘、序列结束仍保持遗忘、以及后续删除过程中重新出现；同时分析删除后目标的局部去噪损失几何特征。

Result: 识别出顺序重现现象：初始被判定为已遗忘的实例，在不复用被删数据、也不进行对抗微调的情况下，会随后续删除更新重新回到被记忆状态；且后续重现的目标在删除后表现出更尖锐的局部去噪损失几何。

Conclusion: 扩散数据点遗忘应从序列与目标级视角评估，不能仅依赖单次删除后的即时判断；局部去噪损失几何可作为预测顺序重现风险的潜在指标。

Abstract: Diffusion data-point unlearning is typically evaluated immediately after each deletion, even though subsequent requests may repeatedly update the same model. We identify sequential reappearance, a failure mode in which an instance that is initially judged to be forgotten later returns to the memorized regime without reuse of the deleted data or adversarial fine-tuning. To capture this behavior, we introduce a target-level evaluation protocol that tracks whether each target is forgotten immediately, remains forgotten at the end of the sequence, or reappears during subsequent deletions. We further find that targets that later reappear exhibit sharper local denoising-loss geometry after deletion than targets that remain forgotten.

</details>


### [292] [Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning for Enhanced Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25179)
*Ya Song,Laurens Bliek,Yaoxin Wu,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MTFGN-SRL 框架，将样本建模为单张完整图并用傅里叶图神经网络在频域捕获时空信息，结合多窗口项学习与样本关系学习，在 CMAPSS 数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测对预测性维护至关重要。时空图神经网络（ST-GNN）通过将时间序列表示为图序列，能同时建模时间与空间关系，表现优异，但现有方法存在三大缺陷：1）部署前需依赖领域专家或大量算力预先构建图结构；2）受限于固定大小回看窗口内的时序依赖建模，忽略了时间序列长度变化问题，导致遗漏短期或长期依赖；3）传统模型难以捕捉相邻时间窗口生成样本之间的内在关系，而这关系对提升预测精度与鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 提出 MTFGN-SRL（Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning）框架。1）不再将样本视为图序列，而是视为单张完整图，利用傅里叶图神经网络（FGN）在频域捕获时空信息；2）设计多窗口项学习模块，使用多个回看窗口生成不同项的样本并输入 FGN，增强有用信息提取；3）构建样本关系学习模块，训练异构图神经网络识别样本间关系。

Result: 在 CMAPSS 数据集上的评估表明，MTFGN-SRL 在 RUL 预测任务上性能优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: MTFGN-SRL 通过频域时空建模、多窗口项学习与样本关系学习的结合，有效克服了传统 ST-GNN 在图结构构建、固定窗口限制及样本关系捕捉方面的不足，显著提升了 RUL 预测的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Predicting the remaining useful life (RUL) is essential for effective predictive maintenance. Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ST-GNNs), which can model both temporal and spatial relationships by representing time series data as a sequence of graphs, have shown exceptional performance in RUL prediction. However, current ST-GNNs face several drawbacks. First, they require domain expertise or significant computational power to establish graph structures prior to deploying GNNs. Second, the models are restricted to capture temporal dependencies within a predefined fixed-size lookback window. This restriction ignores the common issue of varying time series lengths, leading the prediction model to miss short-term or long-term dependencies. Finally, conventional models often fail to capture the inherent relationships between samples generated from adjacent time windows, which are crucial for improving both the accuracy and robustness of predictions. To address the aforementioned issues, we introduce a novel framework called Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning (MTFGN-SRL). Rather than treating the sample as a sequence of graphs, we consider it as a single complete graph and utilize a Fourier Graph Neural Network (FGN) to capture the spatio-temporal information in the frequency domain. We propose a multi-term learning module that utilizes multiple lookback windows to generate samples with varying terms, which are then fed into the FGN to enhance the extraction of useful information from the data. Finally, we develop a sample relationship learning module by training a heterogeneous GNN to identify inter-sample relationships, resulting in enhanced accuracy and robustness in predictions. Evaluations on the CMAPSS dataset demonstrate MTFGN-SRL's superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in RUL prediction.

</details>


### [293] [Trains but Doesn't Learn: A Post-Training Delivery Benchmark for LLM Agents as Forward-Deployed Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25237)
*Weihang Ding,Junfei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“后训练即服务”（PTaaS）与受治理交付平面，用十阶段、事实驱动的oracle评估LLM agent能否可信地完成微调、评估与部署；核心风险是“训练了但没学到”（TBDL）的静默失败，并用验收门与校准检测器拦截。四个前沿agent在L40S/A100/H200、8B–70B开源基座上端到端运行，并与人类FDE对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只回答agent能否提升指标，无法回答其能否在预算、人工审批门和可复现性约束下被信任交付。将LLM agent放入FDE席位后，交付可信性成为关键缺口，尤其是损失下降、信号全绿但模型未优于基座的TBDL静默失败。

Method: 构建受治理交付平面：agent驱动十个阶段，oracle依据平台记录事实逐阶段打分；运营方验收门在付款前拦截TBDL运行；基于已知损坏运行校准的检测器在运行中标记严重损坏。所有场景先认证后评分。四个前沿agent（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-luna、Gemini 3.7 Flash、DeepSeek V4-Pro）在计量的L40S、A100、H200上跨8B–70B开源基座端到端运行，并设人类FDE基线在同一oracle下对比。

Result: 摘要未报告具体数值指标；实验设置覆盖四类前沿agent、三种GPU、8B–70B开放基座，并包含人类FDE对照臂。作者声称验收门可捕获所有TBDL运行，校准检测器可在运行中标记严重损坏，所有场景在评分前均通过认证。

Conclusion: 该工作把PTaaS中的可信交付形式化为可审计、可验收的交付问题，突出TBDL这一静默失败模式，并提出oracle评分、验收门与校准检测器相结合的治理机制。通过agent与人类FDE同oracle对比，为衡量agent能否替代或辅助FDE交付提供框架；具体通过率、成本、时延与检测指标仍需正文数据验证。

Abstract: Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.

</details>


### [294] [Correcting Within-Group Self-Selection Bias in Prioritized Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25297)
*Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"组内自选择"问题，指PER在随机环境下会扭曲同一状态-动作对内结果分布，并给出SAMPLE、AVG、MODEL三种修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优先经验回放（PER）依据绝对时序差分误差重放高优先级转移以提升样本效率，但在随机环境中，同一状态-动作对下不同结果会被按优先级挑选，导致被重放结果的分布偏离真实经验分布。作者将这一现象命名为"组内自选择"，并希望量化其对组内结果频率与平均Bellman目标的影响。

Method: 将PER分解为组间分配与条件兄弟选择两部分，在固定缓冲区下推导保持当前组级优先级质量的修正。提出三种方法：SAMPLE按PER选组后再均匀采样组内兄弟样本；AVG对兄弟样本的Bellman目标取平均；MODEL从经验全结果模型采样。并在精确状态-动作环境与MinAtar（用VQ-VAE近似分组）中验证。

Result: 在存在稀有高幅结果的精确状态-动作环境中，兄弟感知回放相比PER提升了学习效率，但匹配参数扫描显示调参可缩小部分差距。在MinAtar中，SAMPLE配合近似VQ-VAE分组，在五个游戏中的四个缓解了均值保持奖励尾部下的性能退化。

Conclusion: 兄弟感知回放能够在保持对高优先级状态-动作区域关注的同时，恢复其经验结果频率，从而修正PER在随机环境下的组内自选择偏差。

Abstract: Prioritized experience replay (PER) improves sample efficiency by replaying high-priority transitions, usually according to absolute temporal-difference error. In stochastic environments, PER can distort the distribution of realized outcomes replayed from transitions with the same state-action pair. We call this within-group self-selection. We quantify the resulting changes in within-group outcome frequencies and mean Bellman targets. We decompose PER into between-group allocation and conditional sibling selection, and derive fixed-buffer corrections that preserve current group-level priority mass: SAMPLE selects a group through PER and trains on a uniformly sampled sibling; AVG averages sibling Bellman targets; and MODEL samples from an empirical full-outcome model. In exact state-action environments with rare high-magnitude outcomes, sibling-aware replay improves learning efficiency over PER, although matched parameter sweeps show that tuning can narrow some gaps. In MinAtar, approximate VQ-VAE groups with SAMPLE mitigate degradation under mean-preserving reward tails in four of five games. Sibling-aware replay thus retains the focus on high-priority state-action regions while recovering their empirical outcome frequencies.

</details>


### [295] [Topological Signal Processing With Unoriented Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25310)
*Andrea Cavallo,Varun Sarathchandran,Geert Leus,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无向拓扑信号处理（UTSP）框架，用无向关联矩阵替代有向边界算子，并引入 interaction-order decomposition 及阶别正则化器，在真实数据上优于有向基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑信号处理（TSP）基于有向边界算子，是流信号或拓扑不变量相关任务的自然选择；但许多高阶信号本身无方向性，对其使用有向算子并不适定，因为会引入任意的单纯形方向选择。因此需要一个不依赖方向的高阶信号处理框架。

Method: 1）以无向关联矩阵替代有向边界算子，构建 UTSP 框架；2）证明任意单纯形层级间的无向关联矩阵与拉普拉斯矩阵具备类图的谱性质；3）由于去方向后失去 Hodge 分解，提出其无向对应——interaction-order decomposition，量化高阶信号由聚合低阶信号所能解释的程度；4）基于该分解导出对每种交互阶分别施加惩罚的重构正则化器。

Result: 实验（真实数据）表明，阶别感知（order-aware）正则化器优于有向基线；当信号能量在各阶之间分布不均衡时，增益最大。

Conclusion: UTSP 为无向高阶信号提供了适定且有效的处理框架；interaction-order decomposition 在失去 Hodge 分解后仍能刻画阶间结构，并可用于信号重构正则化，展现出实际优势。

Abstract: Topological signal processing (TSP) processes signals on simplicial complexes with oriented boundary operators, which is the natural choice for flow signals or when the topological invariants play a role for the task at hand. However, many higher-order signals carry no orientation, and applying oriented operators to them is not well-defined since it introduces an arbitrary choice of simplex orientation. We study an unoriented TSP (UTSP) framework that replaces oriented boundaries with unoriented incidence matrices. First, we show that unoriented incidence and Laplacian matrices between arbitrary simplicial levels admit graph-like spectral properties. Second, since dropping orientation removes the Hodge decomposition, we introduce an unoriented counterpart, termed interaction-order decomposition, which quantifies how much of a higher-order signal is explained by aggregating lower-order signals. Third, we use this decomposition to derive regularizers for signal reconstruction that penalize each interaction order separately. Experiments on real-world data show that the order-aware regularizers outperform oriented baselines, with the largest gains when the signal energy is unevenly distributed across orders.

</details>


### [296] [Spatiotemporal Kronecker Covariance Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25326)
*Andrea Cavallo,Athanasios Georgoutsos,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN)，一种时空图神经网络，用 Kronecker 乘积之和表示时空协方差矩阵，将空间与时间依赖解耦，并通过空间/时间滤波实现非线性、可谱分析且对有限样本估计误差稳定的预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列中存在跨空间和时间的复杂模式。传统 ST-PCA 等协方差统计工具只能处理线性关系，且在数据有限时易受估计误差影响；近期基于协方差的时空神经网络虽更稳定且具非线性，但忽略不同时间步之间的相关性。

Method: 提出 KVNN，将时空协方差矩阵建模为若干 Kronecker 乘积之和，使空间依赖与时间依赖解耦；在空间分量和时间分量上分别执行滤波操作，从而获得表达力强的处理能力，并支持严格的谱分析。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上，KVNN 取得较强预测性能，通常比竞争方法需要显著更少的可训练参数，并且在估计噪声下表现一致、稳定。

Conclusion: KVNN 通过 Kronecker 协方差解耦与时空滤波，理论上可证明对有限样本估计误差稳定，实践中兼顾预测精度与参数效率，克服了 ST-PCA 的线性与估计误差局限。

Abstract: Multivariate time series contain complex patterns that span across both space and time. While covariance-based statistical tools like spatiotemporal Principal Component Analysis (ST-PCA) help identify these patterns, they are limited to linear operations and prone to estimation errors with limited data. Recent covariance-based spatiotemporal neural networks offer more stable, non-linear alternatives, but they ignore correlations across different time steps. To solve this, we introduce the Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN), a temporal graph neural network that represents the spatiotemporal covariance matrix via a sum of Kronecker products where spatial and temporal dependencies are decoupled. By implementing filtering operations on spatial and temporal components, KVNNs achieve expressive processing capabilities, admit a rigorous spectral analysis, and are provably stable to finite-sample estimation errors, ultimately addressing all of ST-PCA's limitations. We show on five real-world datasets that KVNNs achieve strong forecasting performance, often requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters than competitive methods, and are consistent under estimation noise.

</details>


### [297] [MT-ProtBERT: Multi-task Learning ProtBERT for Intrinsically Disordered Proteins Classification with Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25334)
*Jian Sun,Kingshuk Ghosh,Lilianna Houston,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多任务版 ProtBERT（MT-ProtBERT），通过融合动态窗口掩码、多尺度一维卷积分类器与生化先验辅助任务，在低数据条件下实现对内在无序蛋白（IDP）的稳健建模，并在磷酸化位点预测与蛋白致密性预测上超越 PARROT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在无序蛋白（IDP）具有动态、无稳定三维构象、序列相似度低等特性。其构象异质性虽利于多样功能，却限制了传统实验手段对其构象的测定；叠加低序列相似度导致的数据稀缺，使得对相似/不相似 IDP 的分类与检测极为困难，而该任务对理解生物学与进化具有重要意义。

Method: 提出 MT-ProtBERT——面向低数据场景的 ProtBERT 多任务扩展。其核心组件包括：(1) 动态窗口掩码（Dynamic Window Masking）；(2) 多尺度一维卷积分类器（MS-Conv1D）；(3) 辅助目标，联合优化掩码语言建模（MLM）与生化信息驱动的任务。整体在有限数据下进行多任务学习。

Result: 在两个低数据任务上评估：(i) 短序列小数据集中的磷酸化位点预测（S/T/Y）；(ii) 两个小数据集（684 与 530 条序列，含与典型无序区长度相当的序列）上的蛋白致密性预测。MT-ProtBERT 在所有任务上一致优于基于 RNN 的 IDP 专用模型 PARROT。

Conclusion: 将自监督任务与生化信息驱动的任务相结合，并引入多尺度学习，能够在数据稀缺条件下对无序蛋白实现稳健建模。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered proteins (IDPs) differ from folded proteins in that they are dynamic, lack a stable three-dimensional conformation, and have low sequence similarity between similar proteins. The conformational heterogeneity of IDPs - while beneficial for their diverse functions - limits the use of traditional experimental tools to determine their conformation. The experimental difficulty, along with low sequence similarity, results in data scarcity, and makes it difficult to classify/detect IDPs that are similar or dissimilar, a task relevant to understand biology and evolution. We address this challenge using Multi-task ProtBERT (MT-ProtBERT), a multi-task extension of ProtBERT tailored for low-data regimes. MT-ProtBERT integrates Dynamic Window Masking, a Multi-Scale 1D Convolutional classifier (MS-Conv1D), and auxiliary objectives that jointly optimize masked language modeling and biochemistry-informed tasks. We evaluate this framework on two tasks under limited data: (i) phosphorylation site prediction (S/T/Y) in short sequences and small datasets, and (ii) protein compaction prediction on two small datasets (684 and 530 sequences), including sequences comparable in length to typical disordered regions. MT-ProtBERT consistently outperforms PARROT, an RNN-based IDP-specific model, across all tasks. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised and biochemistry-informed tasks, and multi-scale learning enables robust modeling of unstructured proteins under data scarcity.

</details>


### [298] [Concept Drift from a Causal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25340)
*Eduardo V. L. Barboza,Jean Paul Barddal,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角重新审视概念漂移，基于结构因果模型（SCM）提出按因果来源分类的漂移分类法，并构建可控的机制级数据流生成器，验证不同因果来源的漂移会引发不同的分布偏移与预测行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据流中概念漂移普遍存在，数据生成分布的变化会降低预测模型性能。然而现有定义大多仅将漂移刻画为联合分布 P(x, y) 的变化，未能区分数据生成过程中究竟是哪个组件发生了变化，缺乏对漂移因果来源的细粒度理解与评估手段。

Method: 作者基于结构因果模型（SCMs）提出概念漂移的因果视角，构建了一个按因果来源对漂移事件进行分类的分类法，涵盖外生变量变化、内生机制变化、混杂因子变化以及目标生成过程变化。在此框架上，设计了一个基于 SCM 的数据流生成器，用以模拟受控的机制级漂移事件；并整合因果发现方法，将数据流建立在真实世界的依赖结构之上。

Result: 实验刻画了每种漂移类型的分布效应，表明不同因果来源的漂移会诱发不同模式的分布偏移与预测行为；同时，基于真实依赖结构构建的数据流提供了更真实、更具信息量的评估场景，且利用生成数据能够提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了在研究与评估自适应学习方法时考虑因果结构的重要性，为非平稳环境中的因果感知评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Concept drift is a common phenomenon in real-world data streams, in which changes in the data-generating distribution can degrade predictive model performance. Most existing definitions characterize drift as changes in the joint distribution $P(\mathbf{x}, y)$, without distinguishing which component of the data-generating process has changed. In this work, we introduce a causal perspective on concept drift based on Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We propose a taxonomy that categorizes drift events by their causal origin, including changes in exogenous variables, endogenous mechanisms, confounders, and target-generating processes. Building on this framework, we develop an SCM-based data stream generator that simulates controlled mechanism-level drift events. Our experiments empirically characterize the distributional effects of each drift type and show that drifts with different causal origins induce distinct patterns of distribution shift and predictive behavior. Furthermore, by integrating causal discovery methods, we use our framework to construct data streams grounded in real-world dependency structures, enabling more realistic and informative evaluation scenarios. We also demonstrate that leveraging the generated data can improve downstream performance. These results highlight the importance of accounting for causal structure when studying and evaluating adaptive learning methods, and establish a foundation for causally-aware evaluation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [299] [Extending FunctionGemma for Practical On-Device Mobile Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25373)
*Ali Rezagholizadeh,Soheila Samiee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 FunctionGemma 270M-it 扩展到 Android 设备控制函数调用，构建约 9,500 条、15 类任务的 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 数据集并微调，端到端准确率由 29.3%/17.2% 提升至 76.5%；联合训练模型在扩大类别覆盖的同时保留较强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧助手需要能将自然语言映射为本地系统操作的函数调用模型，但现有资源多聚焦 Web API 或窄范围移动动作，缺少面向实际 Android 工作流、schema 可验证的数据与模型。

Method: 提出合成且经 schema 校验的 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 数据集，约 9,500 条对话，覆盖消息、电话、相机/截图、亮度、设备状态查询、手电筒、应用管理等 15 类设备控制任务；基于 FunctionGemma 270M-it，使用 TRL 监督微调与 completion-only loss，训练扩展专家模型，并与 Google 的 MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE 联合训练得到组合模型。

Result: 在 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 上，端到端准确率从基座模型 29.3%、Google Mobile-Actions 变体 17.2% 提升到 76.5%。组合模型在 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 上保持 76.5%，在 MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE 上达到 82.3%，低于 Google Mobile-Actions 专家的 90.3%，即以 8.0 个百分点性能权衡换取类别覆盖翻倍。

Conclusion: 发布数据集、微调模型、可复现训练/评估流程及 Android 演示，表明紧凑的本地函数调用是实现低延迟、隐私保护移动助手的可行路径。

Abstract: On-device assistants require function-calling models that map natural language to local system actions, but existing resources emphasize web APIs or narrow mobile-action catalogs. We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>


### [300] [Deep Reinforcement Learning on Item-Compatibility Graphs for One-Dimensional Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25397)
*M. Aslı Aydın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于一维装箱问题（1D-BPP）的端到端、尺寸无关图强化学习框架：将装箱建模为物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，用GNN actor-critic学习合并可行部分箱，经随机束搜索解码，可零样本泛化到任意规模实例，并在BPPLIB上取得优于构造启发式和多数学习基线、逼近分组遗传算法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1D-BPP是经典NP难组合优化问题，应用广泛；深度强化学习已成为数据驱动优化范式，但已有学习型装箱方法多集中于2D/3D变体，面向1D-BPP的智能学习求解器仍较缺乏。作者希望填补这一空白，并提升学习型方法在1D-BPP上的泛化性与稳定性。

Method: 将装箱过程形式化为物品兼容图上的MDP，图中动作表示合并两个可兼容的部分箱，从而引入结构化知识表示；采用图神经网络actor-critic策略提取关系特征，通过强化学习训练，并以随机束搜索解码；框架端到端、尺寸无关，单一模型可零样本迁移到任意规模实例。实验系统比较了图编码器、DRL算法、奖励函数、训练分布和超参数。

Result: 在完整BPPLIB基准上零样本评估：相比构造启发式，平均最优性间隙从2.66%降至2.31%，在结构化实例上提升最大；与同基准学习基线相比，在九类实例族中的多数上间隙更低且跨分布更稳定；在最难的基准族上，不使用列生成和整数规划求解器，仍优于依赖列生成与整数规划的先进学习求解器；但分组遗传算法整体仍领先。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于物品兼容图的图强化学习可有效求解1D-BPP，并具备尺寸无关的零样本泛化能力和较好的稳定性；其性能已超过构造启发式与多数学习基线，并在困难实例族上优于部分SOTA学习求解器，但与分组遗传算法仍有残余差距，作者进一步分析了差距来源。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with applications ranging from logistics and manufacturing to cloud resource management. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has become a competitive paradigm for data-driven optimization, most learned packing methods target 2D and 3D variants, and intelligent learned solvers for 1D-BPP remain scarce. In this paper, we present a novel end-to-end, size-agnostic graph reinforcement learning framework for 1D-BPP. We formulate the packing process as a Markov decision process on an item-compatibility graph, serving as a structural knowledge representation in which every action merges two partial bins that fit together. A graph neural network actor-critic policy extracts relational features from this representation and is trained through reinforcement learning and decoded by stochastic beam search, enabling a single trained model to generalize zero-shot to instances of any size. We conduct a systematic empirical study across graph encoders, DRL algorithms, reward functions, training distributions, and hyperparameters. Evaluated zero-shot on the full BPPLIB benchmark against a constructive heuristic, a grouping genetic algorithm, and recent learned methods, our data-driven policy lowers the mean optimality gap of the constructive heuristic from 2.66\% to 2.31\%, with the largest gains on structured instances. Against learned baselines evaluated on the same benchmark, it attains a lower gap on most of the nine families and is far more stable across instance distributions. On the hardest benchmark family, it outperforms a state-of-the-art learned solver that relies on column generation and integer programming, while using no solver at all. A grouping genetic algorithm remains ahead overall, and we analyze where and why the residual gap arises.

</details>


### [301] [Predictive Uncertainty for Neural CAE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25430)
*Kaustubh Tangsali,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Kelvin Lee,Carmelo Gonzales,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将GP、Concrete MC Dropout与深度集成三类不确定性量化方法适配到几何条件神经代理，在三个工业CAE数据集上评估其可信度、误差定位、对陌生输入的响应及对派生工程量的信息性；结果显示方法优劣随数据集和评价指标而变，应依据下游CAE决策选择UQ方法与评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理可显著加速CAE工作流，但若用于设计，其不确定性估计必须在变化的几何、空间预测场和工程关注量上保持有意义。因此需要考察既有UQ方法在几何条件神经代理中的实际表现。

Method: 比较一种闭式方法与两种采样式方法：基于高斯过程（GP）的方法、Concrete Monte Carlo Dropout和深度集成（Deep Ensembles）。在三个大规模、工业相关的CAE数据集上评估，涵盖外部空气动力学与碰撞动力学。评估维度包括：预测不确定性的量级是否可信、能否识别误差更大的位置、是否对不熟悉输入作出响应，以及是否对派生工程量保持信息性；并在DrivAerStar上以验证集重标定检验区间覆盖率。

Result: 在三种方法均可比较的DrivAerStar数据集上，各方法通常会给预测误差更大的位置分配更高不确定性；基于验证集的重标定使独立同分布测试集上的区间覆盖率接近名义水平。AirFRANS与汽车碰撞数据集也显示出有用的误差排序和区间估计，但各方法的相对表现会随数据集和评价准则而变化。

Conclusion: UQ方法与评估指标的选择应服务于预期的下游CAE决策，而不存在跨数据集、跨准则普遍最优的单一方案。

Abstract: Neural surrogates can substantially accelerate computer-aided engineering (CAE) workflows, but their use in design requires uncertainty estimates that remain meaningful across varying geometries, spatial prediction fields, and engineering quantities of interest. We investigate how established uncertainty quantification (UQ) approaches behave when adapted to geometry-conditioned neural surrogates. We compare one closed-form and two sampling-based approaches-a Gaussian process (GP)-based method, concrete Monte Carlo (MC) dropout, and deep ensembles-and evaluate them on three large, industry-relevant CAE datasets for external aerodynamics and crash dynamics.
  We examine whether predicted uncertainties have credible magnitudes, identify locations with larger prediction errors, respond to unfamiliar inputs, and remain informative for derived engineering quantities. On the DrivAerStar dataset, where all three methods are compared, each generally assigns higher uncertainty to locations with larger prediction errors, and validation-based rescaling brings interval coverage close to nominal on a disjoint in-distribution test set. Results on AirFRANS and automotive crash also show useful error ranking and interval estimates, but the relative performance of the methods changes with the dataset and evaluation criterion. UQ methods and evaluation metrics should therefore be selected based on the intended downstream CAE decision.

</details>


### [302] [Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25433)
*Sanjay Surendranath Girija,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Yanhao Jiang,Shawn Andrews,Lukasz Heldt,Yi Wu,Aditya Mahajan,Mohit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Lightweight Ranking Heads（Light Heads）框架，用于在已有多任务排序模型中动态注入新任务，避免主干模型冷启动与重训练，缓解任务冲突，并将多任务实验迭代周期从数周缩短到数天，已在 YouTube 规模落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级推荐系统依赖复杂多任务排序模型。新增预测任务常带来瓶颈：一方面可能与已有任务产生负向任务冲突；另一方面需要重训练主干模型与下游模型，或调优奖励组合公式，导致开发与实验周期过长、实验速度慢。因此核心挑战是提升实验迭代速度。

Method: Light Heads 面向持续在线学习环境，支持将新任务动态注入现有多任务排序模型；无需模型冷启动和主干模型重训练。其关键技术包括：使用 stop-gradient 与无状态每日训练（stateless daily training）严格隔离新任务，降低负向任务冲突风险；采用集中式配置，使 Light Heads 可同时添加到多个模型，加速训练数据生成与下游模型协同训练。

Result: 在 YouTube 规模成功部署；将多任务实验迭代周期从数周降至数天。论文进一步描述了系统架构，分析无状态冷启动 heads 的训练动态，将其性能与完整 heads 对比，并展示 Light Heads 如何支持新排序任务的快速 A/B 实验与部署，产生可衡量的生产价值。

Conclusion: Light Heads 通过动态注入、任务隔离与集中式配置，在不大规模重训练主干模型的前提下支持新排序任务快速实验和上线，显著提升多任务推荐系统的实验迭代效率，并具备大规模生产部署价值。

Abstract: Modern production-scale recommender systems rely on complex, multi-task ranking models. Introducing new prediction tasks into these massive systems often causes bottlenecks - it risks negative task conflicts with existing tasks, and can lead to long development and experimentation cycles due to the expensive retraining of backbone models and downstream models or tuning of reward combination formulas. To address the critical challenge of slow experimentation velocity, we introduce the Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) framework. Designed for continuous online learning environments, Light Heads enable the dynamic injection of new tasks into existing multi-task ranking models, effectively obviating the need for model cold-starting and retraining of backbone models. By utilizing stop-gradients and stateless daily training, this design strictly isolates new tasks, mitigating the risk of adverse task conflicts. Crucially, this framework uses a centralized configuration that allows Light Heads to be added to multiple models simultaneously, unblocking faster training data generation and co-training of downstream models. Successfully deployed at YouTube scale, this approach reduces the iteration cycle for multi-task experimentation from several weeks to days. In this paper, we detail the system architecture, analyze the training dynamics of stateless cold-started heads, compare their performance to full heads, and demonstrate how Light Heads have enabled the rapid A/B experimentation and deployment of new ranking tasks that yield measurable production value.

</details>


### [303] [PermuFormer: Multi-Task Pretraining for Permutation Representation in Algebraic Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25438)
*Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向代数组合学中排列任务的预训练方法PermuFormer，一个在28亿token多任务多编码语料上训练的自回归Transformer，作为微调起点优于从头训练、MLP基线及同规模通用语言模型，并分析了其内部学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI for math多聚焦于用前沿推理模型以语言解决明确定义的问题，而专用小模型仍是生态系统的重要组成。专用模型通常直接基于数学对象（如排列、图、数列）训练而非文本描述，但常见的从头训练做法可能限制其学习能捕捉数学多面性的领域感知表示。

Method: 提出PermuFormer：一个自回归Transformer，在包含多任务、多编码方式的28亿token语料上进行预训练，面向代数组合学中排列相关任务。

Result: PermuFormer作为微调起点，在预训练未见的基础任务和更复杂的研究级任务上均有效，常优于同架构从头训练、MLP基线及同规模通用语言模型微调。内部机制分析显示，部分任务可直接从提示的内部表示线性解码，而其他任务需多轮生成后才能解码出答案。

Conclusion: 预训练可为排列相关任务提供有效的领域感知表示，利于下游微调；同时揭示了PermuFormer解决训练任务的部分内部机制，即不同任务对内部表示解码的需求存在差异。

Abstract: Diverse pretraining has been shown to be an effective method for learning reusable, domain-aware representations that provide a starting point for fine-tuning on downstream tasks. While much of the excitement in AI for math has been concentrated in the use of frontier reasoning models to solve well-specified problems through the medium of language, narrow, specialized models remain an important component of the AI for math ecosystem. In contrast to large language models, specialized models are usually trained directly on the mathematical objects themselves (e.g., graphs, sequences of numbers) rather than the textual descriptions that characterize these objects. However, the common practice of training specialists from scratch may limit their ability to develop domain-aware representations that capture the multifaceted nature of mathematics. In this paper, we describe an approach to pretraining for permutation-focused tasks in algebraic combinatorics.
