We introduce PermuFormer, an autoregressive transformer trained on a 2.8 billion token multi-task, multi-encoding corpus. We show that PermuFormer is an effective starting point for fine-tuning on basic tasks unseen during pretraining and more complex research-level tasks, frequently outperforming the same architecture trained from scratch, baseline MLPs, and a fine-tuned generic language model of comparable size. We also analyze some of the internal mechanisms by which PermuFormer learns to solve training tasks. For example, we show that while some tasks can be linearly decoded directly from the internal representation of the prompt, other tasks require multiple rounds of generation before the answer can be decoded.

</details>


### [304] [Mean Velocity Matching: Rethinking Generative Dynamics in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25444)
*Yunhong Zhang,Changjie Cao,Zhihua Zhang,Bingli Liu,Zongjie Cao,Zongyong Cui,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出均值速度匹配（Mean Velocity Matching, MVM），用单一学习场直接参数化扩散模型的随机逆向动力学，无需额外估计或重建得分，并引入 √t 缩放参数化以解决 t=0 附近目标无界的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于速度的生成模型只需学习单一传输场，形式简洁，但其标准形式是确定性的；若扩展到随机情形，通常需要额外的得分信息或速度—得分中间重建步骤。作者希望在保留单场预测简洁性的同时，直接支持随机逆向动力学。

Method: MVM 构造一个高斯扰动过程，使其复原导向速度 (x_0-x_t)/t 的条件期望直接构成逆向 SDE 的漂移项，因此单一学习场即可参数化随机逆向过程，无需单独估计或重建得分。由于直接回归该速度在 t→0 时无界，MVM 引入 √t 缩放参数化，在保持逆向动力学不变的同时给出有界训练目标。同一学习场还诱导出确定性概率流 ODE，从而在统一框架下研究随机与确定性采样。

Result: 基于 Transformer 的生成模型在 ImageNet 32×32 上达到 \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID 的 FID（\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE NFE），在 ImageNet 256×256 上达到 \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID 的 FID（\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE NFE）。受控的 SDE—ODE 对比表明：在极低 NFE 下 ODE 表现更好，而在函数评估次数充足时随机逆向过程取得更低的 FID。

Conclusion: MVM 为随机逆向动力学提供了一种直接的单场参数化方法，在保持有竞争力的生成质量的同时，统一了随机与确定性采样，并揭示了二者在不同 NFE 下的性能权衡。

Abstract: This work studies prediction parameterization for stochastic generative dynamics in diffusion models. Existing velocity-based generative models provide the simplicity of learning a single transport field, but their standard formulation is deterministic, whereas stochastic extensions generally require additional score information or an intermediate velocity-to-score reconstruction. To retain single-field prediction while directly supporting stochastic reverse dynamics, this paper introduces Mean Velocity Matching (MVM). MVM constructs a Gaussian perturbation process for which the conditional expectation of a restoration-oriented velocity, $(x_0-x_t)/t$, directly forms the reverse-SDE drift. Consequently, a single learned field is sufficient to parameterize the stochastic reverse process without separately estimating or reconstructing the score. Because direct regression of this velocity becomes unbounded near $t=0$, MVM further introduces a $\sqrt{t}$-scaled parameterization that preserves the reverse dynamics while yielding a bounded training target. The same learned field also induces a deterministic probability-flow ODE, enabling stochastic and deterministic sampling to be studied within a unified formulation. Experiments with Transformer-based generative models achieve an FID of $\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID$ at \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $32\times32$ and $\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID$ at \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $256\times256$. Controlled SDE--ODE comparisons further show that the ODE performs better under very low NFE, whereas the stochastic reverse process achieves lower FID when sufficient function evaluations are available. These results demonstrate that MVM provides a direct single-field parameterization of stochastic reverse dynamics while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [305] [A Practical Recipe for Semi-Supervised Federated ASR: Online Pseudo-Labels with Server Update Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25471)
*Wonho Bae,Zakaria Aldeneh,Martin Pelikan,Jan "Honza" Silovsky,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Sheikh Shams Azam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究半监督联邦学习（SSFL）在自动语音识别（ASR）中的训练稳定性问题：伪标签误差会在输出序列和训练轮次中累积并导致发散。作者指出，要缩小与全监督联邦学习的差距，关键在于耦合的“教师（teacher）”和“锚点（anchor）”两个设计轴，并据此给出训练准则。实验在11组对比中9组超越最强基线，域内平均提升20.8%，跨域提升10.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSFL利用客户端无标签数据和服务器少量有标签种子数据训练模型，由教师生成伪标签。ASR场景尤其脆弱：伪标签错误会沿输出序列传播，并在多轮训练中复合，最终导致模型发散，与全监督联邦学习存在较大差距。因此，作者希望系统分析并缩小这一差距。

Method: 围绕两个耦合设计轴展开分析：1）教师轴：比较“每客户端在线教师”（客户端自身演化模型）、“广播全局教师”（每轮固定的单服务器模型）以及“过渡教师”（在第r轮由全局切换为在线）；2）锚点轴：服务器在轮次之间持续用有标签数据训练，以稳定在线教师并控制收敛；同时考察数据增强、批大小等影响输入噪声和梯度噪声的设置，以及种子数据离散度及其与客户端数据重叠程度对所需稳定化程度的域依赖性。

Result: 在线教师单独使用会发散，但一旦被锚点稳定，便可匹配甚至超过广播全局教师：域内优势明显，域偏移下具备竞争力。随着种子数据增强，在线教师优势缩小；“过渡教师”可匹配或超过二者。服务器必须在轮次间继续用有标签数据训练，否则在线教师会漂移；这种交替训练比种子模型本身更主导收敛。激进的教师选择只有在锚点稳定训练后才有效，且对数据增强和批大小高度敏感。方法在11组对比中9组优于最强先前方法，域内平均提升20.8%，跨域平均提升10.0%，缩小了与全监督联邦学习的差距。

Conclusion: 教师轴与锚点轴不可分割：更强的伪标签教师需要匹配的服务器锚点稳定机制，稳定化强度取决于域特性与种子数据分布。该研究为ASR中的SSFL提供了可操作的设计准则，并显著缩小了与全监督联邦学习的性能差距。

Abstract: Semi-supervised federated learning (SSFL) trains models on clients' unlabeled data using a teacher to generate pseudo-labels, with a small labeled seed dataset on the server. Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is particularly fragile here: pseudo-label errors compound across the output sequence and across training rounds into divergence, leaving a large gap to fully-supervised FL. We show that closing this gap turns on two coupled design axes -- the teacher (which model generates the pseudo-labels) and the anchor (the server-side updates on labeled data that stabilize training). On the teacher axis, a per-client online teacher (each client's own evolving model) diverges on its own, but once stabilized it matches or beats the broadcast global teacher (one server model, fixed within a round) -- decisively in-domain and competitively under domain shift. As the seed grows stronger and the online teacher's advantage narrows, a transitioning teacher (global $\rightarrow$ online at round $r$) matches or beats both. On the anchor axis, the server must keep training on labeled data between rounds -- otherwise the online teacher drifts -- and this interleaving, more than the seed model, governs convergence. The two axes are inseparable: aggressive teacher choices pay off only once the anchor stabilizes training, which is highly sensitive to data augmentation and batch size -- the settings that govern how much input and gradient noise the server injects. How much stabilization is needed is domain-dependent, governed by the dispersion of the seed data and its overlap with client data. These findings yield guidelines for SSFL in ASR training, improving over the strongest prior method on 9 of 11 pairs, by $20.8\%$ on average in-domain and $10.0\%$ cross-domain, narrowing the gap to fully-supervised FL.

</details>


### [306] [Terminal Shrinkage Averaging Reveals a Schedule-Estimator Interaction in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25482)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“终端收缩平均”（TSA），通过在最终原始参数与近期检查点平均之间插值，解耦学习率调度与模型估计器，从而在训练末期兼顾优化进展并降低终端方差；在NanoChat深度22实验中提升验证质量，并在达到GPT-2级别的时间上快于公开基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM预训练直接返回最终原始迭代权重，将两个本可分离的设计耦合在一起：一是决定参数轨迹的学习率调度，二是用于部署的模型估计器（原始最终迭代或检查点平均）。促进优化进展的调度未必能最小化最终权重的方差。作者希望分离二者，以便在训练后期继续保持进展，同时降低返回模型的终端波动。

Method: 提出Terminal Shrinkage Averaging（TSA）：在最终原始迭代与近期检查点平均之间进行插值，以权衡近期优化进展与终端方差。方法上先在局部二次近似下分析TSA如何改变偏好的终端学习率调度，再通过一系列受控的NanoChat实验检验调度与估计器之间的交互作用。

Result: 受控NanoChat实验验证了TSA与终端学习率调度之间的交互效应；在深度22的NanoChat上，联合使用调度与估计器可提升验证质量。此外，一项达到GPT-2级别的time-to-GPT-2运行比实验所用公开基线更快完成，提供了基准加速的初步证据。

Conclusion: 将学习率调度与模型估计器显式解耦是有效的：TSA可在训练末期维持优化进展并减少终端方差，其收益可迁移到更深模型并改善验证质量，且初步显示可加速达到GPT-2级别性能的时间。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) pretraining conventionally returns the raw final iterate. This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training while reducing variation in the returned model. To this end, we propose \emph{Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)}, which interpolates between the raw final iterate and the average of recent checkpoints to balance recent progress against terminal variation. We analyze how TSA changes the preferred terminal learning-rate schedule under a local quadratic approximation and test this interaction through a sequence of controlled NanoChat experiments. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting gains transfer to depth-22 NanoChat, where the combined schedule and estimator improve validation quality. A qualifying time-to-GPT-2 run also finishes faster than the public baseline used in our experiments, providing preliminary evidence of benchmark acceleration.

</details>


### [307] [Learning Defensive Policies against Diverse Inference Attacks for Smart Meter Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25484)
*Ruichang Zhang,Mustafa A. Mustafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究智能电表数据的隐私保护问题，将防御非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）攻击建模为黑盒推理防御，提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架，通过电池负载整形注入具有家电级特征的误导性信号，从而提升对未知异构攻击者的泛化防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电表数据能提供细粒度家庭能耗可见性，但NILM攻击可从聚合信号中推断家电级用电信息，暴露用户行为隐私。实际中攻击者模型未知且异构，传统防御难以泛化，因此需要一种面向未知攻击者的鲁棒隐私保护方法。

Method: 论文将智能电表隐私保护形式化为黑盒推理防御问题，目标是在多样化、未见攻击者下降低家电级信息可恢复性。提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架，学习基于电池的负载整形策略，向聚合信号注入真实但误导性的家电级签名，以破坏NILM利用的结构化模式。采用自监督聚合结构隐私探针，提供基于重建误差的替代奖励；并构建签名库，使扰动与家电相关且可通过电池控制物理实现。理论上说明代理引导优化可提升攻击者多样性下的推理鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实数据集UK-DALE和REDD上实验，方法表现出较强的跨模型与跨家电泛化能力。面对六种未见NILM攻击者，在UK-DALE的四种家电和REDD的五种家电上，所提防御分别使家电级RMSE平均提高107%和166%，F1分数分别降低79%和80%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于代理引导分层强化学习与电池负载整形的黑盒防御，能够有效干扰NILM攻击并泛化到未见攻击者与不同家电，为智能电表隐私保护提供了物理可实现且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Smart meter (SM) data provides fine-grained visibility into household energy consumption, but also exposes users to privacy risks. Inference attacks, known as non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM), can perform appliance-level inference from aggregate signals and recover sensitive behavioral patterns. In practice, attacker models are unknown and heterogeneous, making robust defense challenging. We formulate SM privacy protection as a black-box inference defense problem, aiming to reduce the recoverability of appliance-level information while generalizing across diverse and unseen attackers. We propose a proxy-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns battery-based load-shaping policies to inject realistic but misleading appliance-level signatures into the aggregate signal, thereby disrupting the structured patterns exploited by NILM. A self-supervised aggregate-structure privacy probe provides a reconstruction-error-based surrogate reward for disrupting recoverable load structure, while a signature library makes the perturbations appliance-relevant and physically realizable through battery control. We provide theoretical rationale showing that proxy-guided optimization improves inference robustness under attacker diversity. Experiments on real-world datasets UK-DALE and REDD demonstrate strong cross-model and cross-appliance generalization. Across six unseen NILM attackers, covering four appliances on UK-DALE and five on REDD, our proposed defense increases average appliance-level RMSE by 107% and 166%, respectively, while reducing F1 score by 79% and 80%.

</details>


### [308] [Continuous Optimization for p-adic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25501)
*Julian Salazar,Dimitri Kanevsky,Matt Harvey,Pascal Getreuer,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个面向 p 进参数模型的连续梯度下降方法：用 Berkovich 仿射线把完全不连通的 Q_p 扩展为路径连通、可微的度量树，从而实现可解释的梯度优化与反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 p 进原生优化器多为离散/组合搜索，因为 Q_p 完全不连通，且标准损失在极小值之外平坦，难以进行连续优化。

Method: 在 Q_p 的 Berkovich 仿射线上工作：这是保持等距性并唯一扩展解析映射的规范路径连通扩张，形成可解释、具局部导数的度量树；据此定义梯度下降、动量、Adam 及反向传播。

Result: 近似方法能有效学习系数在 Q_p 中的线性模型以完成模运算（一种 R 上线性模型无法表达的 XOR 类任务）；还展示了动量与 Adam 变体、线性回归，以及二进编码层级（Quillian 语义网络）分类。

Conclusion: 该工作为 p 进机器学习提供了连续可微优化框架，回应 Martins (2025) 提出的开放问题，并开源库 https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml。

Abstract: We present the first method for native, continuous gradient descent for machine learning models with $p$-adic parameters. Existing native optimizers are discrete, mostly combinatorial searches, as the $p$-adic numbers $\mathbb{Q}_p$ are totally disconnected, with standard losses that are flat away from their minima. To enable continuous optimization, we propose working with $\mathbb{Q}_p$ via its Berkovich affine line: a canonical, path-connected expansion of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ that preserves its isometries and uniquely extends its analytic maps. This hull is a metric tree with interpretable points and local derivatives, which we show enables effective optimizers and backpropagation. We formulate gradient descent and show that its approximations efficiently learn linear models with coefficients in $\mathbb{Q}_p$ to do modular arithmetic, an XOR-like task not expressible by linear models in $\mathbb{R}$. We also demonstrate momentum and Adam variants, linear regression, and classification on binary-encoded hierarchies (Quillian semantic networks), addressing open problems posed by Martins (2025). Library at https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml

</details>


### [309] [Hill Sampling for Test-Time Scaling: A Simple and Better Alternative to Repeated Sampling, Evolution, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25510)
*Jacob Beck,Philip V. Ogren,Ari Kobren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Hill Sampling：一种极简的测试时计算策略——冻结 LLM 反复采样程序编辑，保留当前最佳程序，并以该程序为条件继续采样；在圆填充、集合和差、Erdos 最小重叠问题上验证其有效性，并质疑复杂进化搜索/测试时训练的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升 LLM 解决可验证科学/算法问题的方法常依赖复杂进化搜索框架或测试时参数更新，但尚不清楚这些机制是否必要。作者希望探究：在测试时投入额外计算时，最简单的策略能走多远。

Method: 提出 Hill Sampling：使用冻结 LLM，反复采样候选程序编辑；维护目前已找到的最佳程序；后续所有采样都以该最佳程序为条件。评估任务包括圆填充、集合和/差、Erdos 最小重叠问题；使用三种开放权重模型。另对直接作用于 LLM 权重的进化策略（ES）进行大规模研究（按参数量计），并比较不同学习率与随机扰动、token 采样随机性等。

Result: Hill Sampling 在圆填充上取得已发表方法中的新 SOTA；在 Erdos 最小重叠问题上优于 AlphaEvolve 参考结果；在有限集合和/差问题上表现强劲。圆填充和 Erdos 结果仅需八张 NVIDIA H100 GPU 数小时墙钟时间。测试时 ES 学习权重的效果反而差于将 ES 学习率设为零；零学习率下仍在固定随机扰动下搜索权重空间，这些扰动有助于探索，但 token 采样随机性更强；重复采样仍明显弱于 Hill Sampling。

Conclusion: 测试时计算分配可采用简单策略：反复对当前已验证的最佳解采样编辑，再考虑引入档案、多样性机制、进化脚手架或测试时参数学习等额外复杂性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can improve solutions to verifiable scientific and algorithmic problems by spending additional computation at test time. Recent systems achieve strong results with increasingly elaborate evolutionary search harnesses or by updating model parameters during test-time training. We ask how much of this machinery is necessary. We introduce Hill Sampling, a simple procedure that repeatedly samples candidate program edits from a frozen LLM, retains the best program found so far, and conditions all subsequent samples on that program. We evaluate the method on circle packing, sums/differences of sets, and Erdos' minimum-overlap problem using three open-weight models. Hill Sampling sets a new state of the art on circle packing among published methods, improves over the AlphaEvolve reference on Erdos' minimum-overlap problem, and achieves strong results on sums and differences of finite sets. The circle-packing and Erdos results require only hours of wall-clock time on eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs. To our knowledge, we also conduct, the largest study, by parameter count, of evolution strategies (ES) applied directly to LLM weights at test time. Surprisingly, learning the weights is worse than setting the ES learning rate to zero: at zero learning rate, the method is still searching in weight space through fixed random perturbations. Those perturbations can help exploration, but randomness from token sampling is stronger still, and repeated sampling remains substantially weaker than Hill Sampling. These results suggest a simple test-time compute allocation strategy: repeatedly sample edits to the best verified solution found so far, before introducing additional complexity such as adding archives, diversity mechanisms, evolutionary scaffolds, or test-time parameter learning.

</details>


### [310] [A JEPA Recipe for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25541)
*Mingyu Jeon,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在表格基础模型先验上引入JEPA式潜在自监督，早期运行中潜在项坍缩并导致编码器退化为常数映射；本文给出使潜在项与数值目标共同收敛的配方，但JEPA臂在147个真实数据集上仍逊于仅数值臂，且训练步数与墙钟时间更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型通过上下文预测单元格值，而世界模型自监督（如JEPA）要求在表示空间进行预测。作者尝试将二者结合，但初步运行中联合嵌入预测架构的潜在项坍缩，并将编码器拖成常数映射。因此需要找到一种配方，使潜在项能在收敛时与数值目标并存，并公平评估其性能与计算代价。

Method: 关键设计：数值预测头从编码器场读取，而非从预测器读取；潜在目标采用指数移动平均（EMA）差值。为公平比较JEPA臂与仅数值臂，两臂均按平台期规则停止训练，不设固定步数预算。作者指出，固定训练时域会把减速误判为性能上限，因为仅数值臂在常规预算之后仍在持续提升。

Result: 收敛后，每臂一次运行，在147个真实数据集上，JEPA臂整体落后于仅数值臂：分类胜负比为32:70（按数据集名称去重后为29:63），回归为8:24。分类差距较小，回归差距更大；各分层与各基准的胜负计数方向一致。JEPA臂达到平台期需要1.42倍训练步数和1.66倍墙钟时间。

Conclusion: 所提配方可避免潜在项坍缩，使JEPA潜在目标与数值目标共同收敛，但在当前设置下并未带来性能收益，反而在多数数据集上弱于仅数值目标，并增加训练成本。未来需进一步分析表格基础模型中潜在自监督的价值与效率权衡。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models learn to predict cell values in context, whereas world-model self-supervision asks for prediction in representation space (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023). On a tabular foundation-model prior, the latent term of a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) collapsed in our earlier runs and took the encoder with it to a constant map. We report a recipe under which the latent term survives to convergence beside the value objective: the value head reads the encoder field rather than the predictor, and the target is an exponential moving average (EMA) difference. To bound its cost against the value-only arm, both arms train until a plateau rule stops them, with no fixed step budget. A fixed horizon had confounded a slowdown with a ceiling, since the value-only arm was still improving well past the usual budget. At convergence, in one run per arm, the JEPA arm trails the value-only arm across 147 real datasets, 32:70 wins to losses on classification (29:63 with one entry per dataset name) and 8:24 on regression, the margin small on classification and wider on regression, and the count leans the same way in each stratum and each benchmark. The JEPA arm (jepa) needs 1.42 times as many steps as the value-only arm (ds), and 1.66 times its wall-clock, to reach its plateau.

</details>


### [311] [DefaultGNN: A Dual-Perspective GNN Framework for Predicting Corporate Default from Buyer-Seller Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25542)
*Junghoon Kim,Hyunsung Kim,Seungyoon Choi,KyoungYong Park,Jihun Lee,YongGu Ji,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用六年电子发票交易数据研究企业间风险传播，发现交易风险具有角色依赖性与规模依赖性，进而构建买方/卖方双视角多层交易网络，提出双视角图神经网络框架 DefaultGNN 用于企业违约预测，并在与持牌信用评级机构合作中验证其可补充现有信用评分、在不大幅增加违约风险的情况下将通过率提升 7–11 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业违约预测是金融风险管理的核心问题，但传统信用模型高度依赖财务报表，而许多企业财务报表稀疏或不可得。企业交易网络提供了反映真实经济活动的补充视角，但风险如何通过买卖关系传播仍研究不足。

Method: 基于六年真实电子税务发票数据开展大规模实证研究，将交易历史与违约事件关联，揭示交易驱动风险的角色依赖性（买方或卖方）和规模依赖性。据此构建买方视角与卖方视角的多层交易网络，并提出 DefaultGNN：一种双视角图神经网络企业违约预测框架。该框架整合两个视角，建模风险沿交易关系流动，并提供基于网络的可解释性可视化，展示困境交易伙伴如何贡献违约风险。

Result: DefaultGNN 相对基于属性的模型和基于图的基线均取得显著提升，尤其对自身风险信号有限的企业效果更明显。与持牌信用评级机构合作验证表明，其预测能补充现有信用评分模型，在获批企业违约风险不增加的情况下，将通过率提升 7–11 个百分点。

Conclusion: 交易网络中的风险传播具有角色与规模异质性，双视角图神经网络能够有效利用买卖关系信息提升企业违约预测，并具备可解释性与现实业务补充价值，可作为传统信用评分体系的有效增强工具。源代码已公开。

Abstract: Corporate default prediction is a core problem in financial risk management, yet traditional credit models rely heavily on financial statements that are often sparse or unavailable for many firms. Corporate transaction networks offer a complementary view of real economic activity, but how risk propagates through buyer-seller relationships remains underexplored. We conduct a large-scale empirical study using real-world electronic tax-invoice data spanning six years that links transaction histories with default events, revealing that transaction-driven risk is both role-dependent (buyer or seller) and scale-dependent. Based on these findings, we construct multiplex buyer-view and seller-view transaction networks and propose DefaultGNN, a dual-perspective graph neural network-based framework for corporate default prediction. DefaultGNN integrates both views to model how risk flows through transactional relationships, achieving strong improvements over both attribute-based and graph-based baselines, especially for firms with limited intrinsic risk signals. We further provide interpretable network-based explanations by visualizing how distressed trading partners contribute to default risk. In collaboration with a licensed credit rating agency, we validate that DefaultGNN's predictions complement existing credit scoring models, improving approval rates by 7-11%p without increasing default risk among approved firms. The source code can be found at https://github.com/jhkim611/DefaultGNN

</details>


### [312] [SambaGraph: Action-Reaction Spatio-Temporal Graphs for Soccer Tactical Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25569)
*Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Henry O. Velesaca,Steven Araujo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SambaGraph：基于2022世界杯全部64场比赛构建的动作—反应时空图数据集与基准，用于足球战术反应建模，并系统评估响应分类、防守检索与LLM推理三类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球战术本质是交互式的：进攻动作会改变对手的防守问题，而观察到的防守反应又依赖于多智能体的整体比赛状态。然而现有工作缺乏能够显式刻画“动作—反应”交互关系、且可复现的图结构数据与基准，难以支持战术响应建模的系统研究。

Method: 基于2022 FIFA世界杯全部64场比赛的追踪数据与事件数据，构建SambaGraph数据集：整理出4,070个以动作中心的回合（episode），表示为时间对齐的23节点“球员—球”图序列，并区分进攻/防守视角；包含反应标签以及26,270个防划分泄露（split-safe）的攻防配对。围绕三类问题展开评测：(1) 从图回合中分类观察到的反应；(2) 为查询进攻检索成功防守；(3) 图衍生摘要是否支持有据可依的LLM推理。采用紧凑签名MLP与融合的图—签名双编码器，并对比局部LLM在直接分类与八候选重排序中的表现。

Result: 紧凑签名MLP在响应分类上取得0.796±0.007的macro-F1；融合图—签名双编码器在全库防守检索上达到0.471±0.029的Hit@5与0.655±0.051的Hit@10。实验表明硬负样本能最大化配对区分度但并不提升检索质量；局部LLM在直接分类上弱于监督编码器，在八候选重排序中也不优于强原始顺序，但能提供有据可依的战术理由。

Conclusion: SambaGraph为基于图的足球战术“策略—反应”研究提供了一个可复现的基准，并揭示了监督编码器与LLM在该任务上的能力边界。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Soccer tactics are interactive: an attacking action changes the opponent's defensive problem, and the observed response depends on the multi-agent match state. We introduce SambaGraph, an action--reaction spatio-temporal graph dataset and benchmark for soccer tactical response modeling. From tracking and event data for all 64 matches of the 2022 FIFA World Cup, we curate 4,070 action-centered episodes represented as temporally aligned 23-node player--ball graph sequences with attack/defense views, response labels, and 26,270 split-safe attack--defense pairs. We study three questions: whether observed responses can be classified from graph episodes, whether successful defenses can be retrieved for a query attack, and whether graph-derived summaries support grounded LLM reasoning. A compact signature MLP obtains $0.796\pm0.007$ macro-F1 for response classification, while a fused graph--signature dual encoder reaches $0.471\pm0.029$ Hit@5 and $0.655\pm0.051$ Hit@10 for full-bank defensive retrieval. Hard negatives maximize pair discrimination but not retrieval quality. Local LLMs underperform supervised encoders for direct classification and do not improve over a strong original order in eight-candidate reranking, but they provide grounded tactical rationales. These results position SambaGraph as a reproducible benchmark for graph-based soccer strategy-response research. Code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/areyesan/SambaGraph.

</details>


### [313] [EMGBlend: Heterogeneity-Aware Self-Supervised Pretraining for Gesture and Force Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25582)
*Yuwei Jia,Cheng Zhong,Jinyang Yu,Zhe Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMGBlend：面向异构公共sEMG数据集的自监督预训练框架，通过共享通道patch、几何感知注意力、频带受限谱目标与数据源平衡，在11个公开源上预训练109M参数模型，并在手势识别、连续力回归和接触分类上优于随机初始化与波形重构对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共表面肌电（sEMG）数据集在电极布局、通道数、频率支持范围和规模上差异显著；直接混合预训练会导致通道语义错位、引入部分设备无法观测的谱目标，并让大规模或高通道数数据集主导学习。

Method: 提出EMGBlend自监督框架：结合共享通道patch与几何感知注意力；将谱目标限制在每条记录支持的频带内；跨数据源平衡采样/暴露。基于11个公开EMG源预训练109M参数模型，并迁移至手势识别、连续力回归与接触分类。

Result: EMGBlend持续优于匹配的随机初始化与波形重构对照。固定预算数据源对照表明，多源预训练提升手势识别，并在力解码上保持竞争力。消融显示几何、频带感知目标与数据源平衡均对迁移有贡献；但跨受试者NinaPro力估计仍然困难。

Conclusion: 异构EMG数据集可通过显式机制设计有效结合，而非简单拼接；EMGBlend为多源sEMG预训练提供了可迁移框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Public surface electromyography (EMG) datasets vary widely in electrode layout, channel count, frequency support, and size. Simply mixing them for pretraining can misalign channel semantics, introduce spectral targets that some devices cannot observe, and let large or high-channel-count datasets dominate learning. We introduce EMGBlend, a self-supervised framework designed around these differences. It combines shared channel patches with geometry-aware attention, restricts spectral targets to each recording's supported frequency band, and balances exposure across data sources. We pretrain a 109M-parameter model on 11 public EMG sources and evaluate it on gesture recognition, continuous-force regression, and contact classification. EMGBlend consistently outperforms matched random initialization and waveform reconstruction controls. Fixed-budget source controls show that multi-source pretraining improves gesture recognition and remains competitive for force decoding. Ablations confirm that geometry, band-aware targets, and source balancing each contribute to transfer, although cross-person NinaPro force estimation remains difficult. Overall, EMGBlend shows how heterogeneous EMG datasets can be combined through explicit mechanism design rather than simple concatenation. Code is available at https://github.com/tamanano/EMGBlend

</details>


### [314] [Rewired or Gated? How Instruction Tuning Shapes Knowledge-Conflict Circuits in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25602)
*Shubham Santosh Pandere,Gautam Ranka,Ritika Varshney,Navya Deshmukh,Roushni Sareen,Roshan Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次对基础模型与指令微调模型的冲突消解回路进行机制级对比，发现指令微调并未重建回路，而是对同一批晚期注意力头进行门控/重加权；行为上使模型更倾向参数记忆，对简短反事实注入更怀疑，但这种稳健性受框架影响且范围有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在提示与参数权重冲突时，由少量可识别的注意力头决定“信提示还是信权重”。指令微调会改变冲突下的行为，但其究竟重构了底层回路，还是仅对既有组件进行门控/重加权，尚不清楚。

Method: 首次开展基础模型与指令模型的冲突消解回路机制对比，覆盖Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen-2.5-3B、Gemma-3-4B三个模型家族；综合使用节点与边归因、叠加角色分析、因果消融、路径修补五种独立方法进行交叉验证。

Result: 五种方法一致指向“门控”：相同晚期层中的相同注意力头被重加权而非替换，节点重叠率高达0.60–0.82。行为上，指令微调使模型更偏向参数记忆，指令模型比基础模型更常拒绝简短反事实上下文，与朴素“用户跟随”预期相反；但这种额外怀疑取决于表述框架，当同一错误主张以连贯、带证据的段落呈现时即消失。因此，指令微调对简短注入的稳健性真实但狭窄。

Conclusion: 冲突回路在指令微调后被保留而非重建，说明基于基础模型校准的可解释性与控制工具，可能直接迁移到其部署用的指令模型上。

Abstract: In language models, the choice between believing the prompt and believing the weights is made by a handful of identifiable attention heads. Instruction tuning changes how models behave under conflict, but whether it rewires the underlying circuit or merely gates/reweights already present components, remains unknown. We provide the first mechanistic base-vs-instruct comparison of conflict-resolution circuits, across three families (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen-2.5-3B, Gemma-3-4B). Five independent methods, node and edge attribution, superposition role analysis, causal ablation, and path patching, converge on gating, with the same heads, in the same late-layers, are found to be reweighted rather than replaced with a high node overlap (0.60-0.82). Behaviorally, tuning shifts models toward parametric memory, making instruct models reject a terse counterfactual context far more than base ones, the opposite of a naive user-following expectation. Yet this added skepticism is a factor of framing since it disappears when the same false claim is delivered as a coherent, evidential passage. The robustness that instruction tuning buys against terse injection is therefore real but narrow. More broadly, we believe that because the conflict circuit is preserved rather than rebuilt, interpretability and control tools calibrated on base models should transfer directly to their deployed instruct siblings.

</details>


### [315] [What Should a Self-Teacher See? Privileged Context Design for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25623)
*Kanghui Tian,Siyuan Liu,Tianxiang Jiang,Shuai Dong,Yizhuo Li,Tian Ding,Yuan Guo,Songze Li,Haowen Hou,Congcong Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在 on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）中，特权的教师上下文并非越完整越好；将完整参考解替换为不同抽象层级的离线编译提示，可在竞赛数学上以更少提示 token 取得更优或相当的学生表现，且最优抽象层级随学生规模与任务变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 OPSD 常让冻结的基座模型以完整参考解（最终答案＋一条特定推理路径）作为特权上下文来评价学生自身 rollout，但“更多特权信息”未必构成更好的教师。作者希望检验教师应看到何种抽象层级的信息，才能最有效地引导学生。

Method: 在固定学生视角与训练设置、分别于 4B 与 8B 规模下，将默认的完整参考解与三类离线编译的抽象上下文比较：命名策略、方法无关框架、问题类别；另设仅答案对照（保留目的地、去除路径）。在竞赛数学主实验中比较 in-domain peak mean、提示 token 量、跨三个随机种子的平均增益，并考察初始 teacher-student KL 与下游表现的关系。

Result: 主实验中，最佳中间抽象上下文相对完整解将 in-domain peak mean 提升：4B 提高 1.4 分，8B 提高 1.6 分，同时提示 token 存储量少一个数量级。三个种子下，框架与类别上下文在两个规模均呈正向平均增益。仅答案条件在主实验中仍具竞争力，与完整解差距在 0.2 分以内。偏好上下文随学生规模与任务变化；初始 teacher-student KL 不能预测下游性能排序。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏教师不应向学生展示其“能看到的全部信息”，而应提供学生仍能据以行动的抽象层级；教师上下文的抽象程度需与学生规模及任务匹配，提示效率与效果可同时改善。

Abstract: More privileged information does not always make a better teacher. We study this tension in on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), where a frozen copy of the base model scores the student's own rollouts under privileged context, conventionally a complete reference solution that bundles the final answer with one particular reasoning path. Holding the student view and training fixed within each scale, we compare that default against three abstractions compiled offline, a named strategy, a method-independent framing, and a problem category, and against an answer-only control that keeps the destination but removes the path. In the primary runs on competition mathematics, the best intermediate contexts improve the in-domain peak mean over the full solution by 1.4 points at 4B and 1.6 at 8B, while storing an order of magnitude fewer hint tokens. Comparisons across three seeds also show positive mean gains for the framing and category contexts at both scales. Answer-only conditioning remains competitive in the primary runs, within 0.2 points of the full solution at these scales. The preferred context varies with student scale and task. Initial teacher-student KL does not order downstream performance. What a self-teacher should see is therefore not everything it could, but the level of abstraction its student can still act on.

</details>


### [316] [An Exploratory Replica-Overlap Probe of the Grokking Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25634)
*A. C. Opus,J. Q. Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该预注册研究检验 grokking 转变中 RSB 启发的成对权重重叠分布是否变化；但因对齐步骤不保持网络函数、数值精度失败及协议偏差，注册结局为 UNDETERMINED（C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID），无法给出确证性零结果或有效的 Parisi 序参量读数。事后仅 frac40 通过完整性检查；dip 检验无功效，唯一在观测效应下有功效的统计量是重叠标准差比约 5.6 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 grokking 转变是否伴随 RSB（replica symmetry breaking）式结构变化，并以 Parisi 序参量/成对权重重叠分布作为探针；同时考察预注册仪器与统计判据是否足以给出确证性结论。

Method: 训练 64 个独立种子网络，覆盖 4 种配置，持续至收敛或 40000 epoch 上限；在 grokking 转变前后比较 RSB 启发的成对权重重叠分布。预注册实现使用隐藏单元置换对齐，并区分 q_wt（经置换对齐计算的权重重叠）与 q_fn（由未置换模型预测计算的功能重叠）。事后分析包括 Hartigan dip 检验、重叠标准差比、集成损失平坦性与 grokking 率。

Result: 注册实现仅置换隐藏单元，未同步置换偏置和 head 内部单元，因此不保持网络函数；所有经该对齐得到的 q_wt 均继承缺陷，q_fn 不受影响。数值精度要求未通过，审计发现协议偏差，故预注册规则无裁决：UNDETERMINED（C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID）。仅 frac40 满足 12/16 checkpoint 完整性要求。事后 Hartigan dip 区间含零（Delta dip 95% CI = [-0.017, 0.034]），但事后校准显示 dip 检验在模拟分离下功效为零，故该区间无信息，不等于“无变化”证据；重叠标准差增加约 5.6 倍。集成损失仅在预设 1% 阈值下近似平坦。grokking 率为 0/16、11/16、16/16，但因训练比例与 split identity 混淆，只能作描述性报告。

Conclusion: 这些数据既不是确证性零结果，也不是对 Parisi 序参量的有效读取；无法对 grokking 是否伴随 RSB 变化作出裁决。唯一在观测效应下具有功效的统计量是重叠标准差比，但仍属事后分析。需修正函数保持对齐、数值精度与协议合规性后，重新预注册并验证仪器，方可进行确证性推断。

Abstract: We trained 64 independently seeded networks in four configurations, continuing each to sustained convergence or a 40,000-epoch ceiling. We then asked whether an RSB-inspired distribution of pairwise weight overlaps changes across the grokking transition. It is the alignment step, not the overlap statistic, that determines what this registered probe can report. The registered implementation permutes hidden units without the corresponding bias and head-internal permutations and therefore does not preserve the network function. Every q_wt value computed through this alignment inherits the defect; q_fn does not, because it is computed from predictions of the unpermuted models. The numerical-precision requirement also failed, and an audit found protocol deviations. Consequently, the pre-registered rule gives no verdict: registered outcome UNDETERMINED (reason code C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID). These data provide neither a confirmatory null nor a validated reading of the Parisi order parameter. Only frac40 cleared the 12/16 checkpoint-completeness requirement. For this configuration, a post-hoc criterion applied to the same data gave a Hartigan-dip interval containing zero (95% CI for Delta dip = [-0.017, 0.034]), whereas the overlap standard deviation increased by a factor of about 5.6. A post-hoc calibration assigns the dip test zero power at the simulated separations; the interval is therefore uninformative, not evidence of no change. The standard-deviation ratio is the only statistic here with power at the observed effect. Ensemble loss was near-flat only under the pre-specified 1% threshold. Finally, grokking rates of 0/16, 11/16 and 16/16 remain descriptive because train fraction is confounded with split identity.

</details>


### [317] [Efficient Cost-Aware LLM Evaluation via Bayesian Bandit Gittins Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25645)
*Qian Xie,Yueli He,Nairen Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将LLM配置选择建模为成本感知贝叶斯赌博机问题，提出GittinsEval，用Gittins最优策略决定评估哪个配置及何时停止，并扩展出可随时推荐全评估/部分评估配置的LCB规则。在GSM8K、PIQA、AlpacaEval、MMLU上，它常以1%–2%的穷举成本达到近零简单遗憾，自适应停止通常触发于1%–10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穷举评估每个候选LLM配置在每个基准项上的表现以选出高性能配置成本过高，因此需要一种在有限评估预算下高效选择配置并决定何时停止的方法。

Method: 将配置选择形式化为成本感知贝叶斯赌博机问题；采用贝叶斯最优Gittins策略确定下一个评估的配置；扩展 anytime 推荐规则，对完整与部分评估的配置统一用LCB风格分数刻画后验不确定性；离线预计算后仅需轻量在线更新，计算高效。

Result: 在GSM8K、PIQA、AlpacaEval、MMLU响应矩阵上持续具有竞争力；在大样本基准上优于配置级贝叶斯优化，在大候选任务上优于成本不敏感赌博机基线；常以1%–2%的穷举评估成本达到近零简单遗憾；自适应停止规则通常在1%–10%成本处触发。

Conclusion: GittinsEval能以极低评估成本高效完成LLM配置选择，兼具贝叶斯最优决策、后验不确定性感知推荐与自适应停止能力，显著降低穷举评估开销。

Abstract: Exhaustively evaluating every candidate LLM configuration on every benchmark item to identify a high-performing one is costly. We formulate configuration selection as a cost-aware Bayesian bandit problem and propose GittinsEval, which draws on the Bayesian-optimal Gittins policy to determine which configuration to evaluate next and when to stop. We extend the policy with an anytime recommendation rule over both fully and partially evaluated configurations, using an LCB-style score to account for posterior uncertainty. GittinsEval is computationally efficient, requiring only lightweight online updates after offline precomputation. Across GSM8K, PIQA, AlpacaEval, and MMLU response matrices, GittinsEval is consistently competitive, with particularly strong gains over configuration-level Bayesian optimization on large-example benchmarks and over cost-unaware bandit baselines on large-candidate tasks. Crucially, GittinsEval often attains near-zero simple regret using only 1% to 2% of the exhaustive-evaluation cost; it also offers an adaptive stopping rule that typically triggers at 1% to 10%.

</details>


### [318] [From Experts to Sub-experts: Fine-grained Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25655)
*Zhentao Tan,Chang Liu,Yao Liu,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出NSFT（Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning），一种面向MoE大语言模型的细粒度参数高效微调框架，将适配粒度从"专家级"细化到"子专家级"，通过结构化通道分组选择任务相关子专家并优化稀疏更新，在多个任务上以更少可训练参数超越代表性PEFT与专家级稀疏微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模扩大，稠密全参数适配成本高昂，催生了MoE等稀疏模块化架构。这引出PEFT的关键问题：应以何种粒度选择与更新参数？现有方法（如LoRA）在预定义权重矩阵上操作，专家级稀疏微调则更新整个被选专家。然而作者观察到被激活的专家内部本身是稀疏的——仅有少部分中间通道对下游任务强烈响应，说明专家级适配粒度仍然过粗，因此需要更精细的适配粒度。

Method: 提出NSFT框架，将MoE适配从专家级细化到子专家级。具体地：沿中间维度将每个专家分解为结构化通道组（structured channel groups），结合路由重要性（routing importance）与专家内激活显著性（intra-expert activation saliency）来选择任务相关的子专家。为优化稀疏部分更新，NSFT引入学习率缩放（learning-rate scaling）与动态梯度缩放（dynamic gradient scaling），以补偿有效更新幅度的减小。

Result: 在OLMoE与Ling-mini-2.0两个模型上，针对具有挑战性的领域特定任务与通用基准进行实验。结果表明，NSFT持续优于代表性PEFT方法与专家级稀疏微调基线，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数，并保持有竞争力的通用能力。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，子专家级适配是面向MoE LLM的一种更精确、更高效的PEFT范式。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) scale rapidly, dense full-parameter adaptation becomes increasingly expensive, motivating sparse and modular architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This shift raises a key question for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT): at what granularity should parameters be selected and updated? Existing PEFT methods such as LoRA operate on predefined weight matrices, while expert-level sparse tuning methods update entire selected experts. However, we observe that activated experts are internally sparse, with only a small fraction of intermediate channels strongly responding to downstream tasks, indicating that expert-level adaptation is still too coarse. We propose NSFT (Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning), a fine-grained PEFT framework that refines MoE adaptation from experts to sub-experts. NSFT decomposes each expert along the intermediate dimension into structured channel groups and selects task-relevant sub-experts by combining routing importance with intra-expert activation saliency. To optimize sparse partial updates, NSFT further introduces learning-rate scaling and dynamic gradient scaling to compensate for the reduced effective update magnitude. Experiments on OLMoE and Ling-mini-2.0 across challenging domain-specific tasks and general benchmarks show that NSFT consistently outperforms representative PEFT and expert-level sparse tuning baselines, while using substantially fewer trainable parameters and preserving competitive general capability. These results suggest that sub-expert-level adaptation is a more precise and efficient PEFT paradigm for MoE LLMs.

</details>


### [319] [Targeted Review for AI-Assisted Biodiversity Surveys: Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25657)
*Timm Haucke,Lauren Harrell,Justin Kay,Mary Clapp,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ACORN 方法，将机器学习预测整合进占域模型，并主动挑选对下游生态推断最有价值的样本供专家审核，从而在有限审核预算下接近全人工标注的生态结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据集越来越多地使用机器学习进行标注，但模型难以做到完美，系统性偏差会传播到科学认知中。因此科学家通常需要审核大量 ML 生成的标签以纠错、避免研究结论被 ML 偏差影响。然而专家审核预算有限，如何将审核精力最优地分配到符合科研目标的样本上，是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出 Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling（ACORN）。该方法将 ML 的连续分数预测纳入占域模型（occupancy modeling，即在环境因子条件下估计物种出现概率），并策略性地选择对下游生态分析信息量最大的样本进行专家审核。研究聚焦生态学家这一特定群体，在相机陷阱（camera-trap）与生物声学（bioacoustic）数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在相机陷阱和生物声学数据集上，ACORN 恢复的生态学结论接近使用全人工标注数据所得结论，同时所需专家审核量显著少于非定向（non-targeted）的审核策略。

Conclusion: ML 辅助的科学工作流应针对下游推断来优化专家投入，而非仅针对分类器准确率，尤其是在人工审核预算有限的情况下。代码已开源。

Abstract: We increasingly use machine learning to label scientific datasets. The models we develop and deploy are improving all the time, but they are not and will likely never be perfect. Mistakes matter, as errors can propagate into our scientific understanding, particularly when systematically biased. Very reasonably, scientists thus review substantial proportions of ML-generated labels to verify or correct mistakes in pursuit of ensuring their scientific findings are not biased by ML. In this work, we focus on helping scientists optimally allocate this reviewing effort relative to their scientific goals. We focus on a specific class of scientists (ecologists) and a specific, widespread, and impactful modeling target (occupancy modeling, which estimates where species are likely to occur, conditioned on environmental factors). We introduce Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling (ACORN), a method that incorporates ML predictions into occupancy models and strategically selects samples for expert review that are maximally informative for downstream ecological analysis. Across camera-trap and bioacoustic datasets, our method recovers ecological conclusions close to those obtained from fully human-labeled data, while requiring substantially fewer expert reviews than non-targeted review policies. Our results suggest that ML-assisted scientific workflows should optimize expert effort for downstream inference, rather than for classifier accuracy alone, especially when human review budget is limited. Our code is available at https://github.com/timmh/acorn

</details>


### [320] [When Riemann flows with Wasserstein: Generative Modeling of Probability Distributions on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25659)
*Doron Haviv,Edward De Brouwer,Rishabh Anand,Rex Ying,Aïcha Bentaieb,Gabriele Scalia,Hector Corrada Bravo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RWEFM 是一种在黎曼流形 $(\mathcal{M},g)$ 的 Wasserstein 空间 $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ 上的生成框架。它通过将神经向量场回归到黎曼最优传输速度，并利用 McCann 位移插值作为条件路径来训练，从而在尊重数据内在几何结构的前提下生成元分布（meta-distributions），并在单细胞、蛋白质构象及一般三角网格等任务上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学数据集（如分子构象系综、单细胞组织测量）天然地应建模为"元分布"——非欧几里得域上概率测度的分布。然而现有生成方法大多假设欧几里得几何，无法刻画这种结构，导致无法捕捉数据的内在几何特征。

Method: 提出 Riemannian Wasserstein Entropic Flow Matching（RWEFM）：在黎曼流形 $(\mathcal{M},g)$ 的 Wasserstein 空间 $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ 上构建生成框架。训练时将神经向量场回归到黎曼最优传输速度，并以 McCann 位移插值作为条件路径；同时提出 Riemannian Entropic Map 作为流形上最优传输映射的 GPU 高效近似。整个方法仅依赖测地距离与投影算子。

Result: 理论上证明该构造构成 $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ 上有效的流匹配方法。实验中，通过尊重数据内在几何，RWEFM 能在超球潜空间中生成完整单细胞样本、在环面上生成蛋白质构象系综，并在一般三角网格上生成分布，验证了方法不局限于具有闭式几何的流形。

Conclusion: RWEFM 将流匹配推广至黎曼流形上的 Wasserstein 空间，为建模非欧几里得域上的元分布提供了理论与实用兼具的生成框架；因其仅需测地距离与投影算子，具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: Many scientific datasets, such as molecular conformational ensembles or single-cell tissue measurements, are naturally modeled as meta-distributions: distributions over probability measures on non-Euclidean domains. Existing generative methods largely assume Euclidean geometry and fail to capture this structure. We introduce Riemannian Wasserstein Entropic Flow Matching (RWEFM), a generative framework on the Wasserstein space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ of a Riemannian manifold $(\mathcal{M},g)$. RWEFM is trained by regressing a neural vector field onto Riemannian optimal transport velocities, using McCann displacement interpolations as conditional paths. We confirm theoretically that this construction leads to a valid flow matching approach on $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ and introduce the Riemannian Entropic Map, a GPU-efficient approximation of the optimal transport map on manifolds. Our experiments show that by respecting the intrinsic geometry of the data, RWEFM can generate whole single-cell samples in hyperspherical latent spaces and protein conformational ensembles on the torus. As RWEFM requires only a geodesic distance and a projection operator, it is not restricted to manifolds with closed-form geometry, which we demonstrate by generating distributions on a general triangulated mesh.

</details>


### [321] [Marginal Log-Likelihood Increments under Dirichlet-Smoothed Markov Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25675)
*Levin David Schwab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Dirichlet 平滑的转移模型，本文推导出向训练集加入单条工作流轨迹对参考加权对数似然的精确变化，证明其为参考条件分布与模型之间 KL 散度的加权缩减，并由此得到采集增益上界、参考加权的协方差恒等式与候选交互的符号判据；在 BPI Challenge 2012 贷款申请日志上的案例研究表明，批次目标的次模/超模性质不成立，且对单条轨迹的排序精度对批次质量既不必要也不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动学习/批量采集场景中，通常以模型似然增益来衡量新增样本的价值，但对 Dirichlet 平滑转移模型而言，单条轨迹带来的似然变化缺乏精确且可解释的解析形式，导致采集增益的上界、参考加权的作用机制以及候选样本间交互性质（是否次模/超模）都不清晰，从而难以判断基于单条样本排序精度进行批次选择的合理性。

Method: 对 Dirichlet 平滑转移模型，解析推导加入单条工作流轨迹引起的参考加权对数似然精确变化，并将其改写为参考条件分布与模型之间 KL 散度的加权缩减；基于该形式导出任意采集策略的增益上界，将毫纳（millinat）级差异表示为可达增益的占比，给出参考加权效应的精确协方差恒等式，以及两候选样本交互项的符号判据。随后在 BPI Challenge 2012 贷款申请日志上进行案例研究，在参考加权与预算单位的四种组合下度量上述三项量，并对比两个使用相同描述符与标签、但对个体增量预测精度不同的回归器（中位 R² 分别为 0.87 与 0.62）所实现的可达增益占比。

Result: 理论上得到三项结果：采集增益的上界（以可达增益占比表示的毫纳级差异）、参考加权效应的精确协方差恒等式、以及候选交互的符号判据，由此证明批次目标既非次模也非超模。案例研究中，仅在参考加权与预算单位的四种组合之一出现正向选择结果；两个回归器中预测个体增量更准确者（中位 R² 0.87 对 0.62）反而实现较小的可达增益占比（61% 对 69%）。

Conclusion: 单条轨迹的排序精度对批次采集质量既不必要也不充分；批次目标的非次模/非超模性质与参考加权、预算单位的选择共同影响采集效果，因此不能简单以个体增量预测精度替代批次层面的增益评估。

Abstract: For a Dirichlet-smoothed transition model, the effect of adding one workflow trace to the training archive is an exact change in reference-weighted log likelihood. We derive that change and show that it is a weighted reduction of Kullback--Leibler divergence between the reference conditionals and the model. From this form we obtain an upper bound on the gain available to any acquisition, which expresses a millinat difference as a share of what is attainable, an exact covariance identity for the effect of the reference weighting, and a sign criterion for the interaction between two candidates, from which the batch objective is neither submodular nor supermodular. A case study on the BPI Challenge 2012 loan-application log measures all three and finds a positive selection result in one of the four combinations of reference weighting and budget unit. There, of two regressors fitted to identical descriptors and identical labels, the one that predicts individual increments far more accurately, median $R^2$ 0.87 against 0.62, realizes the smaller share of the attainable gain, 61 against 69 per cent, so ranking accuracy for individual traces is neither necessary nor sufficient for batch quality.

</details>


### [322] [Graph Domain Adaptation Does Not End with Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25692)
*Ziqian Liu,Yongxue Xu,Enze Zhang,Jiaqi Zhang,Hao Wang,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EviGDA，一种证据增强的图域自适应框架，在图表示自适应之外引入一个无图局部专家，两个专家独立优化、仅在推理时以固定概率混合，在10个数据集、16个迁移任务上超越现有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图域自适应（GDA）方法主要通过传播重设计、分布对齐或源到目标迁移建模来适应图表示，但目标域预测仍依赖单一图传播路径。由于图感知专家与无图局部专家在拓扑偏移下可能呈现不同的失效模式，单一图表示是否穷尽了目标域中可用的预测证据仍存疑。

Method: EviGDA 在图表示自适应基础上补充一个无图局部专家：图感知专家执行消息传递与熵感知边缘对齐；无图局部专家仅从源节点特征和标签学习，不进行图传播也不做目标对齐。两个专家独立优化，仅在推理阶段通过任务级固定概率混合（constant probability mixture）进行组合，从而在无需联合训练、学习式路由或目标伪标签的情况下保留互补证据。

Result: 在10个数据集和16个迁移任务上的大量实验表明，EviGDA 优于当前最先进（state-of-the-art）的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将图感知专家与无图局部专家以固定概率混合的方式互补，EviGDA 有效利用了两类专家在不同拓扑条件下的互补预测证据，为缓解单一图传播路径的证据局限提供了一种简洁且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph domain adaptation (GDA) transfers knowledge from a labeled source graph to an unlabeled target graph under shifts in both node attributes and graph structure. Existing methods primarily adapt graph representations through propagation redesign, distribution alignment, or source-to-target transition modeling, but still rely on a single graph-propagating path for target prediction. This leaves open whether an adapted graph representation exhausts the predictive evidence available in the target domain, since the graph-aware expert and graph-free local expert may exhibit different failure modes under topological shifts. To address this limitation, we propose EviGDA, an Evidence-Augmented Graph Domain Adaptation framework that complements graph representation adaptation with a graph-free local expert. The graph-aware expert performs message passing and entropy-aware marginal alignment, while the graph-free local expert learns solely from source node features and labels without graph propagation or target alignment. The two experts are optimized independently and combined only at inference through a task-level constant probability mixture, preserving complementary evidence without joint training, learned routing, or target pseudo-labels. Extensive experiments on ten datasets and 16 transfer tasks show that EviGDA outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [323] [Fully Byzantine-Resilient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25701)
*Haejoon Lee,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究分布式拜占庭容错Actor-Critic多智能体强化学习，提出FRAC-MARL方法，利用两跳消息冗余识别可靠消息，在拜占庭边攻击下证明参数几乎必然收敛到无攻击情形相同的极限点，并给出拓扑条件与多项式时间验证方法，在合作多机器人编队控制任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式Actor-Critic多智能体强化学习方法在拜占庭攻击下只能保证参数收敛到无攻击极限点附近的邻域，导致性能下降；需要实现完全容错，使学习结果不受通信层攻击影响。

Method: 提出FRAC-MARL去中心化方法：每个智能体利用两跳消息中的冗余信息识别可靠消息。在价值和团队奖励函数线性参数化、拜占庭边攻击且攻击局限于通信层的设定下，分析时变通信图上的收敛性；引入新的拓扑条件以保证收敛，给出构造满足该条件网络的系统方法，并证明该条件可在多项式时间内验证。

Result: 在拜占庭边攻击下，证明智能体参数几乎必然收敛到与无攻击情形相同的极限点，而非仅收敛到其邻域；给出可构造且可多项式时间验证的拓扑条件；在合作多机器人编队控制任务上验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: FRAC-MARL通过利用两跳消息冗余，在通信层拜占庭攻击下实现与无攻击情形一致的收敛性能，为分布式容错Actor-Critic多智能体强化学习提供了理论保证与可验证的网络设计条件。

Abstract: We study distributed Byzantine-resilient actor-critic multi-agent reinforcement learning (AC-MARL), where agents collectively learn policies through local interactions. Existing methods guarantee convergence of the agents' parameters only to a neighborhood of the attack-free limit points, resulting in degraded performance. We propose Fully Resilient AC-MARL (FRAC-MARL), a decentralized method in which each agent leverages redundancy in two-hop messages to identify reliable messages. Under linear parameterizations of the value and team-reward functions and Byzantine edge attacks, where adversarial behavior is confined to the communication layer, we prove that agents' parameters converge almost surely to the same limit points as in the attack-free case over time-varying communication graphs. We introduce a novel topological condition for the convergence of our method, present a systematic method to construct such networks, and prove that this condition can be verified in polynomial time. Finally, we demonstrate our method on cooperative multi-robot formation control tasks.

</details>


### [324] [Slow Decay and Silenced Expression: Iterated Subliminal Trait Transfer in Language-Model Lineages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25721)
*Ryan Vo,Duc-Vu Nguyen,Matt Kretchmar,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究“阈下学习”中的特征能否跨模型谱系多代传递：将同一特征注入三个 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 副本并迭代训练至第 10 代，发现特征在三个谱系中持续存在，但行为表达逐步衰减；同时特征可“内部存在而行为不显”，且用第 10 代学生的激活位移对基座进行 steering 可诱导出被筛选到的行为表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地使用其他模型的输出进行训练，形成“谱系”（lineages），其中某一代模型携带的特征可能传递给下一代。已有“阈下学习”（subliminal learning）工作表明，教师模型的特征可通过不含该特征内容的过滤数据传递给学生，但证据仅覆盖单次训练步骤，尚不清楚这种特征在跨代谱系中会保持还是衰减。

Method: 将目标特征注入三个 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 副本，并从每个副本出发将训练步骤迭代至深度 10 代。对每一代模型，在同一组 held-out prompts 上用两种方式读取特征：一是关键词筛选（keyword screen），检测模型输出中是否出现该特征的表达；二是激活探针（activation probe），将每个模型相对基座的位移投影到由其他谱系教师构建的方向上。此外还进行两项评估：移除默认 system prompt 后重新测量第 10 代学生；以及用第 10 代学生在默认 system prompt 下测得的位移，对未处理的基座模型进行 steering。

Result: 第一，特征在三个谱系、十代传递中持续存在，但行为表达下降：被注入的模型在每次 completion 上都表达该特征，关键词筛选率在第一步后降至 55.6%，到第 10 代降至 21.1%；基座模型在 300 个 completion 中筛选匹配为 0。第二，特征可以内部存在而行为缺失：在评估时移除默认 system prompt 后，第 10 代学生在每个 prompt 上的关键词筛选率均为 0，而激活探针分数在每个 prompt 上仍为正。进一步，用第 10 代学生（在默认 system prompt 下训练和测量）的位移去 steering 未处理的基座，即使在移除 system prompt 的情况下也能诱导出可被关键词筛选到的特征表达；而同一学生在移除 system prompt 时并无该特征表达。

Conclusion: 阈下特征并非只在单步训练中有效，而可在模型谱系中跨十代持续存在，但其可观察行为表达会随代际衰减，并受 system prompt 等评估条件显著影响。激活探针能够揭示行为上不可见的内部痕迹，且该内部位移具有因果作用，可对基座模型 steering 并诱导出行为表达。因此，评估模型谱系中的阈下传递时，需要区分内部表征与行为读出，并考虑提示条件对特征显隐的影响。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly trained on the outputs of other models, forming chains that we call lineages, in which a trait present in one generation can pass to the next. Prior work on subliminal learning has shown that a teacher's trait can transmit to a student through filtered data carrying none of the trait's content. However, the evidence covers only a single training step. We study whether such a trait holds or fades across lineages. We instill the trait into three copies of Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and iterate the training step to depth ten from each, reading every generation two ways on the same held-out prompts: a keyword screen that looks for expressions of the trait in the model's output, and an activation probe that projects each model's displacement from the base onto a direction built from the other lineages' teachers. We report two findings. First, the trait persists through ten generations across three lineages. The instilled models express it on every completion; the keyword-screen rate falls to 55.6% after the first step and to 21.1% by generation ten. The base itself matches the screen on none of its 300 completions. Second, the trait can be present internally while absent behaviorally. When the model's default system prompt is removed at evaluation, the generation-ten students' keyword-screen rate is zero on every prompt while the probe score stays positive on every prompt. Steering the untreated base with the displacement of a generation-ten student, which is trained and measured under the default system prompt, induces screened expression of the trait even with the system prompt removed, while that same student shows no expression of the trait with the system prompt removed.

</details>


### [325] [Signed Graph Pre-Training and Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25722)
*Zihan Mei,Rong Pan,Yuzhou Chen,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TopoSIGN——首个面向符号图的拓扑引导图预训练与提示学习框架，结合磁符号拉普拉斯结构编码器与基于Dowker复形持续同调的拓扑分支，实现符号图上的迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号图广泛存在于信任-不信任网络、金融相关系统、生物交互图等领域，其边可正可负且可有向。现有符号图神经网络虽提升了特定任务的学习效果，但符号图上的图迁移学习仍发展不足，缺乏通用的预训练与提示学习框架。

Method: TopoSIGN由三部分构成：(1) 基于磁符号拉普拉斯（magnetic signed Laplacian）的结构编码器；(2) 新颖的持续同调（persistent homology）分支，通过Dowker复形的持续图像（Dowker-complex persistence images）刻画符号拓扑；(3) 将融合后的嵌入输入到提示学习（prompt learning）函数中，从而实现跨任务的迁移。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实世界数据集上的实验表明，TopoSIGN能够有效提取符号图中的有用结构信息，并验证了所提出通用框架的可适配性与灵活性。

Conclusion: TopoSIGN作为面向符号图的先驱性拓扑引导预训练与提示学习框架，弥合了符号图表示学习中迁移能力的空白，展示了拓扑信息融合在符号图通用建模上的潜力。

Abstract: Signed graphs arise in trust--distrust networks, financial correlation systems, biological interaction graphs, and many other domains in which edges can be positive or negative and may also be directed. While signed graph neural networks have improved task-specific learning, graph transfer learning on signed graphs remains underdeveloped. In this paper, we introduce TopoSIGN, a pioneer topology-guided graph pre-training and prompt learning framework for signed graphs. TopoSIGN combines a structural encoder built on the magnetic signed Laplacian with a novel persistent-homology branch that summarizes signed topology through Dowker-complex persistence images. The fused embeddings are then transferred to a prompt learning function. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the efficacy of TopoSIGN in extracting useful structural information in signed graphs, as well as the adaptability and flexibility of the proposed general framework.

</details>


### [326] [Self-Supervised Combinatorial Optimization with Constraints via Frank-Wolfe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25728)
*Akbar Rafiey,Yifei Xu,Nikolaos Karalias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通用自监督学习框架，用于求解组合优化问题：允许神经网络输出任意连续向量，再通过基于 Frank-Wolfe 与近似 Carathéodory 的几何分解，将其近似为可行解的稀疏凸组合，从而得到几乎处处可微的自监督损失，并在推理时自动获得舍入保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化具有离散、硬约束特性，而神经网络训练依赖连续梯度优化，二者存在核心矛盾。现有将组合目标连续松弛到凸域的方法，通常需要投影步骤以强制网络输出位于可行多面体内，且依赖针对具体问题的临时构造，缺乏通用性。

Method: 作者不再约束网络输出位于可行多面体内，而是允许其预测任意连续向量。随后使用基于 Frank-Wolfe 方法的几何分解算法，结合近似 Carathéodory 结果，将这些可能位于可行域外的预测近似表示为可行解的稀疏凸组合。该分解诱导出一个几乎处处可微的自监督损失，其定义为离散目标的期望值；同一过程在推理阶段还可提供自动舍入保证。

Result: 在多个组合优化任务上取得较强的实证性能，包括二次分配问题（Quadratic Assignment Problem）、最大覆盖问题（Maximum Coverage）和旅行商问题（Traveling Salesperson Problem）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种更通用的连续松弛与自监督训练范式，避免了对可行域投影和问题特定构造的依赖，同时统一了训练损失构造与推理舍入，为神经网络求解硬约束组合优化问题提供了可扩展框架。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning for combinatorial optimization has emerged as a promising paradigm for solving discrete optimization problems with neural networks, but a central challenge remains: handling hard combinatorial constraints within continuous, gradient-based training. Continuously extending combinatorial objectives to convex domains is a powerful technique, yet existing approaches often require projection steps that constrain neural network outputs to lie inside the feasible polytope and rely on ad-hoc and problem-specific constructions. We propose a general framework in which the neural network is allowed to predict arbitrary continuous vectors that could potentially lie outside of the feasible polytope. These predictions are then approximated by sparse convex combinations of feasible solutions using a geometric decomposition algorithm based on Frank--Wolfe methods and approximate Caratheodory results. This decomposition induces an a.e.-differentiable, self-supervised loss defined as the expected value of the discrete objective. The same procedure provides an automatic rounding guarantee at inference time. We demonstrate strong empirical performance across multiple combinatorial problems, including the Quadratic Assignment Problem, Maximum Coverage, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem.

</details>


### [327] [Beyond Class Marginals: Bounding Rehearsal Gaps without Freezing Class Co-occurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25735)
*Congren Dai,Nat Roongjirarat,Fei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将类平衡回放中“同一类相邻两次回放出现的时间间隔”（rehearsal gap）从类频率与类共现中解耦，提出 randomised-pass replay (RPR)：每轮 shuffled pass 访问每个常驻类一次。在 C 个常驻类、replay batch size b≤C 时，RPR 保持时间平均类边际平衡，并将 gap 上界限制为 2·ceil(C/b)−1，且不依赖未来类信息、不增加回放样本或前向传播。ER-ACE 实验中最终平均准确率相对独立类平衡检索提升 0.72–1.67 个百分点，但效果依赖学习器与运行区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类平衡回放通常只控制类的出现频率（class marginal），但不决定同一类连续两次被回放之间的时间间隔。该 rehearsal gap 可能独立影响遗忘与分类器偏置，因此需要将其与类频率、类共现分开研究。

Method: 提出 randomised-pass replay (RPR)：对固定 C 个常驻类，在每个 shuffled pass 中每个常驻类访问一次；当 replay batch size b≤C 时，保持平衡的时间平均类边际，并给出 gap 上界 2·ceil(C/b)−1，以及常驻类集合变化时的 churn-conditional bound。调度器不使用未来类信息，也不增加回放样本或前向传播。另用线性头 ER-ACE 诊断分析 incoming batch 与 replay batch 联合缺失时的单侧 classifier-bias gradient，并通过移除 incoming-loss mask 做机制消融。

Result: 线性头 ER-ACE 诊断中，更长的缺席 episode 与更大的负向 bias displacement 相关；移除 incoming-loss mask 会削弱调度效应。主 ER-ACE 实验中，在 reservoir storage 下，RPR 相比 independent class-balanced retrieval 将最终平均准确率提高 0.72–1.67 个百分点；balanced storage 下也有正向效果。预训练 ViT 在测试的 LT10 流、小 replay batch 下表现正向，但匹配的更大 batch 对照无实质效果。固定周期与 reused-pass 对照会同时改变多个时间统计量，因此实验未能将 rehearsal-gap 长度与其他时间依赖形式完全隔离。

Conclusion: RPR 将 rehearsal gap 作为独立于类边际与类共现的调度维度，并在部分持续学习设置中带来稳定但有限的准确率增益。其收益取决于学习器与运行区间；现有对照不能完全证明所有效果仅由 rehearsal-gap 长度导致，因此结论应限定为特定条件下的经验性改进。

Abstract: Class-balanced replay controls class frequency but does not determine the interval between successive replay appearances of a class. We study this interval, the rehearsal gap, separately from the class marginal and class co-occurrence, and introduce randomised-pass replay (RPR), which visits each resident class once per shuffled pass. For a fixed set of C resident classes and replay batch size b less than or equal to C, RPR preserves the balanced time-averaged class marginal and bounds every gap by 2*ceil(C/b)-1; a churn-conditional bound applies while the resident set changes. The scheduler uses no future class information and adds no replay examples or forward passes. In a linear-head ER-ACE diagnostic, joint absence from the incoming and replay batches produces a one-sided classifier-bias gradient. Longer absence episodes are associated with larger negative bias displacement, and removing the incoming-loss mask attenuates the scheduling effect. In the primary ER-ACE experiments, RPR improves final average accuracy by 0.72-1.67 percentage points relative to independent class-balanced retrieval under reservoir storage, with positive effects also observed under balanced storage. Pretrained ViTs show positive effects on the tested LT10 streams with small replay batches, while matched larger-batch controls show no material effect. Fixed-cycle and reused-pass controls change more than one temporal statistic, so the experiments do not isolate rehearsal-gap length from all other forms of temporal dependence. The accuracy effects depend on the learner and operating regime.

</details>


### [328] [Modular Norm RandOpt: Population-Efficient Ensembling through Architecture-Aware Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25745)
*Kirato Yoshihara,Hiroaki Hamade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Modular Norm RandOpt：用模块级自然范数与校准尺度替代 RandOpt 的全局扰动尺度，在不改变候选选择与多数投票的前提下，以更少候选数和墙钟时间取得更高平均准确率，表明扰动几何是预训练模型周围无梯度搜索的关键设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RandOpt 通过扰动语言模型权重并集成高排名候选进行多数投票，但其全局扰动尺度忽视了不同模块几何结构的异质性，导致候选效率与搜索效率受限。

Method: 提出 Modular Norm RandOpt，一种架构感知采样方法：采用模块级自然范数与校准尺度进行权重扰动，同时保留 RandOpt 的候选选择与多数投票机制。

Result: 在 Countdown 上以少 3 倍候选、GSM8K 上少至少 12 倍候选超过 RandOpt，并节省墙钟时间；在 7 个任务与 3 个 Qwen 规模（0.5B–3B）上，Countdown、GSM8K、MATH-500 各规模平均准确率均高于 RandOpt；增益扩展到 Llama 3.2 3B 与 Gemma 3 4B 的 Countdown 和 GSM8K；Qwen2.5-1.5B 上在相当主运行评估预算下平均准确率高于迭代基线；GSM8K 的尾部密度诊断显示候选缩减仅约 1.2–1.8 倍，而多数集成提升与更有利的 correct-expert support 相关。

Conclusion: 扰动几何是围绕预训练模型进行群体高效、无梯度搜索的关键设计选择；模块级范数与校准尺度能显著提升候选效率与集成性能。

Abstract: RandOpt samples weight-perturbed language models and ensembles top-ranked candidates through plurality voting, but its global perturbation scale ignores heterogeneous module geometry. We propose \mbox{\textbf{\emph{Modular Norm RandOpt}}}, an architecture-aware sampling method using module-wise natural norms and calibrated scales while preserving selection and voting. It outperforms RandOpt using $3\times$ fewer candidates on Countdown and at least $12\times$ fewer on GSM8K, with corresponding wall-clock savings. Evaluations across seven tasks and three Qwen scales ($0.5$B--$3$B) show higher mean accuracy than RandOpt on Countdown, GSM8K, and MATH-500 at every scale. The gains extend to Llama 3.2 $3$B and Gemma 3 $4$B on Countdown and GSM8K. On Qwen2.5-1.5B, our ensembles also achieve higher mean accuracy than iterative baselines on both tasks at comparable main-run evaluation budgets. On GSM8K, a tail-density diagnostic implies only a $1.2$--$1.8\times$ candidate reduction, while most ensemble improvement is associated with more favorable correct-expert support. These results highlight perturbation geometry as a key design choice for population-efficient, gradient-free search around pretrained models.

</details>


### [329] [Minimal Recurrent Behavioral Memory for Imitation under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25757)
*Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Rui Min,Ruitong Tian,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究部分可观测下复现专家所需的最小循环记忆：以行为商的条件熵给出瞬时需求，并用兼容关系刻画循环记忆的最小性；提出单载体测量协议分离行为充分性、超额码率与信息路径，实验表明学习该表征仍困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，复现专家行为需要多少循环记忆？瞬时需求可由专家行为商的条件熵确定，但循环记忆还必须保留未来观测在投入使用前无法恢复的区分，因此需要精确刻画最小循环行为记忆及其可测量性。

Method: 将最小循环行为记忆刻画为兼容关系：在传递情形下，其等价类达到精确最小值；一般情形转化为闭兼容状态分配上的熵最小化，并给出有限实例的精确证书。提出单载体测量协议，分离行为充分性、超额码率以及由观测或其他记忆路径携带的信息；实验中的比特需求指在给定占用下诱导的符号行为模型。

Result: 在操作任务中，隐藏模式增至512时，学习码率仍接近0比特与2比特需求；等待期瞬时需求为零，但预期记忆呈2→1→0需求。学习该表征困难：事件无关的未来行为监督在单一冻结配置下获得36/40充分种子，并将最长视界像素设置从0/8提升至6/8充分留出种子，闭环成功率从0.08升至0.57。在未修改社区基准上，协议认证延迟无关需求，充分码在中延迟匹配。监督有助于承诺，但会诱导预测冗余；退火后模仿与码率训练可减少该冗余。

Conclusion: 该工作将信息论目标与可学习性分离：最小循环记忆可由兼容关系及闭兼容状态分配上的熵最小化精确刻画，但学习该表征仍具挑战；监督设计需在促进承诺与避免预测冗余之间权衡。

Abstract: What is the least recurrent memory needed to reproduce a specified expert under partial observability? The instantaneous requirement is the conditional entropy of the expert's behavioral quotient, but recurrence must also preserve distinctions that future observations will not restore before use. We characterize this minimal recurrent behavioral memory by a compatibility relation: under transitivity its classes attain the exact minimum, while the general case is an entropy minimization over closed compatible state assignments, with exact certificates on finite instances. A sole-carrier measurement protocol separates behavioral sufficiency, excess code rate, and information carried by observations or other memory paths; experimental bit requirements refer to the induced symbolic behavioral model under the stated occupancy. Across manipulation tasks, learned code rates remain near zero- and two-bit requirements as hidden modes grow to $512$, and anticipatory memory follows a $2\to1\to0$ requirement despite zero instantaneous demand during waiting. Learning this representation remains difficult: event-agnostic future-behavior supervision yields $36/40$ sufficient seeds with one frozen configuration and improves the longest-horizon pixel setting from $0/8$ to $6/8$ sufficient held-out seeds (closed-loop success from $0.08$ to $0.57$). On unmodified community benchmarks, the protocol certifies delay-independent requirements, which sufficient codes match at mid-delay. The supervision aids commitment but can induce predictive surplus; annealing it lets imitation and rate training reduce that surplus, separating the information-theoretic target from the ability to learn it.

</details>


### [330] [Disentangling Heterogeneous Traffic Dynamics for Multi-Step Traffic Forecasting via Adaptive Spectral Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25777)
*Zijun Huang,Chenrui Fu,Wenhao Wang,Xiaochuan Gou,Chih-Chieh Hung,Guanyao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ADNet，通过可学习互补频谱分解将交通流自适应拆分为主导与残差成分，并用双分支时空模型分别预测后融合，在 TraffiDent 数据集多步预测中取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步交通预测困难在于交通信号包含异质时间动态：既有持续稳定的模式，也有快速变化的波动。现有方法通常用统一表示建模，或依赖预定义分解规则，难以灵活分离不同可预测性的成分，从而限制预测能力。

Method: 提出自适应分解网络 ADNet，一种组件特定（component-specific）的预测框架。核心是可学习互补频谱分解机制：对每个频率 bin 学习其分配到主导成分与残差成分的比例，而非硬性频率划分；每个频率 bin 可以不同比例同时贡献于两个成分，并与预测目标联合优化。分解重构后的两个成分分别输入两个专用的时空预测分支，最后融合各分支预测得到多步预测结果。

Result: 在 TraffiDent 数据集的 Alameda 与 Orange 区域上，ADNet 在 24 项“区域-预测步长-指标”对比中取得 20 项最优，尤其在较长预测步长上提升更明显。容量控制消融实验表明，可学习分解显著优于固定分解，并且在双分支结构之外仍带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 自适应分解与组件特定建模对多步交通预测有效，验证了将异质时间动态按可学习比例拆分并分别建模、再融合的框架优势。

Abstract: Accurate multi-step traffic forecasting remains challenging because observed traffic signals contain heterogeneous temporal dynamics with different characteristics and levels of predictability. Existing approaches typically model these dynamics within a unified representation or rely on predefined decomposition rules, which may limit their ability to flexibly separate persistent patterns from rapidly varying fluctuations. To address this issue, we propose the Adaptive Decomposition Network (ADNet), a component-specific forecasting framework that adaptively disentangles traffic dynamics into dominant and residual components. ADNet introduces a learnable complementary spectral decomposition mechanism that determines the contribution of each frequency bin to the two components. Unlike hard frequency partitioning, every frequency bin can contribute to both components with different learned proportions, allowing the decomposition to be optimized jointly with the forecasting objective. The reconstructed components are then modeled by two dedicated spatiotemporal forecasting branches, and their predictions are integrated to generate the final multi-step forecast. Experiments on the Alameda and Orange regions of the TraffiDent dataset show that ADNet achieves the best performance in 20 of the 24 reported region-horizon-metric comparisons, with particularly clear gains at longer forecasting horizons. Capacity-controlled ablation experiments further show that the learnable decomposition substantially outperforms a fixed decomposition and provides additional improvements beyond the dual-branch architecture alone. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive decomposition and component-specific modeling for multi-step traffic forecasting.

</details>


### [331] [A Lightweight Plastic-Memory Framework for Graph Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25781)
*Zihan Mei,Zhili Qin,Tongze Zhang,Hongyuan Liu,Junming Shao,Qinli Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向图小样本增量学习的轻量“可塑记忆”框架，通过动态演化的微聚类记忆模块与记忆驱动元学习，在缓解灾难性遗忘的同时提升对新类的少样本适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图增量学习需应对动态图数据中的灾难性遗忘。现有方法多依赖回放或蒸馏保留节点知识，但计算开销大、效率低；在新类标注数据稀缺的真实场景下，该问题进一步加剧。因此需要一种轻量、高效且适用于小样本图增量学习的方案。

Method: 1) 构建随时间演化的可塑记忆模块，持续更新与扩展记忆以容纳新类并保留旧知识；2) 设计轻量且有效的演化微聚类结构，动态更新类原型、子原型及其交互权重；3) 基于该记忆模块提出记忆驱动元学习框架：内循环增强对新任务的适应性，外循环维持对早期任务的稳定性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上进行大量实验，结果表明该框架在旧知识稳定性与新知识适应性之间取得了更优平衡，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 可塑记忆与元学习的结合为图小样本类增量学习提供了轻量、有效的解决思路，能够在标注稀缺条件下缓解灾难性遗忘，并提升模型对新类的快速适应能力。

Abstract: Graph Incremental Learning has garnered increasing attention as dynamic graph data continues to emerge across diverse fields. Conventional approaches primarily address catastrophic forgetting by preserving node-related knowledge through replay or distillation techniques; however, they often incur high computational costs and inefficiency. This issue is further exacerbated in real-world scenarios where labeled data for new classes is scarce. In this paper, we propose a novel lightweight plastic-memory framework specifically designed for few-shot incremental learning on graphs. The core idea of our framework is the construction of a plastic-memory module that evolves over time, continuously updating and expanding its memory to accommodate new classes while retaining previously learned knowledge. In contrast to existing techniques, our memory module is both lightweight and effective, featuring an innovative evolving micro-clustering structure that dynamically updates representations of class prototypes, sub-prototypes, and their interaction weights. Building on this memory module, we introduce a memory-driven meta-learning framework that enhances adaptability to new tasks in its inner loop while maintaining stability for earlier tasks in the outer loop. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the framework's superior performance in balancing stability for old knowledge and adaptability to new knowledge.

</details>


### [332] [Evaluating Accuracy and Probabilistic Reliability of Zero-Shot Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25788)
*Panagiotis Michael,Moysis Symeonides,Demetris Trihinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对六种时间序列基础模型（TSFM）在能源、交通、金融数据集上进行基准评测，揭示点预测精度与概率校准之间存在根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM 旨在实现零样本预测、免除任务特定训练，但现有研究常忽视预测精度与概率校准之间的权衡，缺乏对不同架构在真实场景中泛化与不确定性量化能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建基准研究，选取六种 TSFM，在能源、交通、金融三类数据集上进行评估，并与统计基线及一个监督式深度学习模型进行对比。

Result: TSFM 整体优于统计方法和监督模型，但存在点精度与概率可靠性的根本权衡：xLSTM 架构在不同预测步长上均具稳健的概率校准；基于 patch 的 Transformer 精度具竞争力但长步长下出现校准问题；基于 Transformer 的模型存在实现最优零样本推理的上下文饱和点。

Conclusion: 研究为真实部署中平衡泛化能力与不确定性量化提供了基于实证的指导，强调选择 TSFM 时需根据任务需求在精度与校准之间取舍。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) promise a paradigm shift toward zero-shot forecasting by eliminating task-specific training. However, existing works often overlook trade-offs between predictive accuracy and probabilistic calibration. This paper presents a benchmark study of six TSFMs evaluated on energy, traffic, and financial datasets. We contrast their performance against statistical baselines and a supervised DL model. The study reveals that while TSFMs outperform statistical methods and supervised models, they are subject to a fundamental trade-off between point accuracy and probabilistic reliability. Specifically, xLSTM architectures provide robust probabilistic calibration across horizons. In contrast, patch-based transformers offer competitive accuracy but face calibration issues at long horizons, while transformer-based models exhibit context saturation points for optimal zero-shot reasoning. These findings offer evidence-based guidance for balancing generalization and uncertainty quantification in real-world deployments.

</details>


### [333] [Latest Exact Match Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25802)
*Moritz Brösamle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LEMA（最新精确匹配注意力）：查询与键二值化，每个查询仅关注最近一次精确匹配的键。理论上证明其配合思维链可模拟 word-RAM，且 word-RAM 也能以每 token 成本与上下文长度无关地模拟 LEMA，建立二者在计算与内存上的紧密对应；同时给出可训练方法与字典式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬注意力变体（如 rightmost hard attention）虽具表达力，但缺乏高效反向模拟，难以刻画其与经典计算模型在计算和内存上的对应关系。作者希望寻找一种限制更强、但理论对应更紧且可实现高效推理的注意力机制。

Method: 1) 定义 LEMA：对 query/key 二值化，仅让 query 关注最近一次精确匹配的 key；2) 理论分析：证明带思维链的 LEMA transformer 可模拟 word-RAM，并证明 word-RAM 可以每 token 成本与上下文长度无关地模拟 LEMA transformer；3) 训练：用 straight-through estimator 处理二值化，并将软注意力代理逐步退火到 LEMA，以应对不可微操作；4) 推理：实现基于字典的推理，将字典存放于主内存而非显存。

Result: 在合成联想召回任务上，LEMA 利用随上下文增长的状态存储并召回大量关联，优于固定状态大小的 gated DeltaNet（GDN）。在首个扩展性测试中训练至 834M 参数的 LEMA 语言模型，其损失与约一半规模的 softmax transformer 相当；在重复稀有短语和针检索任务上仍落后于 softmax transformer，但在更长距离上的召回优于同规模 GDN。字典式推理在状态增长情况下仍实现与 GDN 相当的恒定生成速度，且字典驻留主内存。

Conclusion: LEMA 通过“精确匹配 + 最新键”约束，在理论上与 word-RAM 形成计算与内存的紧密对应，并在训练、扩展实验与推理效率上展示了可行性；其增长状态带来长距离召回优势，但部分任务质量仍不及同规模 softmax transformer，后续可继续探索性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: We introduce latest exact match attention (LEMA), an attention variant for transformers where queries and keys are binarized and each query attends only to the latest exactly matching key. We prove that LEMA transformers with chain of thought can simulate word-RAMs, as was recently shown for the less restrictive rightmost hard attention. In contrast to prior hard attention variants, the restriction to exact matches enables an efficient converse direction: word-RAMs can simulate LEMA transformers at a cost per token independent of the context length. Together, these results yield a close correspondence between the two computational models in terms of both compute and memory. Beyond the theory, we propose a training method for LEMA transformers that handles their non-differentiable operations with a straight-through estimator for the binarization and a soft attention surrogate annealed towards LEMA. On a synthetic associative recall task, LEMA models trained this way use their growing state to store and recall a large number of associations, outperforming gated DeltaNet (GDN) with its fixed state size. As a first scaling test, we train LEMA language models with up to 834 million parameters. They match softmax transformers of around half their size in loss and, on repeated rare phrases and a needle-retrieval task, remain behind softmax transformers but recall across longer distances than GDN models of comparable size. Finally, we implement dictionary-based inference for LEMA transformers and show constant generation speed comparable to GDN despite their growing state, with the dictionaries residing in main memory rather than VRAM. Code is available at https://github.com/moritzbroe/latest_exact_match_attention.

</details>


### [334] [Auditing Proxy-Based Validation Across Text Spans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25808)
*Daein Weon,Dong Ho Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，当评估分数与其廉价代理标签取自同一文本片段时，二者的一致可能源于共享的表面证据而非代理所要代表的目标构念。作者提出"验证契约"框架，并主张在评分片段之外重新读取代理，以此检验代理一致性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估分数常通过与廉价代理标签的一致性来验证。但当分数与代理由同一文本跨度计算时，这种一致性可能来自二者共享的表面证据，而非代理意在代表的目标构念。既有验证实践未明确区分二者，可能高估代理一致性的意义。

Method: 作者将分数、其跨度、代理与目标构念显式声明为"验证契约"，并在评分跨度之外严格重读该代理规则。在HotpotQA正确性对照实验中，仅改变共享文本边界；在OR-Bench上，抑制各模型反复出现的开头模板，并与等量删除作比较；同时调研11个外部契约与抽样的路由研究。

Result: HotpotQA实验中，在50字符前缀处分数与代理的一致性远高于与正确性的一致性，差距为+0.184，但从120字符起收敛至至多+0.045；该短前缀处分数仍能预测答案串是否出现在后文（AUC 0.634），而等价检验显示其与正确性的一致性仅相当于随机水平。OR-Bench上，抑制反复出现的开头模板后，分数与拒绝代理的关联大部分消失，等量删除则几乎无影响，构念一致性始终处于随机水平。11个外部契约中仅3个支持离跨度对照，且抽样的路由研究无一发布所需生成文本。

Conclusion: 论文呼吁基于代理的验证主张应做到三点：声明每个标签读取自哪个跨度、在报告代理一致性之外同时报告构念一致性、并发布可供在评分跨度之外重读代理的生成文本。

Abstract: Evaluation scores are often validated by their agreement with inexpensive proxy labels. When the score and the proxy are computed from the same text span, however, that agreement can arise from surface evidence the two share rather than from the semantic construct the proxy is meant to represent. We make the distinction explicit by declaring the score, its span, the proxy and the target construct as a validation contract, then re-evaluating that proxy rule strictly outside the scored span. In a controlled HotpotQA correctness experiment varying only the shared text boundary, the score agrees with its proxy far better than with correctness at a 50-character prefix: the gap is +0.184, collapsing to at most +0.045 from 120 characters onward. At that short prefix the score still predicts whether the answer string appears later (AUC 0.634) while an equivalence test places its agreement with correctness at chance, so the reported proxy agreement does not establish that the score ranks correctness. On OR-Bench, suppressing each model's recurring opening templates removes most of the score's association with the refusal proxy, while matched-volume deletion removes almost none and construct agreement stays at chance. Only three of eleven external contracts support the off-span control, and none of the routing studies we sampled released the generations it needs. We therefore ask that a proxy-based validation claim declare the span each label is read from, report the construct agreement beside the proxy agreement, and release the generations that let the proxy be re-read off the scored span.

</details>


### [335] [You Only Need 2/3 of the Chosen Experts: An Empirical Study of Dynamic Expert Pruning in Fine-Grained MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25809)
*Yuanteng Chen,Qiwei Lai,Chen Tianqi,Peisong Wang,Yuantian Shao,Nanxin Zeng,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对细粒度MoE的动态专家剪枝进行系统实证研究，发现每token专家选择高度冗余：均匀保留约2/3专家即可平均保持98.8%的未剪枝性能，并带来1.2–1.7倍实测加速；动态分配在保守预算下增益有限（<1%），仅在激进剪枝时相对均匀截断最多恢复3.0%，且增益集中于生成式任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE已成为开放权重LLM的主流架构，专家数量多且每token选择数增加，使动态专家剪枝成为降低推理成本的重要路径。然而，现有证据主要来自较粗粒度架构和基于似然评分的多项选择基准，细粒度场景下仍存在三个关键问题：每token专家选择冗余程度如何、现有剪枝方法能多有效利用该冗余、以及什么因素决定模型对剪枝的敏感度。

Method: 作者对12个细粒度MoE检查点、覆盖9个架构家族进行系统性实证研究，核心评测套件包含11个基准，覆盖知识问答、数学、代码生成和通用推理。方法上比较均匀截断（uniform truncation）与已发表动态分配规则在匹配专家预算下的表现，并在两个服务后端测量实际加速；同时分析模型规模、思考模型与多模态属性对激进剪枝敏感度的影响。

Result: 专家选择远比领域常用操作点所假设的更冗余：均匀保留约三分之二被选专家，平均可保持98.8%的未剪枝性能，仅需改变一个整数即可实现，并在两个服务后端获得1.2–1.7倍实测加速。这一简单基线使保守预算下动态分配空间很小：即使最佳已发表规则在匹配专家预算下与均匀截断差距也低于1%。其价值在激进剪枝下显现，最佳规则相对均匀截断最多恢复3.0%，增益集中在退化最严重的生成式任务。剪枝敏感度还取决于模型：更大模型和思考模型更鲁棒，多模态模型更脆弱。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了细粒度MoE可舍弃多少专家计算，并明确了动态分配在何种条件下值得其额外复杂度，为实际部署和未来剪枝方法设计提供了依据。核心启示是：保守预算下简单均匀截断已接近最优，动态分配主要应在激进剪枝和生成式任务中发挥价值，同时需考虑模型类型差异。

Abstract: Fine-grained mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures have become a mainstream design for open-weight LLMs, with hundreds of experts and increasingly many selected per token. This shift makes dynamic expert pruning an attractive route to cheaper inference. Yet existing evidence comes largely from coarser architectures and likelihood-scored multiple-choice benchmarks, leaving three central questions open in the fine-grained regime: how redundant per-token expert selection is, how effectively existing pruning methods exploit that redundancy, and what governs a model's sensitivity to pruning. We fill this gap with a systematic empirical study of twelve fine-grained MoE checkpoints spanning nine architecture families, with a core suite of eleven benchmarks covering knowledge QA, mathematics, code generation, and general reasoning. We find that expert selection is far more redundant than the field's operating points assume: uniformly retaining about two thirds of the selected experts preserves 98.8% of unpruned performance on average, requiring only a one-integer change and delivering 1.2-1.7x measured speedup across two serving backends. This simple baseline leaves little room for dynamic allocation at conservative budgets: even the best published rules differ from it by under 1% at matched expert budgets. Their value emerges under aggressive pruning, where the best rules recover up to 3.0% over uniform truncation, with gains concentrated in the generative tasks that suffer the sharpest degradation. Sensitivity to aggressive pruning also depends on the model: larger and thinking models are more resilient, whereas multimodal models are more vulnerable. Together, these findings reveal how much expert computation fine-grained MoEs can dispense with, and establish when dynamic allocation earns its complexity, informing both practical deployment and future pruning methods.

</details>


### [336] [Multi-View Fair Clustering Guided by Cross-View Sensitive Information Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25811)
*Mudi Jiang,Jiahui Zhou,Xinying Liu,Zengyou He,Zhikui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG
