TL;DR: 该论文提出一种由跨视图敏感信息差异引导的多视图公平聚类框架：先估计各视图的敏感依赖程度，再通过偏置排序的非对称对齐机制让敏感依赖高的视图向敏感依赖低的视图学习，并用跨视图差异自适应调节对齐，同时对共识软分配施加公平正则化，从而在聚类质量与群体公平性之间取得较好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图聚类旨在利用多视图互补信息发现潜在簇结构，但在社会敏感场景中，公平性至关重要。现有公平多视图聚类方法通常将公平约束引入表示学习或聚类分配，却对不同视图采用近乎统一的公平机制，未显式区分各视图在跨视图学习中的敏感依赖差异。实际中不同视图可能编码不同程度敏感信息；若忽略这种跨视图差异，高敏感依赖视图可能影响低敏感依赖视图，进而损害聚类性能与公平性。

Method: 提出一种由跨视图敏感信息差异引导的多视图公平聚类框架。具体包括：1）估计每个视图的敏感依赖程度；2）设计偏置排序的非对称对齐机制，鼓励敏感依赖较高的视图向敏感依赖较低的视图学习；3）利用跨视图差异自适应调节对齐过程；4）对共识软分配施加公平正则化，以进一步促进群体公平。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在聚类质量与群体公平性之间实现了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并利用跨视图敏感信息差异，并采用非对称对齐与公平正则化，可有效缓解统一公平机制带来的负面影响，为公平多视图聚类提供了一种兼顾聚类性能与群体公平性的新思路。

Abstract: Multi-view clustering (MVC) aims to uncover latent cluster structures by exploiting complementary information from multiple views. Despite substantial progress in clustering performance, fairness remains an important concern when MVC is applied to socially sensitive scenarios. Recent fair multi-view clustering methods have introduced fairness constraints into representation learning or clustering assignments. However, these methods generally treat different views under a largely uniform fairness mechanism, without explicitly distinguishing their varying levels of sensitive dependence during cross-view learning. In practice, different views may encode substantially different levels of sensitive information. Ignoring such cross-view discrepancy can allow highly sensitive-dependent views to influence less sensitive-dependent ones during cross-view learning, potentially degrading both clustering performance and fairness. To address this issue, we propose a novel multi-view fair clustering framework guided by cross-view sensitive information discrepancy. Specifically, we estimate the sensitive dependence of each view and develop a bias-ranked asymmetric alignment mechanism that encourages views with higher sensitive dependence to learn from those with lower sensitive dependence, while cross-view discrepancies are further exploited to adaptively regulate the alignment process. Moreover, fairness regularization is imposed on the consensus soft assignments to further promote group fairness. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a favorable balance between clustering quality and group fairness.

</details>


### [337] [CacheDyG: Decoupling Temporal Propagation for Efficient Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25814)
*PinHeng Zong,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CacheDyG：一种缓存-精炼（Cache-refine）框架，通过将时序传播与常规参数更新解耦，利用非训练缓存存储图感知的节点-时间表示，仅在训练中更新轻量缓存精炼器、自适应残差门与链接预测器，并选择性刷新缓存，从而在五个动态图基准上以更少可训练参数和更低运行时间取得有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图神经网络可同时建模结构依赖与时序动态，但现有方法通常将时序图传播与每个优化轮次耦合，并为每个节点-时间对维护大规模可训练表示，导致反复计算变化很小的历史结构，带来显著的训练开销与参数开销。

Method: CacheDyG 的核心是缓存式解耦：构建按时间排序的时序依赖缓存，将图感知的节点-时间表示存储于不可训练缓冲区中；在标准训练轮次中从缓存读取表示，仅更新轻量缓存精炼器、自适应残差门和链接预测器；同时采用选择性缓存刷新，使缓存表示与监督目标保持对齐，并避免逐轮次的稀疏传播。

Result: 在五个动态图基准数据集上，CacheDyG 相比基线方法使用了显著更少的可训练参数和更低的运行时间，同时获得了更具竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于缓存的解耦机制为可扩展的动态图学习提供了一种有效原则。

Abstract: Dynamic graphs are widely used to model time-evolving relational systems in real-world applications. Dynamic graph neural networks provide an effective framework for capturing both structural dependencies and temporal dynamics in such data. However, they typically intertwine temporal graph propagation with every optimization epoch and often maintain large trainable representations for each node-time pair. This design repeatedly recomputes largely unchanged historical structures, leading to substantial training and parameter overhead. To address this critical issue, we propose CacheDyG, a Cache-refine framework for efficient Dynamic Graph learning. Specifically, it decouples temporal propagation from routine parameter updates by constructing a time-ordered temporal dependency cache that stores graph-aware node-time representations in non-trainable buffers. During standard training epochs, CacheDyG reads from the cache and updates only a lightweight cache refiner, an adaptive residual gate, and the link predictor. Selective cache refresh further keeps cached representations aligned with the supervised objective while avoiding epoch-wise sparse propagation. Experiments on five dynamic graph benchmarks show that CacheDyG adopts substantially fewer trainable parameters and lower runtime to obtain more competitive predictive performance than baselines. These results demonstrate that cache-based decoupling provides an effective principle for scalable dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [338] [Protocol before progress: leakage-aware evaluation of AIS trajectory prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25827)
*Zobeir Raisi,Vali Mohammad Nazarzehi Had*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出 AIS 船舶轨迹预测的性能提升常被归因于新架构，但评测协议本身可能是主要误差来源；作者构建防泄漏的船/时间/区域不相交划分，并在丹麦与美湾两套 AIS 语料上审计 TrAISformer、GATransformer 及受控 AISFormer 重构模型，发现协议效应常大于架构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AIS 轨迹预测研究多将误差下降归功于新模型架构，却很少把评测协议作为误差来源进行量化。若数据划分存在船舶、时间或区域泄漏，模型可能通过记忆或分布重叠获得虚高指标，导致架构贡献被高估、结论难以泛化。

Method: 构建 leakage-aware 评测协议：采用 vessel-disjoint、time-disjoint、region-disjoint 三种划分，并在两个交通特征不同的语料上验证——丹麦全国 AIS 交通 31 天、美国墨西哥湾休斯顿与加尔维斯顿近岸 30 天。审计对象包括 TrAISformer、GATransformer，以及受控的 AISFormer-inspired 重构模型；通过 greedy decoding 与 best-of-16 oracle decoding 对比、消融图注意力与水道特征、并分析测试上下文经纬度分布，分离协议效应与架构效应。

Result: 两个语料均出现三类协议效应：第一，TrAISformer 的 best-of-16 oracle 解码相对 greedy 解码将误差降低 2.1–3.2 倍；第二，共享船舶的划分使一小时 greedy 误差降低 23–25%，而紧凑 0.43 M 参数编码器仅 ≤2%；第三，区域不相交划分使 TrAISformer 在美国语料的一小时误差从 2.2 km 升至 24.6 km，原因是 99.9% 测试上下文落入训练未见的经度分箱，而基于局部偏移的编码器不受影响。架构机制影响较小：GATransformer 的图注意力在两套语料均无可测收益，其水道特征贡献 12–22%；时间不相交划分效应跨语料不稳定，为 13% 与 2%。作者公开划分与代码。

Conclusion: AIS 轨迹预测的评测协议可显著主导报告误差，防泄漏划分与解码策略必须被明确控制；区域外推与船舶泄漏会严重扭曲结论。紧凑的局部偏移编码器对区域不相交更鲁棒，而图注意力等架构机制收益有限；应发布标准划分与代码以支持可复现、可比较的评测。

Abstract: Reported gains in vessel-trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data are credited to new architectures, but the evaluation protocol is rarely measured as a source of error reduction. We build a leakage-aware protocol with vessel-, time- and region-disjoint splits and apply it to two corpora with different traffic: 31 days of Danish national AIS traffic and 30 days of US Gulf coast traffic off Houston and Galveston. On both, we audit TrAISformer, GATransformer, and controlled AISFormer-inspired reconstructions. Three protocol effects appear in both corpora. First, TrAISformer's best-of-16 oracle decoder lowers error by a factor of 2.1-3.2 relative to greedy decoding. Second, a split that shares vessels lowers its greedy error by 23-25% at one hour, against 2% or less for a compact 0.43 M-parameter encoder. Third, a region-disjoint split raises TrAISformer's one-hour error from 2.2 to 24.6 km on the US corpus, because 99.9% of the test contexts fall in longitude bins never seen in training; the encoder built on local offsets is unaffected by this. Architectural mechanisms matter less: GATransformer's graph attention gives no measurable benefit on either corpus, while its waterway feature is worth 12-22%. The effect of a time-disjoint split is not stable across corpora (13% versus 2%). We release the splits and code.

</details>


### [339] [In-Context Guidance: Learning Inter-Task Synergies via Numerical Foundational Models for Few-Shot Multitask Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25836)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Jiao Liu,Zhao Wei,Puay Siew Tan,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ICG-MTO 框架，利用冻结的数值基础模型通过上下文学习为多任务优化提供任务间耦合关系的先验引导，缓解少样本场景下的负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务优化（MTO）需同时求解多个优化任务，但在评估预算受限的少样本场景中，任务间关系估计不准确，易导致负迁移，且传统方法仅依赖稀缺的已观测数据难以纠正。

Method: ICG-MTO 采用三阶段流程：(1) 由已评估解构造算法特定的上下文查询；(2) 用冻结的基础模型经上下文学习推断刻画任务间预测关系的引导信号；(3) 将该信号转化为算法特定引导，用于最大后验耦合估计。该方法在优化早期数据稀缺阶段起正则化作用，随任务特定观测增多逐步释放控制权。具体实例化为两个算法：ICG-MTBO（采用方向性适应度类别查询，引导多任务贝叶斯优化中的任务间耦合估计）与 MFEA-II（采用决策空间重叠查询，引导随机交配概率估计）。

Result: 在合成基准、真实机械臂控制问题上的实验，以及在不同采集函数和进化多任务设定下的评估，均表明 ICG-MTO 在少样本多任务优化中具有有效性与通用性。

Conclusion: ICG-MTO 通过基础模型上下文引导改进任务间耦合估计，为少样本多任务优化提供了有效且可推广的通用框架，并能灵活嵌入贝叶斯优化与进化多任务等不同算法范式。

Abstract: Multi-task optimization (MTO) addresses a set of optimization tasks simultaneously, often suffering from inaccurate inter-task relationship estimation under limited evaluation budgets, leading to negative transfer. This paper introduces In-Context Guidance Multitask Optimization (ICG-MTO), a novel framework that leverages numerical foundational models to improve inter-task coupling estimation in few-shot scenarios. Unlike conventional methods that rely solely on scarce observed data, ICG-MTO employs a frozen foundational model to infer auxiliary guidance through in-context learning. The framework operates through three stages: constructing an algorithm-specific in-context query from evaluated solutions, using the foundational model to infer a guidance signal characterizing predictive relationships among tasks, and translating this signal into algorithm-specific guidance for maximum-a-posteriori coupling estimation. This approach provides regularization during the early, data-scarce stages of optimization and gradually relinquishes control as task-specific observations accumulate. We instantiate the framework in multitask Bayesian optimization as ICG-MTBO, using directional fitness-class queries to guide inter-task coupling estimation, and further instantiate it in MFEA-II using decision-space-overlap queries to guide random mating probability estimation. Experiments across synthetic benchmarks and a real-world robot arm control problem, together with evaluations under different acquisition functions and evolutionary multitasking, demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of ICG-MTO for few-shot multitask optimization.

</details>


### [340] [Gaussian Flow-Matching Schedules: Implications for Sampling and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25839)
*Arsène Claustre,Hugo Negrel,Claire Boyer,Kimia Nadjahi,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对中心化且协方差可交换的高斯分布，该文表明方向依赖的流匹配调度可解耦为“方差路径”和“因子分解”两个独立设计维度：前者决定中间分布与概率流，后者在保持该流不变的同时控制不可约回归方差。作者还分析了有限步欧拉采样的精度与精确N步采样的必要漂移界，并为任意固定路径给出闭式因子分解，以最小化或恒定化时间平均回归方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配的调度同时影响采样动态和回归目标方差，二者通常相互耦合，难以分别设计与分析。该文旨在将调度设计解耦，明确方差路径与因子分解各自的作用，并为采样精度和训练方差提供理论刻画，从而连接测地路径与对数路径等典型调度。

Method: 在中心化可交换高斯设定下，将方向依赖调度分解为方差路径与因子分解。采样侧，分析有限步欧拉法的精度，并推导精确N步采样所需的漂移界；训练侧，对任意固定方差路径，推导闭式因子分解，以优化时间平均回归方差或使其沿路径保持恒定。

Result: 方差路径完全决定中间律和概率流；因子分解不改变该概率流，但可控制不可约回归方差。分析给出了有限步欧拉精度、精确N步采样的必要漂移界，并建立了测地路径与对数路径之间的联系。对于任意固定路径，可获得使时间平均回归方差最小或沿路径恒定的闭式因子分解。

Conclusion: 该文将流匹配调度设计解耦为方差路径选择与因子分解选择，提供了兼顾采样精度与训练方差的理论工具。结果表明，可在不改变生成概率流的前提下优化回归方差，并为实际调度设计提供原则性指导。

Abstract: Flow-matching schedules affect both sampling dynamics and the variance of the regression target. For centered commuting Gaussians, we show that a direction-dependent schedule decomposes into two independent design choices: a variance path, which fully determines the intermediate laws and probability flow, and a factorization, which leaves this flow unchanged while controlling irreducible regression variance. On the sampling side, we analyze finite-step Euler accuracy and derive a necessary drift bound for exact N -step sampling, connecting the geodesic and the logarithmic path. On the training side, for any fixed path, we derive closed-form factorizations that either minimize time-averaged regression variance or make it constant along the path.

</details>


### [341] [Neural Approximation by Function Composition: Rigidity and Doubly Exponential Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25874)
*Wentao Huang,Haizhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究深度网络逼近能力的一种基本复合机制：单标量生成函数迭代的几何加权和。作者先给出分段线性生成元的刚性定理，说明该机制对函数复杂度的限制；再利用精确余项恒等式构造光滑生成元，实现平方与多项式逼近的双指数深度误差衰减，并在高维幂级数情形得到 O(e^{-cL^{1/d}}) 的均匀逼近率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络通过仿射映射与非线性激活的复合逼近函数，但“复合”本身如何产生逼近能力仍未被充分理解。该机制是 tent-map 构造 \(x-x^2\) 以及 Yarotsky、W. E 等人递归表示的基础，因此需要厘清其能力边界与可扩展性。

Method: 将问题抽象为单标量生成函数迭代的几何加权和；针对连续分段线性且有限段数的生成元，证明刚性/次数上界并给出非仿射二次函数的几何因子下界；利用精确余项恒等式设计光滑生成元，结合乘法模块，并按单项式次数分配网络深度。

Result: 刚性定理表明：若生成元为有限段连续分段线性函数，则任何可用该机制表示的 \(C^3\) 函数至多二次；对非仿射二次函数，几何因子至少为 \(1/4\)。构造性结果：光滑生成元的迭代可在总深度上实现平方逼近的双指数误差衰减，并经乘法模块推广到每个固定多项式；对 \([-1,1]^d\) 上系数绝对可和的幂级数，按单项式次数分配深度可在每个内部立方体上达到 \(O(e^{-cL^{1/d}})\) 的均匀逼近误差。

Conclusion: 生成元动力学与余项估计共同决定深度分配和深度网络的逼近速率。该结果既揭示了 tent-map 方法的局限，也补充了层次基与递归多项式构造等既有方法，并给出了基于生成元设计的高效逼近框架。

Abstract: Deep neural networks approximate functions by composing affine maps with nonlinear activations, but how composition itself creates approximation power is not yet fully understood. We investigate a fundamental mechanism: geometrically weighted sums of iterates of a single scalar generator function. This mechanism underpins the classical tent-map construction of the function \(x - x^2\) and related recursive representations used by Yarotsky, W. E, et al., to analyze the approximation powers of deep neural networks.
  First, we establish a rigidity theorem: for continuous piecewise linear generators with a finite number of segments, any \(C^3\) function that can be represented in this way is at most quadratic. For non-affine quadratic functions, the geometric factor is at least $1/4$. This result both reveals limitations of the tent-map approach and complements existing methods based on hierarchical bases and recursive polynomial constructions. Second, using an exact remainder identity as guidance, we construct a smooth generator whose iterates yield doubly exponential error decay in total depth for square approximation and, through multiplication modules, for each fixed polynomial. For power series with absolutely summable coefficients on \([-1,1]^d\), distributing depth according to monomial degree yields a uniform approximation error of order \(O(e^{-cL^{1/d}})\) on each interior cube. These findings demonstrate how generator dynamics and remainder estimates govern depth allocation and approximation rates of deep neural networks.

</details>


### [342] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of SechKAN on 1D Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25876)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了KAN变体SechKAN的有效性：该网络以双曲正割(sech)函数作为基函数，并通过1D投影将参数量降至与MLP相当的水平。在三个1D分类数据集(UCI HAR、ElectricDevices、Crop)上与EfficientKAN、MLP、CNN1D、ResNet1D、DSCNN1D在近似参数量预算下对比，SechKAN整体具竞争力，在Crop上表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理(KART)与神经网络设计的结合催生了KANs，已应用于STEM及AI任务。但KAN通常参数量较大，因此作者希望探索一种以sech为基函数、并通过1D投影压缩参数量的KAN变体(SechKAN)，使其参数量可与MLP相比，从而评估其在1D分类任务中的实用性。

Method: 提出SechKAN：以双曲正割(sech)函数作为可学习边上的基函数，并采用1D投影以降低参数量至MLP量级。在三个1D分类数据集(UCI Human Activity Recognition、ElectricDevices、Crop)上评估，与EfficientKAN、MLP、CNN1D、ResNet1D、DSCNN1D在约略相当的参数预算下进行对比，并开展消融实验考察网格大小(grid size)与归一化(normalization)的影响。

Result: SechKAN在三个数据集上均取得具竞争力的性能，其中在Crop数据集上表现尤为强劲。消融研究表明网格大小与归一化会影响性能，说明SechKAN的有效性依赖于具体数据集与架构选择。

Conclusion: SechKAN作为一种参数高效的KAN变体，在1D分类任务中可与MLP、CNN1D、ResNet1D、DSCNN1D等有效网络竞争，其性能受数据集和设计选择(如网格大小、归一化)影响。源码与实验实现已公开于https://github.com/hoangthangta/SechKAN_1D。

Abstract: The connection between the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem (KART) and neural network design has led to the development of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), with applications ranging from STEM problems to AI tasks. In this paper, we investigate the effectiveness of a KAN variant, SechKAN, which relies on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions as basis functions, with a 1D projection to reduce the number of parameters to a level comparable to MLPs. We evaluate SechKAN on three 1D classification datasets: UCI Human Activity Recognition (UCI HAR), ElectricDevices, and Crop, and compare it with several effective networks, including EfficientKAN, MLP, CNN1D, ResNet1D, and DSCNN1D, using approximately comparable parameter budgets. The results indicate that SechKAN achieves competitive performance across the three datasets, with particularly strong performance on Crop. Ablation studies further show that grid size and normalization affect performance, suggesting that SechKAN's effectiveness depends on the dataset and architectural choices. Our source code and experimental implementation are publicly available at: https://github.com/hoangthangta/SechKAN_1D.

</details>


### [343] [AURA: Angular Update Rate Adaptation for training complex-valued neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25914)
*Enrico Ballini,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup,Tito Andriollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AURA 是一种面向复值神经网络的逐参数步长自适应方法，利用相邻复参数更新在长度、方向与旋转方向上的“一致性”来放大或缩小步长；它可叠加于任意一阶优化器且不改变更新方向、无需额外梯度计算。与 Adam、Muon 结合后在四个复杂度递增的测试任务中，多数情况下加速收敛，仅有少量每步开销，并给出了失效条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复值神经网络（CVNNs）越来越多地用于复值数据，但训练常沿用实值一阶优化器。这类方法的效率高度依赖步长，且其步长规则忽略了复平面中可利用的角度信息，因此需要为复域设计更合适的步长自适应机制。

Method: 提出 AURA（Angular Update Rate Adaptation），一种可插拔的逐参数步长自适应方法：可加在任意一阶优化器上，也可移除，且不改变其更新方向。AURA 对每个复参数的连续两次更新进行一致性度量，综合考虑长度、对齐程度与旋转方向；当更新一致时增大步长，不一致时减小步长。该方法不增加额外梯度评估，每步仅需廉价的向量运算。作者将 AURA 与 Adam、Muon 结合，并在四个复杂度递增的测试案例中与知名一阶优化器比较，任务从标量复函数逼近到物理信息训练；全程使用全连接神经网络。

Result: 除步长外的所有超参数在各测试案例中保持固定；其中一个案例还在相同预算下对各优化器的超参数进行调参。实验表明，AURA 在大多数情况下能改善其基础优化器的收敛性，且每步开销较小；同时作者识别了 AURA 未能带来改进的条件。

Conclusion: AURA 为复值神经网络提供了一种通用、低成本、不改方向的步长自适应方案，能有效提升基础一阶优化器的收敛表现，但其收益并非普遍成立，存在明确适用边界。

Abstract: Complex-valued neural networks (CVNNs) are increasingly adopted for complex-valued data; however, they are often trained with first-order optimizers inherited from the real-valued case. The efficiency of these methods depends largely on the step size, and their step-size rules ignore the angular information available in the complex plane. We address step-size adaptation in the complex domain by introducing AURA (Angular Update Rate Adaptation), a per-parameter step-size adaptation that can be added on top of any first-order optimizer, and removed from it, without altering its update direction. AURA measures the agreement between consecutive updates of each complex parameter, in length, alignment, and sense of rotation, and enlarges the step when they are consistent and reduces it when they are not. It requires no additional gradient evaluations and only inexpensive vector operations per step. We combine AURA with Adam and Muon and compare the resulting methods with well-known first-order optimizers on four test cases of increasing complexity, ranging from the approximation of scalar complex functions to physics-informed training. Fully connected neural networks are used throughout this work. All hyperparameters other than the step size are held fixed across test cases; for one case, we also tune the hyperparameters of each optimizer under the same budget. Our empirical tests show that AURA improves the convergence of its base optimizer in most cases with a small per-step overhead, and we identify the conditions under which it fails to do so.

</details>


### [344] [Beyond Scalar Sensitivity: Activation-Aware Mixed-Precision LLM Quantization with Cross-Layer Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25916)
*Akihiro Yoshida,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出混合精度权重量化中常用的标量敏感度代理会把每个权重矩阵的Hessian压缩为单个数并独立处理模块，导致模块间敏感度排序不可靠；作者证明了标量代理相对完整激活感知二次型存在可达 \(\sqrt{\kappa(\mathbf{A})\kappa(\mathbf{B})}\) 的乘性失真，并提出两阶段方法 CASA：用基于Kronecker分解Hessian的激活感知度量替代标量代理，并通过跨层局部搜索优化比特宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度权重量化通常建模为多选择背包问题（MCKP），但现有求解器依赖标量敏感度代理：将每个权重矩阵的Hessian坍缩为单一数值，并把各模块独立处理。作者旨在揭示这种简化在LLM模块中的理论缺陷，并解决由此导致的跨模块敏感度排序不可靠问题。

Method: 论文首先从理论上证明，即使采用最优标量代理，相对于完整的激活感知二次型也会产生最高达 \(\sqrt{\kappa(\mathbf{A})\kappa(\mathbf{B})}\) 的乘性失真，其中 \(\kappa(\mathbf{A})\)、\(\kappa(\mathbf{B})\) 分别为输入侧和输出侧Hessian因子的条件数。为缓解该问题，提出 Cross-layer Activation-aware Sensitivity Allocation（CASA）两阶段方法：Stage 1 用由Kronecker分解Hessian导出的激活感知度量替代标量代理，将MCKP转化为连续松弛具有闭式解的形式；Stage 2 使用跨层感知的局部搜索，基于端到端模型损失评估比特宽度更新。

Result: 在多个LLM和不同比特预算上的实验表明，CASA相比最新的标量代理基线取得更低的困惑度，尤其在超低比特宽度（每权重小于3比特）时优势明显。此外，零样本准确率上的性能增益与各模型模块平均条件数相关，验证了所推导的失真上界可作为标量代理失效的实用指标。

Conclusion: 标量敏感度代理在LLM混合精度量化中存在可量化的乘性失真，其严重程度可由Hessian因子的条件数刻画；采用激活感知的Kronecker分解度量与跨层局部搜索的CASA能有效提升低比特量化性能，且条件数可作为判断标量代理可靠性的诊断指标。

Abstract: Mixed-precision weight quantization is commonly formulated as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), yet existing solvers rely on scalar sensitivity proxies that collapse each weight matrix's Hessian into a single number and treat every module independently. We prove that even the optimal scalar proxy incurs multiplicative distortion up to $\sqrt{κ(\mathbf{A})κ(\mathbf{B})}$ relative to the full activation-aware quadratic, where $κ(\mathbf{A})$ and $κ(\mathbf{B})$ denote the condition numbers of the input- and output-side Hessian factors. This bound varies from $10^1$ to $10^{13}$ for typical LLM modules, making inter-module sensitivity ranking unreliable. To address these limitations, we propose Cross-layer Activation-aware Sensitivity Allocation (CASA), a two-phase method. In Stage 1, the scalar proxy is replaced by an activation-aware metric derived from the Kronecker-factored Hessian, reducing the MCKP to a form whose continuous relaxation admits a closed-form solution. In Stage 2, a cross-layer-aware local search evaluates bit-width updates using the end-to-end model loss. Experiments on multiple LLMs across different bit budgets show that CASA achieves lower perplexity than the latest scalar-proxy baselines, especially at ultra-low bit-widths ($<3$ bits per weight). Moreover, the performance gain in zero-shot accuracy tracks the per-model average condition-number over modules, confirming the distortion bound as a practical indicator of scalar-proxy failure.

</details>


### [345] [Certified Against Which Oracle? Execution Labels Set the Reported Risk of Conformal Abstention for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25938)
*Jiamiao Liu,Dewen Qiao,Yu Zhang,Xuetao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册干预实验证明：text-to-SQL 的 conformal 弃权证书（abstention certificate）的可靠性完全受限于其校准所用的正确性标签（oracle）。在 Spider-Realistic 上，用基准蒸馏的多实例测试套件替换原有单一数据库后，证书的留出风险被显著低估；且两个 oracle 均无法复现专家判定的风险。因此证书应同时报告两个 oracle，一致性评分应在未参与其构建的 oracle 下评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: conformal 弃权证书的真实性取决于其校准所依赖的正确性标签，而现有不确定性流程从基准自带的单一数据库读取执行一致性标签，该 oracle 已知偏宽松（lenient）。作者要检验：更换 oracle 会如何改变证书所报告的风险、以及评分本身的评估。

Method: 在 Spider-Realistic 上进行预注册干预：将原有单一数据库替换为基准蒸馏的多实例测试套件。覆盖四个 SQL 专用检查点和两种划分方案。引入两名 SQL 专家的盲标标签，并对更严格 oracle 的拒绝样本做 AI 标注普查，另以预注册的盲审专家复核其标记。

Result: 替换 oracle 后，证书留出风险比其自身标签所报告高出 2.73–10.23 个百分点；两个 oracle 均未复现专家判定的风险。在专家盲标下，名义 0.10 校准的证书在两项检查点上实际风险达 20.0 和 17.2 个百分点。更严格的 oracle 双向出错：多数被拒答案并非被判错误，部分被接受的实为错误；AI 普查发现其拒绝样本中 1/4 至 1/3 存在语义错误。每个执行一致性评分在其聚类构建所用 oracle 的标签下都表现更优（16/16）。在专家标签下，用套件聚类替代自带数据库聚类使 AUROC 在两项检查点上分别提升 6.96 和 1.53 个百分点，第二项上专家区间排除了套件标签报告的 8.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 证书应同时用两个 oracle 报告；oracle 相对差异只有在基准经过审计后才能解读为语义风险；一致性评分应在未参与其构建的 oracle 下进行评估。

Abstract: A conformal abstention certificate for text-to-SQL is only as truthful as the correctness labels it is calibrated on. The uncertainty pipelines that read confidence off execution consistency take those labels from the single database a benchmark ships, an oracle known to be lenient. We run a preregistered intervention on Spider-Realistic, swapping that database for the benchmark's distilled multi-instance test suite. Across four SQL-specialist checkpoints and two split schemes, the swap raises the certificate's held-out risk 2.73 to 10.23 points above the risk its own labels report. Neither oracle reports the risk experts assign. Under blinded labels from two SQL experts, a certificate calibrated at a nominal 0.10 carries 20.0 and 17.2 points of risk on two checkpoints. The stricter oracle errs in both directions: most of the answers it rejects are not judged wrong, and some of those it accepts are. An AI-assigned census of what it rejects finds a semantic error in a quarter to a third of them, depending on the population. It attributes most of the rest to underspecified questions, synthetic instances or suspected reference-query defects, a flag supported by a preregistered blinded expert audit. The oracle also decides how a confidence score is judged. Every execution-consistency score looks better under the labels of the oracle that built its clusters, in 16 of 16 combinations. Under expert labels, building such a score on suite clusters instead of shipped-database clusters raises its area under the ROC curve (AUROC) by 6.96 points on one checkpoint and 1.53 on the other. On the second, the expert interval excludes the 8.3 points the suite labels report. A certificate should be reported with both oracles, and an oracle-relative difference read as semantic risk only after the benchmark is audited. A consistency score should be evaluated under an oracle that did not build it.

</details>


### [346] [Exploring Solver-Level Warmstarting for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25962)
*Annelot Bosman,Minghao Liu,Marta Kwiatkowska,Holger Hoos,Jan van Rijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究在神经网络验证中引入求解器级热启动（solver-level warmstarting），通过复用先前求解信息来加速验证；实验表明在扰动半径、输入数据和网络发生变化时，热启动通常能显著减少验证时间，并能在时限内解决从头求解无法完成的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证虽能为网络行为提供形式化保证，但最坏情况下计算不可行，即使是常见的对抗鲁棒性验证问题也是 NP-完全问题，因此需要提升求解效率。

Method: 探索将求解器级热启动用于神经网络验证，以利用先前求解结果中的信息；设计通用且可潜在适配先进验证器的流水线，并在扰动半径、输入数据和网络本身等属性变化时评估其运行时间影响。

Result: 在大多数情况下，热启动可显著降低验证时间；同时，它使一些在给定时间限制内无法从头求解的实例能够被成功验证。

Conclusion: 求解器级热启动是提升神经网络验证效率的有效手段，具有通用性和可扩展性，尤其适合利用相关验证问题之间的相似性来加速求解并扩大可验证实例范围。

Abstract: Neural network verification has become a key tool for providing formal guarantees on the behaviour of neural networks. However, many verification problems remain computationally intractable in the worst case: even for common adversarial robustness specifications, verification is NP-complete. Here, we explore the application of solver-level warmstarting for neural network verification to exploit information from previous solutions. We study the effect on running time as several properties are modified, including perturbation radii, input data and the networks themselves, using a pipeline that is generalisable and potentially adaptable to state-of-the-art verifiers. Our results show that warmstarting can significantly reduce verification time in most cases. Moreover, warmstarting enables the successful verification of instances that could not be solved from scratch within the given time limit.

</details>


### [347] [GeoPair: Geometry-Preserving Cross-Layer Factorization for Training-Free Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25963)
*Baher Mohammad,Ammar Ali,Stamatios Lefkimmiatis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的 Transformer 压缩框架，通过跨层权重配对与共享字典分解，并结合结构化稀疏，在多种架构、规模和模态上实现 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练压缩流程通常逐层独立优化，或采用忽略层特定激活几何的启发式分组策略，未能原则化地利用 Transformer 的跨层冗余。

Method: 顺序优化跨层权重配对与共享字典分解；识别结构兼容的投影，并学习共享表示以更好保留各层不同的校准几何；避免强制相邻层共享基或启发式合并激活统计；结合结构化稀疏，实现高效权重分解且不牺牲功能保真度。

Result: 在多种架构、规模和模态上取得最先进结果，持续优于独立结构化权重分解以及基于启发式分组策略的成对权重分解方法。

Conclusion: 以收敛的优化驱动流程替代启发式工程策略，为可扩展、跨模态的 Transformer 压缩奠定了更具理论依据的基础。

Abstract: Transformer architectures exhibit cross-layer redundancies, yet post-training compression pipelines typically optimize layers in isolation or rely on heuristic grouping strategies that disregard layer-specific activation geometries. We introduce a principled, training-free framework that sequentially optimizes cross-layer weight pairings and shared-dictionary factorizations. Rather than forcing weights of adjacent layers to share a basis or heuristically merging activation statistics, our approach identifies structurally compatible projections and learns a shared representation that better preserves each layer's distinct calibration geometry. Coupled with structured sparsity, this yields highly efficient weight decompositions without sacrificing functional fidelity. Across diverse architectures, scales, and modalities, our method achieves state-of-the-art results, consistently outperforming independent structured weight decompositions and alternative pairwise weight factorizations, which operate under heuristic grouping strategies. By replacing heuristic engineering strategies with a convergent, optimization-driven pipeline, we establish a theoretically grounded foundation for scalable, transformer compression across different modalities.

</details>


### [348] [Interweaving Marginals into Multivariate Sample Paths: Training-Free Dependence Construction for Probabilistic Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25980)
*Jinmyeong Choi,Jinkwan Jang,Seul Lee,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究免训练地将冻结的概率时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的逐坐标边缘预测分布耦合为多变量未来轨迹样本路径；通过固定各通道—时域坐标的经验边缘样本集来隔离耦合效应，发现历史时序与通道关系能显著改善依赖诊断，并主张将依赖重构视为概率TSFM的独立后处理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率TSFM通常只提供逐坐标（channel-horizon）的预测分布，但边缘分布无法唯一确定多变量未来轨迹的联合分布，因此多变量预测中的依赖结构未被刻画。作者关注如何在不重新训练模型的情况下，将冻结TSFM的边缘分布耦合为多变量预测样本路径。

Method: 提出免训练的耦合方法，将冻结TSFM的边缘预测分布转化为多变量预测样本路径。主要评估设置中，固定每个通道—时域坐标的经验边缘样本多重集在不同方法间保持一致，从而单独隔离“耦合”这一因素的影响；同时也在去除固定边缘约束、直接采样路径，以及原生多变量骨干推理的设置下进行验证。

Result: 历史时序关系与通道关系能够显著改善相应的依赖诊断指标。该模式在去除固定边缘约束并直接采样路径时仍然存在，并且在原生多变量骨干推理下也保持稳定。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于概率TSFM，依赖重构应被视为一个独立的后处理问题；在边缘分布之外恢复多变量联合依赖具有独立且重要的研究价值。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series foundation models (TSFMs) provide coordinate-wise predictive distributions, but these marginals do not determine a joint distribution over multivariate future trajectories. We study training-free coupling of frozen TSFM marginals into multivariate forecast sample paths. Our primary evaluation fixes the empirical marginal sample multiset at every channel--horizon coordinate across methods, isolating the effect of coupling alone. Historical temporal and channel relations substantially improve their corresponding dependence diagnostics. The same pattern persists when the fixed-marginal constraint is removed and paths are sampled directly, and remains present under native multivariate backbone inference. These results support treating dependence reconstruction as a distinct post-processing problem for probabilistic TSFMs.

</details>


### [349] [Theory for groupoid equivariant neural networks: an approach for steerable CNNs on bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25987)
*Alberto Ibort,Maria Jimenez-Vazquez,Juan M. Perez-Pardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出群胚等变神经网络理论：以群胚、局部双截断伪群、测度和输入/输出表示丛为对称数据，证明双截断等变核定理，并在有界平面域上实现稀疏架构，实验显示在对称性无法由全局群作用实现时精度至少提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等变卷积网络通常假设一个群全局作用于信号空间。该假设对有界或分层域不合适：环境刚体运动可能只在域的一部分上可行，而边界会引入传递群作用无法捕捉的几何类型。

Method: 构建群胚等变神经网络理论，其中对称数据包括一个群胚、选定的局部双截断伪群、一个测度以及输入和输出表示丛。对对象空间上的积分通道，证明双截断等变核定理：等变性等价于两点核上的传输约束，其解由每对轨道上的一个联合稳定化子缠绕算子分类。以有界平面域为案例，架构通过离线零空间基和稀疏 gather--transform--scatter 操作实现。另用 Poisson--Dirichlet 核研究评估边界感知归纳偏置，并表明精确逆保持矩形的全局对称性，但不保持一般真局部双截断。

Result: 数值结果表明，当对称性无法由群作用全局实现时，所提架构具有显著优势；相对于所测试模型，精度至少提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作将等变网络从全局群作用推广到群胚与局部双截断框架，为有界或分层域提供可实现的边界感知等变架构，并通过核定理给出参数化与分类，实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: Equivariant convolutional neural networks are usually built from a group acting globally on the space of signals. This hypothesis is inappropriate for many bounded or stratified domains: an ambient rigid motion may be admissible only on part of the domain, and the boundary introduces geometric types that are invisible to a transitive group action. We develop a theory of groupoid-equivariant neural networks in which the symmetry datum consists of a groupoid, a selected pseudogroup of local bisections, a measure, and input and output representation bundles. For integral channels on the object space, we prove a bisection-equivariant kernel theorem: equivariance is equivalent to a transport constraint on the two-point kernel, and its solutions are classified by one joint-stabilizer intertwiner on each orbit of pairs. As a case study we apply the theory to bounded planar domains.
  The resulting architecture is implemented through offline nullspace bases and sparse gather--transform--scatter operations. A Poisson--Dirichlet kernel study is used separately to assess boundary-aware inductive bias; the exact inverse is shown to preserve the global symmetries of the rectangle but not general proper local bisections. The numerical results show that the proposed architectures provide significant advantages when symmetries cannot be globally implemented by group actions and provide an accuracy improvement of at least one order of magnitude with respect to the models tested.

</details>


### [350] [The Dynamics of Quasiregular Neural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26018)
*Matthia Sabatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在受控准规则回归任务中考察神经网络对“主导规律”与“系统性例外”的学习竞争，发现网络会先部分习得例外，随后回归主导规律（过度规则化），最后再恢复；例外越罕见，过度规则化越强，且该现象随规律类型不同而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习问题同时包含主导规律与系统性例外；语言习得中的U型学习现象提示，神经网络可能在习得例外后又暂时回归规律。作者希望在一个解已知、可控的设定中隔离并理解这种规律与例外之间的竞争机制。

Method: 构建受控的“准规则回归”（quasiregular regression）问题：其中规则解与例外解均被显式定义且已知。在此设定下训练神经网络，并系统改变例外的稀有程度与所考察的规律类型，追踪模型在训练过程中的学习动态。

Result: 神经网络能够部分习得例外，随后暂时回归主导规律，最终重新恢复对例外的学习，呈现U型动态。当例外较为罕见时，这种过度规则化（overregularization）显著增强，尽管例外在早期已被习得；此外，不同规律下的过度规则化程度并不相同。

Conclusion: 该工作以可控实验隔离出神经网络学习中规律与例外之间的一种简单竞争形式，为理解语言习得中的U型学习及更一般的规则—例外学习动态提供了机制性证据。

Abstract: Many learning problems combine a dominant regularity with systematic exceptions. Motivated by U-shaped learning in language acquisition, we study this interaction in controlled quasiregular regression problems where regular and exceptional solutions are explicitly known. Neural networks can partially acquire exceptions, subsequently regress toward the dominant regularity, and finally recover. This overregularization becomes substantially stronger when exceptions are rare, despite their early acquisition, but does not emerge equally across all regularities considered. Our results isolate a simple form of competition between regularities and exceptions during neural learning.

</details>


### [351] [BOBA: Dynamic Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26021)
*Merlin Angel Kelly,Rishan Patel,Alexander Thomas,Ziyue Zhu,Zikun Quan,Tom Carlson,Youngjun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于主动推断自由能原理的新采集函数BOBA，用于动态贝叶斯优化，显式量化未来状态预测不确定性，改善非平稳环境下的探索-利用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态黑箱优化中目标函数随时间演化、最优位置持续漂移，传统动态贝叶斯优化（DBO）方法（如UCB等标准采集函数）未显式考虑时间变化，导致样本分配次优、难以跟踪移动最优解。

Method: 提出BOBA（Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference），一种受主动推断自由能原理启发的采集函数；通过引入前瞻性不确定性量化，估计函数变化中的不确定性，从而在非平稳场景下实现更合理的探索-利用权衡。

Result: 在合成动态基准上与当前最优DBO方法对比：BOBA在查询受限设置下显著降低遗憾值（regret），在时间受限设置下保持竞争力；并分析不同探索策略的BOBA变体，展示探索-利用平衡可针对不同动态函数类型进行调节。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为动态贝叶斯优化提供了一种基于自由能的采集函数，并揭示主动推断原理如何增强非平稳环境优化，对需要持续适应的实时应用具有启示意义。

Abstract: Dynamic black-box optimization presents significant challenges for Bayesian Optimization (BO), as the objective function evolves over time, causing optimal locations to shift continuously. Existing dynamic BO (DBO) methods using standard acquisition functions such as Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) fail to explicitly account for temporal variations, leading to suboptimal sample allocation and poor tracking of moving optima. Here, we propose BOBA (Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference), a novel acquisition function inspired by free energy principles from active inference that explicitly minimizes predictive uncertainty about future states in dynamic environments. BOBA extends traditional acquisition functions by incorporating a forward-looking uncertainty quantification that estimates uncertainty in function changes, enabling more informed exploration-exploitation trade-offs in non-stationary settings. We evaluate BOBA on synthetic dynamic benchmarks, comparing against state-of-the-art DBO methods. Our experiments demonstrate that BOBA significantly improves regret in query-restricted settings, while remaining competitive in time-limited settings. We further analyze variants of BOBA with different exploration strategies, showing how the exploration-exploitation balance can be tuned for different types of dynamic functions. This work contributes both a free energy-based acquisition function for DBO and insights into how active inference principles can enhance optimization in non-stationary environments, with implications for real-time applications requiring continuous adaptation.

</details>


### [352] [MICRO: Multi-Fidelity Active Search for Severe Error Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26025)
*Orlando Leone,Niclas Pokel,Pehuén Moure,Yingqiang Gao,Roman Boehringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MICRO 是一种多保真主动搜索框架，通过联合建模廉价质量评分与昂贵标注损失，并利用影响聚类与 rollout 估计来分配共享预算，从而最大化已确认严重错误的发现数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工反馈在成本与信息量上存在差异：强反馈能揭示严重错误但代价高昂，而廉价的质量评分可用于判断哪些样本值得进一步标注。因此需要在有限预算下有效整合不同保真度的反馈，以提升严重错误发现效率。

Method: 提出 MICRO（Multi-Fidelity Impact Clustered Rollout）主动搜索框架：为不同反馈类型分配共享预算；以样本特征为条件联合建模质量评分与标注损失，用于指导采集；对采集候选按其对严重性概率的预测影响进行聚类，以选择多样化样本；再通过 rollout 估计候选样本的发现价值。

Result: 在 WMT20 英德任务上，质量评分有助于改进损失重建与严重性预测。MICRO 在四种预算与评分成本设置下取得最高平均发现数量；在其中一种设置下与适配的 MF-ENS 性能相近，而在其余三种设置下显著优于全部六种对比策略（包括两种 rollout 对照），且达到 p&lt;.001 显著性。

Conclusion: MICRO 表明，将廉价评分与昂贵标注统一纳入多保真主动搜索，并结合影响聚类与 rollout 价值估计，可在预算约束下更有效地发现严重错误，优于现有对比方法。

Abstract: Human feedback can vary in cost and informativeness. Strong feedback can reveal severe errors but is costly, so cheaper quality ratings can help decide which items to annotate. We propose MICRO (Multi-Fidelity Impact Clustered Rollout), an active search framework that allocates a shared budget to these feedback types to maximise confirmed severe error discoveries. MICRO jointly models ratings and annotation losses conditional on item features to steer acquisition. It clusters acquisitions by their predicted impact on severity probabilities to select diverse candidates, then uses rollout to estimate their discovery value. Experiments on WMT20 English-German show that ratings improve both loss reconstruction and severity prediction. MICRO achieves the highest mean discovery count across four budget and rating cost settings, with similar performance to adapted MF-ENS in one and significant gains over all six comparison policies, including two rollout controls, in the other three $(p<.001)$.

</details>


### [353] [xWhyL: Causal Interactive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26037)
*Nicholas Tagliapietra,Florian Peter Busch,Moritz Willig,Matej Zečević,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 xWhyL 形式框架，将解释转化为与观测数据互补的学习信号，从而从解释中学习因果模型；理论上证明在“因果拔河”冲突下可拒绝误设解释；具体实现 CIL 表明专家解释能高效支持因果发现并区分解释正误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释是因果推理的核心，认知科学已表明人类解释驱动力本身就是学习因果性的机制；但人工智能 largely 忽视了从这种溯因信号中学习。XAI 虽 increasingly 借助因果模型生成解释，但反向问题——解释能为因果性做什么——仍未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 xWhyL，一个连接因果性与 XAI 的形式框架，核心是从解释中学习因果模型。发展数学理论，将解释翻译为与观测数据互补的学习信号，并展示其如何克服观测因果发现的局限。针对解释可能源于错误信念并与数据冲突的“因果拔河”（Causal Tug-of-War）张力，证明框架拒绝误设解释而非吸收它们的条件。实际实例化为 Causal Interactive Learning（CIL），利用专家解释支持因果发现。

Result: 理论证明在特定条件下框架能拒绝错误解释；CIL 表明专家解释可高效支持因果发现，并区分正确与错误解释，同时使解释信号能够补充观测数据、突破观测因果发现的限制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从解释中学习因果模型的新方向，将解释作为因果发现的学习信号，桥接因果推理与 XAI，并为解释与数据冲突时的稳健学习提供理论条件。

Abstract: Explanations are central to causal reasoning, and cognitive science has long established that the human drive to explain is itself a mechanism for learning about causality. Despite this, learning from those abductive signals is largely ignored in artificial intelligence. While explainable AI (XAI) increasingly draws on causal models to generate explanations, the converse direction about what explanations can do for causality remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we propose xWhyL, a formal framework connecting causality and XAI by learning causal models from explanations. We develop a mathematical theory that translates explanations into a learning signal complementary to observational data, and demonstrate how it enables overcoming the limits of observational causal discovery. As explanations can be derived from incorrect beliefs and clash with data, a tension we call the Causal Tug-of-War, we prove conditions under which our framework rejects misspecified explanations rather than absorbing them. Our practical instantiation, Causal Interactive Learning (CIL), shows how expert explanations can efficiently support causal discovery and distinguish correct from incorrect explanations.

</details>


### [354] [Towards Adaptive Federated Graph Clustering: A Global Community-aware Contrastive Learning-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26063)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Wang Luo,Guocong Quan,Miao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AdaFGC：一种基于全局社区感知对比学习的自适应联邦图聚类框架，用于解决联邦图聚类中聚类数目预设不合理与社区间分离不充分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图学习可在不共享私有图数据的前提下协同训练模型，但现有方法多聚焦监督任务。现实图数据往往规模大且无标签，联邦图聚类因此重要但仍不成熟。其核心挑战在于客户端间子图异质性导致社区结构具有客户端特异性，现有方法存在两类局限：(1) 依赖不现实的预定义簇基数假设；(2) 社区间分离不完整。

Method: AdaFGC 的核心设计包括：1) 引入一组过完备的全局社区锚点，对全局社区结构建模，并通过跨客户端锚点精炼自适应估计聚类基数；2) 采用全局社区感知对比学习方案，将共享锚点作为对比原型，在客户端间显式强化社区级吸引与排斥；3) 辅以节点级与拓扑级目标，稳定本地表示学习。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AdaFGC 在多项聚类指标上持续优于现有的监督与无监督联邦图学习基线。

Conclusion: AdaFGC 通过全局社区锚点、自适应基数估计与社区感知对比学习，有效缓解了子图异质性带来的聚类难题，为无标签分布式图数据的联邦聚类提供了可行且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train graph models without sharing their private graph data, providing a promising paradigm for mining knowledge from distributed graph repositories. While most existing FGL methods focus on supervised tasks, real-world graphs are often massive and unlabeled, making federated graph clustering an important yet still immature research direction. Notably, this task is particularly challenging due to the inherent subgraph heterogeneity across clients, which leads to client-specific community structures. In this work, we identify two critical limitations in existing federated graph clustering methods: (1) unrealistic pre-defined cluster cardinality assumptions and (2) incomplete inter-community separation. To address these challenges, we propose AdaFGC, an Adaptive Federated graph clustering framework based on Global community-aware Contrastive learning. AdaFGC introduces an over-complete set of global community anchors to model the global community structure and adaptively estimate clustering cardinality via cross-client anchor refinement. In addition, it employs a global community-aware contrastive learning scheme that uses the shared anchors as contrastive prototypes to explicitly enforce community-level attraction and repulsion across clients, complemented by node-level and topology-level objectives that stabilize local representations. Extensive experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that AdaFGC consistently outperforms existing supervised and unsupervised FGL baselines across multiple clustering metrics.

</details>


### [355] [FuncCode: Compressing Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks in Function Space with Hardware-Aware Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26067)
*Kazi Ahmed Asif Fuad,Lizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuncCode 提出一种与基函数无关的 KAN 压缩方法：对采样后的边响应构建共享码本，分别编码 basis 分支与 base 分支，并以量化、位打包格式导出码本与逐边索引，在 MNIST/CIFAR 与 FPGA 加速器上实现显著压缩且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN 用可学习的一元函数替代标量边权，提升表达能力，但每条边需存储多个系数（常另加 base 分支），导致参数内存开销大，制约部署。作者观察到采样后的边响应比系数表示具有更低的有效秩（低 13–35%），说明函数空间存在可利用的共享冗余。

Method: FuncCode 为 basis-agnostic 压缩框架：对采样的边响应构建共享码本；对 basis 分支与 base 分支独立编码（分别聚类/码本化）；以量化与位打包方式导出码本及逐边索引。通过复制实验对照函数空间聚类与系数空间聚类，验证收益来源。

Result: 采样边响应的有效秩比系数表示低 13–35%；函数空间聚类与系数空间聚类统计上持平，稳定增益来自保留两分支各自的共享结构。十随机种子 MNIST 上，Spline 与 GRAM KAN 分别压缩 31.6× 和 17.6×，精度仅降 0.31/0.34 pp；6.1M 边卷积 KAGN 压缩 19.9×，CIFAR-10/100 分别距稠密精度 0.54/1.89 pp。压缩后逐边索引占存储权重位高达 99.4%，表示呈 index-bound。在 9 个 bit-exact FPGA 加速器上，相对稠密 INT4 将 SplineKAN 布局后权重内存降低 3.87×，且不增加周期数或延迟。

Conclusion: FuncCode 表明函数空间冗余可被有效利用，且分别编码 basis/base 分支是关键；该方法在精度损失极小的前提下大幅压缩 KAN 参数内存，并对硬件部署友好（不牺牲时延），代码已开源。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace scalar edge weights with learnable univariate functions, increasing flexibility but also parameter memory because each edge stores multiple coefficients, often together with a separate base branch. We introduce FuncCode, a basis-agnostic compression approach that forms shared codebooks from sampled edge responses, codes the basis and base branches independently, and exports the resulting codebooks and per-edge indices in a quantized, bit-packed format. Across spline and polynomial KANs, sampled edge responses exhibit $13$--$35\%$ lower effective rank than their coefficient representations. Further replicated controls show that function-space clustering alone is statistically tied with coefficient-space clustering; the consistent accuracy gain comes from preserving the distinct sharing structure of the two branches. On a ten-seed MNIST benchmark, FuncCode compresses spline and GRAM KANs by $31.6\times$ and $17.6\times$ with only $0.31$ and $0.34$ pp accuracy loss. On a 6.1M-edge convolutional KAGN, it achieves $19.9\times$ compression while remaining within $0.54$ pp of dense accuracy on CIFAR-10 and $1.89$ pp on CIFAR-100. After compression, per-edge indices account for up to $99.4\%$ of stored weight bits, making the representation index-bound. Across nine bit-exact FPGA accelerators, FuncCode reduces SplineKAN post-route weight memory by $3.87\times$ relative to dense INT4, without increasing cycle count or latency. The FuncCode implementation is available at https://github.com/OSU-STARLAB/FuncCode.

</details>


### [356] [Fast Matrix Multiplication in fp8: Certified Coefficient Optimization and Measured Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26077)
*Shuxiao Xie,Shuyang Xie,Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将Strassen型算法的不同实现视为变基轨道上的设计问题，引入系数泛函Φ并证明其全局最优值，从而在fp8低精度下预测并选择误差更低的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一精确乘积与相同乘法次数的Strassen型算法可有多种实现，但变基会重塑系数几何，导致fp8误差不同，因此需要回答“应运行哪种实现”。既有稳定性分析只控制最坏情况ℓ1增长，不能刻画期望误差幅度；Dumas–Pernet–Sedoglavic优化器也仅能称为“可能最优”，缺乏全局最优性证明。

Method: 为每种实现附加系数泛函Φ，作为系数几何的标量摘要，并在变基轨道上最小化Φ。将该问题表述为Hadamard流形上的Kempf–Ness问题，通过矩映射零点精确认证全局Φ最优；利用de Groote分类将最优性扩展到所有精确实秩-7的2×2分解。进一步引入块缩放e4m3模型，其中Φ是相对期望均方误差的首阶系数，并用实验检验Φ预测的排序与真实fp8误差是否一致。

Result: 精确矩映射零点给出Φ_min=200/9；所有精确实秩-7实现，在固定噪声系数下，其Φ预测RMS常数至少是三次算法的5/3倍。排序与重变基实验支持该预测，在测试的融合块缩放机制内成立。来自四类架构的真实matmul tile上，Φ最优实现落入fp8低误差区域。两个约70B模型在真实deep_gemm内核上，同一实现消除了经典Strassen相对干净模型超额NLL的10%–55%。

Conclusion: 算法实现选择由此从调参问题转变为数学可认证的设计问题：低精度成为一个独立轴，具有全局Φ最优值，并已测得与fp8误差的相关性。

Abstract: A Strassen-type algorithm has many realizations with the same exact product and multiplication count yet different fp8 error because basis changes reshape coefficient geometry, posing the question of which to run. No current account settles this: classical stability controls worst-case $\ell_1$ growth, not the expected-error magnitude, and the Dumas--Pernet--Sedoglavic optimizer could only be called probably optimal, its global optimality unproved. To settle this, we attach to each realization a coefficient functional $Φ$, a scalar summary of its coefficient geometry, which we minimize over the change-of-basis orbit. This Kempf--Ness problem on a Hadamard manifold lets us certify the global $Φ$ optimum rather than merely search for it: an exact moment-map zero fixes $Φ_{\min} = 200/9$, and de Groote's classification extends that optimality to every exact real rank-7 $2\times2$ decomposition. Every exact real rank-7 realization therefore has a $Φ$-predicted RMS constant at least $5/3$ times that of the cubic algorithm, at fixed noise coefficient. We then introduce an explicit block-scaled e4m3 model in which $Φ$ is the leading-order coefficient of relative expected mean-squared error, and we test the resulting $Φ$-predicted ordering against realized fp8 error. Ordering and re-basing experiments support that prediction within tested fused block-scaled regimes, and on real matmul tiles from four architecture families the $Φ$-optimal realization falls in the fp8 low-error region. Across two $\sim$70B models on real deep_gemm kernels, the same realization removes 10 to 55% of classic Strassen's excess NLL over the clean model. Algorithm realization thus becomes a mathematically certified design problem rather than a tuning choice: an independent low-precision axis with a global $Φ$ optimum and measured fp8 relevance.

</details>


### [357] [Margin-Drop Coordinates for Cross-Budget Robustness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26081)
*Yanliang Huang,Zhen Zhang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Sitong Zhu,Zhuoqi Zeng,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 固定预算鲁棒性评估可能选错冻结视觉编码器；浅层攻击存活率几乎不能预测更深预算下的崩溃，而浅层“漂移”坐标可高秩相关地预测崩溃，并能分解修复路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算鲁棒性评估可能选出在浅层攻击下表现好、但在相同评估加强后迅速失效的编码器；作者希望判断浅层评估中是否已包含足够信息来识别这种“预算脆弱性”。

Method: 对每个干净正确样本记录：干净成对间隔、一阶线性化间隔下降尺度、从干净起点单步攻击的间隔下降、迭代攻击达到的间隔下降。按该尺度归一化后得到三个间隔下降坐标：干净间隔余量、单步不足、漂移（迭代攻击超出单步扰动的额外归一化间隔下降）。三者可重构归一化攻击后间隔，从而决定通过/失败。

Result: 在42个预训练冻结视觉编码器上，浅层存活率与后续PGD-10到PGD-200崩溃几乎无秩信息（Spearman -0.006）；浅层漂移坐标中位数对同一崩溃的秩相关为+0.811。该结果在留出编码器池和ℓ∞评估下仍成立。在仅对11个编码器做深度评估时，按浅层漂移排序可召回17个高崩溃编码器中的11个，而按存活率排序仅召回5个。完整坐标分解还能区分固定预算残差相同但更深预算下分化的案例，并在干预下分离“间隔修复”与“漂移修复”，揭示端点鲁棒性无法识别的不同修复路径。

Conclusion: 浅层评估的存活率不足以识别预算脆弱性；浅层漂移坐标是预测深度崩溃的有效指标，且完整间隔下降坐标分解可解释固定预算残余与深度预算分化，并区分不同鲁棒性修复机制。

Abstract: Fixed-budget robustness evaluation can select the wrong frozen vision encoder. An encoder that survives a shallow attack may lose most of that robustness when the same evaluation is strengthened. We ask whether the shallow evaluation contains enough information to identify this budget fragility. For each clean-correct sample, the evaluation records the clean pairwise margin, the first-order linearized margin-drop scale, the margin drop from a clean-start one-step attack, and the drop reached by an iterative attack. Normalizing by that scale gives three margin-drop coordinates capturing clean margin slack, one-step shortfall, and drift, where drift is the additional normalized margin drop the iterative attack reaches beyond the one-step perturbation. Together, they reconstruct the normalized post-attack margin and therefore the pass-or-fail outcome. Across 42 pretrained frozen vision encoders, the shallow survival rate carries essentially no rank information about subsequent PGD-10 to PGD-200 collapse, at Spearman -0.006, while the median shallow drift coordinate ranks the same collapse at +0.811. The result persists in a held-out encoder pool and under an $\ell_\infty$ evaluation. With deep evaluation limited to 11 encoders, ranking by shallow drift recovers 11 of the 17 high-collapse encoders, compared with 5 under survival-rate ranking. The full coordinate decomposition further distinguishes cases that share the same fixed-budget residual but diverge at deeper budgets, and separates margin repair from drift repair under interventions, revealing distinct repair paths that endpoint robustness alone does not identify.

</details>


### [358] [CoEvo: Oracle-Grounded Self-Evolution of a Single Model for Multi-Step Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26094)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoEvo：一种基于确定性预言机的自进化框架，让单一模型交替扮演 Proposer 与 Solver，通过组内辩论定位分歧步骤并由预言机裁决生成过程级监督，同时构造渐难课程，实现多步因果推理的自进化。8B 模型路径正确率达 82.1%，超过 71.4% 的最强专有参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步因果推理需逐步链式约束，早期错误会静默传播，且错误逻辑得到正确答案可逃过结果级检测。专业领域中教师 LLM 中间步骤易错、安全约束限制云端蒸馏、条件变化要求适应，因此自进化成为可行路径。但朴素自进化会崩溃：仅用结果奖励会利用分布捷径，弱自评会把虚假路径强化为稳定失败模式。

Method: 利用“生成正确链难、验证单步易”的不对称性，在高风险领域引入确定性、可查询的预言机（如物理模拟器或规则引擎），仅校验模型断言步骤，超出规则则弃权。CoEvo 中单一模型交替作为 Solver 与 Proposer：Solver 生成竞争链，组内辩论暴露分歧步骤（能力边界代理），预言机裁决为过程级监督；Proposer 在预言机约束内构造渐难场景，将课程导向深度多跳链。两角色联合更新，使训练压力与模型共同进化。

Result: 在工业、临床、法律多步因果推理基准上，CoEvo 使 8B LLM 持续自进化，路径正确率 82.1%，超过蒸馏基线和最强专有参考 71.4%；并能泛化到未见类别与系统，同时保持根因准确率。

Conclusion: CoEvo 表明，借助领域内确定性预言机提供过程级监督，可避免朴素自进化的崩溃，实现无需教师蒸馏的可扩展自进化，并提升多步因果推理的路径正确性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-step causal reasoning requires chaining inferences where each step constrains the next. An early error propagates silently, and a correct answer reached via flawed logic evades outcome-level detection. In specialized domains, teacher LLMs err on intermediate steps, safety constraints restrict cloud distillation, and shifting conditions demand adaptation, leaving self-evolution as the practical route. Naive self-evolution can collapse: outcome-only rewards let the model exploit distributional shortcuts, and weak self-evaluation reinforces spurious paths into stable failure patterns. We exploit a key asymmetry: generating a correct chain is hard, but verifying a single step is easy. Many high-stakes domains admit a deterministic, queryable oracle, a physics simulator or rule engine over codified constraints. It checks asserted steps without teacher-level ability and abstains beyond its rules; it can check what the model asserts, never replace it. This enables CoEvo, an oracle-grounded self-evolution framework where a single model alternates between Proposer and Solver. As Solver, the model generates competing chains; intra-group debate exposes disagreement steps, a proxy for the capability boundary, and the oracle adjudicates them into process-level supervision. As Proposer, the same model constructs progressively harder scenarios inside oracle constraints, steering the curriculum toward deep multi-hop chains. Both roles are updated jointly, so training pressure co-evolves with the model. On industrial, clinical, and legal multi-step causal reasoning benchmarks, CoEvo enables an 8B LLM to sustain self-evolution, surpassing distillation baselines and the strongest proprietary reference on path correctness (82.1% vs. 71.4%). The trained model generalizes to unseen categories and systems, preserving root-cause accuracy.

</details>


### [359] [Certified Mechanistic Interpretability: Lifting Single-Input Findings to Bounded Neighbourhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26112)
*Zhen Zhang,Yanliang Huang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于约束多项式-zonotope（CPZ）传播的框架，将机械可解释性中逐输入的注意力机制观察，提升为有界扰动集合上的可认证陈述；针对三个内部注意力查询构造可解程序，并在Transformer块传播中精确保留softmax单纯形与LayerNorm零均值恒等式，进而以递归Jacobian zonotope将证书扩展至多层深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性通常以单个输入为单位逆向工程Transformer电路，所得机制解释仅适用于该输入，缺乏在有界输入邻域上的保证。作者旨在弥补这一缺口，使机制性观察具备可认证的覆盖范围，并支持在经验启发式可能失效的场景中辅助下游决策。

Method: 核心为约束多项式-zonotope（CPZ）传播：将三个内部注意力查询——top-k稳定性、证据质量（evidence mass）与注意力熵——形式化为注意力权重单纯形上的可处理优化程序；证明CPZ在Transformer块中的传播能够精确保留softmax单纯形与LayerNorm零均值恒等式；进一步构造递归Jacobian zonotope，通过在输入端对块堆栈线性化，将同一类证书扩展到层深维度，并避免逐层生成元数量增长。框架在Transformer注意力上实例化。

Result: 所生成的证书能够强化单输入检查无法分辨的机制性陈述，并为经验启发式可能产生误导的情形提供下游决策依据。摘要未报告具体数据集、基线或定量指标，主要贡献为理论框架与可认证性保证。

Conclusion: 该工作将机械可解释性从单输入观察推进到有界扰动邻域上的认证分析，为Transformer注意力机制提供了可跨层扩展、保持结构不变量的形式化证书框架。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability reverse-engineers transformer circuits one input at a time, leaving observed mechanisms without guarantees over bounded input neighbourhoods. We address this gap with a framework based on constrained polynomial-zonotope (CPZ) propagation that lifts mechanistic-interpretability observations from a single input to certified statements over a bounded set of perturbations. Three internal-attention queries (top-$k$ stability, evidence mass, and attention entropy) are formulated as tractable programs over the simplex of attention weights, and CPZ propagation through transformer blocks is shown to preserve the softmax simplex and the LayerNorm zero-mean identity exactly. A recursive Jacobian zonotope construction extends the same certificates across layer depth by linearising the block stack at the input and avoids per-layer generator growth. We instantiate the framework on transformer attention; the resulting certificates offer a way to sharpen mechanistic statements that single-input inspection cannot resolve on its own, and to inform downstream decisions in regimes where empirical heuristics may be misleading.

</details>


### [360] [Bridging the Data Gap: Digital Twin as a New Paradigm for AI-based Radio Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26214)
*Éloi Sainte-Beuve,Guillaume Larue,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Quentin Lampin,Ali Al Khansa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以3D数字孪生（DT）作为环境世界模型，驱动射线追踪生成大规模合成信道冲激响应（CIR）序列，并训练序列神经网络实现无设备定位（DFL）；主张将无线感知建模为“环境条件化”的本地模型学习问题，并给出基于状态空间模型的初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知规模化面临真实标注数据稀缺、场景多样且站点特异的问题；单一全局模型难以适应不同环境。作者希望利用数字孪生可配置、可扩展的合成数据能力，降低对仪器化目标和真实采集的依赖。

Method: 以环境3D数字孪生为核心：提供几何、材质及收发机位置，输入射线追踪引擎，生成大量合理场景（人员/物体移动、布局变体、季节与天气条件等）下的时间索引CIR；再由这些合成序列训练序列神经网络，将CIR时间序列映射为空间占用估计，实现无设备定位。方法上强调“环境条件化学习”：不追求单一全局模型，而是针对站点特定DT训练或微调局部模型。首个实验引入一种新的状态空间模型（SSM）架构，并在多个房间几何中训练与评估。

Result: 在多个房间几何条件下完成训练与评估，定位性能表明该数字孪生驱动的环境条件化学习与序列建模方案具有可行性与潜力。

Conclusion: 数字孪生可作为规模化无线感知的关键使能器，通过合成CIR数据与站点特化模型实现无设备定位；未来可拓展到更多环境条件与场景变体，并验证跨站点泛化与部署效果。

Abstract: We present a methodology that places a 3D digital twin (DT) of the environment as the main enabler behind the development of radio sensing at scale. The DT acts as a world model, providing geometry, materials, and transmitter/receiver placements to a ray-tracing engine that generates time-indexed channel impulse responses (CIRs) for large numbers of plausible scenes (moving people and objects, layout variants, seasonal/weather conditions, etc). From these synthetic sequences, we train a sequential neural network that maps CIR time series to spatial occupancy estimates, enabling device-free localization (DFL) without instrumented targets. We posit that sensing is best approached as an environment-conditioned learning problem: rather than seeking a single global model, we advocate training or fine-tuning local models specialized to a site-specific DT. As a first experiment, we introduce a novel State Space Model architecture, trained and evaluated across multiple room geometries. The localization performances obtained demonstrate the potential of the approach.

</details>


### [361] [Beyond Imitation: Auditing the Recoverability of Reasoning in Distilled Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26216)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Han Wang,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用“前缀恢复”诊断教师解与学生的推理兼容性，并将其与交叉熵/反向KL梯度冲突联系起来，发现反向KL蒸馏在小于2B的学生上收益最稳定，且三种测量共同指向容量依赖的迁移区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正确的教师解只有在学生能够继续其推理时才是有效监督；分布级蒸馏代价高，需要一种实用诊断来预判学生能从教师解中恢复多少推理，以及何时应选择反向KL等分布匹配目标。

Method: 以“前缀恢复”为核心指标：揭示已验证解的25%、50%、75%后，测试学生能否正确完成剩余推理。进一步在全词表上计算交叉熵与反向KL梯度的余弦冲突，并考察0.6B至8B的相邻Qwen3师生对；在固定1000条轨迹队列上比较不同学生规模，并通过独立目标干预验证反向KL的救援效果。

Result: 反向KL蒸馏在小于2B的两个学生上对数学与代码任务提升最一致。学生规模从0.6B增至4B时，平均前缀恢复率由71.0%升至91.9%，稳健—脆弱恢复差距由46.0个百分点收窄至14.4个百分点；教师固定为8B时，冲突分离度由0.993降至0.233。目标干预显示反向KL对脆弱轨迹的救援最大。

Conclusion: 前缀恢复、梯度冲突与目标干预三种测量定位到同一容量依赖迁移机制：当正确推理轨迹的恢复性不均衡时，分布匹配的改进空间最大。前缀恢复可作为选择高成本分布级蒸馏的实用诊断工具。

Abstract: A correct teacher solution becomes useful supervision when the receiving student can continue its reasoning. We measure this compatibility with prefix recovery: after revealing 25%, 50%, or 75% of a verified solution, we test whether the student completes it correctly. We connect recovery to the cosine conflict between cross-entropy and reverse-KL gradients over the full vocabulary. Across adjacent Qwen3 teacher-student pairs from 0.6B to 8B parameters, reverse-KL distillation delivers its most consistent mathematical and code improvements for the two students below 2B parameters. On a fixed cohort of 1,000 trajectories, average prefix recovery rises from 71.0% to 91.9% as student size increases from 0.6B to 4B, and the robust-fragile recovery gap contracts from 46.0 to 14.4 percentage points. With the teacher fixed at 8B, conflict separation falls from 0.993 to 0.233. An independent objective intervention finds the largest reverse-KL rescue on fragile trajectories. The three measurements locate the same capacity-dependent transfer regime: distribution matching has the greatest headroom when correct traces remain unevenly recoverable. Prefix recovery provides a practical diagnostic for selecting costly distribution-level distillation.

</details>


### [362] [MSA-CITE: A Co-Adapted LoRA Specialist Ecology for Fixed-Budget Small-Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26217)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出 MSA-CITE：将同一 Qwen3-4B 骨干上四条被弃置的冻结 LoRA 分支视为可组合的部署资产，在固定四代生成预算下每条分支采样一次，通过等价类分组、校准先验打分与确定性平局规则读出答案，在数学任务上优于最强单分支基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型语言模型通常只保留单一后训练检查点并反复采样，浪费了后训练过程中产生的多个检查点。作者质疑这一惯例，主张将多个被丢弃的检查点视为可组合的部署资产，以在几乎不增加额外机制的前提下挖掘其集体价值。

Method: 以单一 Qwen3-4B 为骨干，保留四条来自不同后训练轨迹的冻结 LoRA 分支。部署时不再从单一分支采样四次，而是将固定的四代生成预算分配给四条分支各生成一次补全。读出阶段（MSA-CITE）将各分支的终态答案归并为等价类，用基于校准得到的来源先验求和为每个等价类打分，并在确定性平局规则下选出代表答案。读出阶段不利用评测结果进行学习，也不引入额外生成、验证器或重排序步骤。

Result: 在 200 条留出数学题上，四路径组合取得 65.5% 准确率，最强单分支基线为 62.0%；在 100 条主题不相交的分布偏移集上为 42.0% 对 40.0%。在同分布条件下，相较同质 SFT 与 Online-OPD 重复采样的提升较为稳健；但相对最强基线的结果及分布偏移条件下的结果尚不具结论性。

Conclusion: 即便未进行协同训练，多个后训练分支也能在部署中产生集体收益。该结论虽范围有限，但提供了一个具体且可操作的发现：多路径分支组合可作为紧凑模型部署的一种有效补充策略。

Abstract: Compact language models are typically deployed by retaining a single post-training checkpoint and sampling it repeatedly. In this work, we challenge this practice by treating multiple discarded checkpoints as composable assets for deployment. Starting from a single Qwen3-4B backbone, we preserve four frozen LoRA branches, each derived from a different post-training trajectory. stead of drawing four generations from one branch, we allocate a fixed four-generation budget by sampling one completion from each branch. Our method, Multi-path Specialist Adaptation with Calibrated Inference-Time Evidence (MSA-CITE), processes the resulting portfolio by grouping terminal answers into equivalence classes, scoring each class via summed calibration-derived source priors, and selecting a representative under deterministic tie-breaking rules. The readout stage does not learn from evaluation results, nor does it introduce additional generations, verifiers, or reranking steps. On 200 held-out mathematics items, the four-path portfolio achieves 65.5% accuracy, compared with 62.0% for the strongest single-branch baseline. On a 100-item subject-disjoint shift, it attains 42.0% versus 40.0%. Under in-distribution conditions, the improvements over homogeneous SFT and Online-OPD repetition are robust; results against the strongest baseline and under shifted conditions are not conclusive. Our findings offer a narrow but concrete contribution: post-training branches, even without co-training, can be collectively beneficial for deployment.

</details>


### [363] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Modeling for Continuous and Subtle GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26231)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自动驾驶车辆中的连续细微GNSS欺骗检测建模为因果序列证据建模问题，提出高阶液态证据检测器（HO-LLN），通过将GNSS位移与车载运动观测的残差转化为不确定性归一化证据，并利用液态动力学估计因果欺骗概率，在AV-GPS数据集上取得AUROC 0.9932、AUPRC 0.9843的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续且细微的GNSS欺骗对自动驾驶车辆构成严重威胁：伪造位置可能在局部仍显得合理，却会逐渐与车载非GNSS传感器观测到的运动状态不一致。现有面向自动驾驶的检测器多依赖残差阈值或特征级分类，对弱GNSS—运动不一致性随时间如何发展并持续这一过程缺乏建模能力，因此难以有效捕捉此类隐蔽欺骗。

Method: 作者将细微GNSS欺骗检测形式化为因果序列证据建模问题，提出高阶液态证据检测器。方法步骤如下：1）比较连续GNSS位置隐含的位移与由独立车载运动观测推断的位移，将其差异转化为不确定性归一化残差证据；2）将当前不一致性、其局部演化、超出正常水平的超额量、累积持续性以及位移有效性表示为因果弱证据；3）把这些线索映射到瞬时、演化与持续三类潜在状态，经有界Kirchhoff式对称交换对齐，并通过显式三阶交互整合，以捕捉其对欺骗的协同支撑；4）采用二阶液态动力学追踪该协同证据的记忆与演化，估计因果欺骗概率，并借助验证集选取的阈值与持续性参数转化为确认告警。

Result: 在AV-GPS数据集族上的实验表明，该方法具有强大的受控泛化与外部泛化能力，并呈现清晰的序列化告警行为。在Dataset-1上，所提检测器取得AUROC 0.9932、AUPRC 0.9843，同时在所有基于学习的基线中实现最低的假阳性率。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了将细微GNSS欺骗检测转化为因果序列证据建模问题的有效性，所提高阶液态证据检测器在精度、泛化性与低误报方面均具优势，代码已开源（https://github.com/pangjunbiao/HO-LLN-Spoofing）。

Abstract: Continuous and subtle GNSS spoofing poses a serious threat to autonomous vehicles because forged positions may remain locally plausible while gradually becoming inconsistent with vehicle motion observed by non-GNSS onboard sensors. Existing AV-oriented detectors commonly rely on residual thresholds or feature-level classification and provide limited modeling of how weak GNSS--motion inconsistency develops and persists over time. This paper formulates subtle GNSS spoofing detection as a causal sequential evidence-modeling problem and proposes a high-order liquid evidence detector. The method first compares the displacement implied by consecutive GNSS positions with that inferred from independent onboard motion observations and converts their difference into uncertainty-normalized residual evidence. It then represents the current inconsistency, its local evolution, excess above the normal level, accumulated persistence, and displacement validity as causal weak evidence. These cues are mapped into instantaneous, evolutionary, and persistent latent states, aligned through a bounded Kirchhoff-inspired symmetric exchange, and combined through an explicit third-order interaction to capture their coordinated support for spoofing. To model how this coordinated evidence develops over time, second-order liquid dynamics track its memory and evolution to estimate causal spoofing probabilities, which are converted into confirmed alarms using validation-selected threshold and persistence parameters. Experiments on the AV--GPS dataset family demonstrate strong controlled and external generalization, together with clear sequential alarm behavior. On Dataset-1, the proposed detector achieves an AUROC of 0.9932 and an AUPRC of 0.9843, while obtaining the lowest false-positive rate among the learning-based baselines. Code: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/HO-LLN-Spoofing.

</details>


### [364] [Can You Delete a Year of Market Data? Machine Unlearning Against Exact Retraining Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26242)
*Junyi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在S&P 500波动率面板上系统基准测试时间序列机器遗忘：删除数据许可到期记录必须移除已训练预测器中的影响，应以“未见过该时段的 oracle”而非删除期低预测精度为目标，并需明确删除范围、按架构与窗口做 oracle 验证及功效感知审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据许可到期后，仅删除存储记录并不能消除模型已编码的影响；机器遗忘希望在不重训的前提下移除这种影响，但金融时序场景中缺乏可对照的基准与评估标准。

Method: 构建3,200组配对基准：参照模型用全量数据训练，oracle 则在不包含目标时段的数据上重训。网格覆盖S&P 500波动率面板上的5种架构、4个滚动折、5个可删除年份、3种实验性删除强度；比较近似遗忘方法与oracle，并分析审计分离、窗口级损失比较及独立性假设。

Result: 2020年危机年在所有架构上产生最大记忆差距；删除该年使3个可部署模型在每个折均改善，2022年熊市中改善最大，而2个不可部署模型表现不一致。在某个Transformer格子中，未训练2020年的oracle仍能以IC=0.51预测2020年，参照模型为0.55；把预测推向噪声会降低测试技能。12个可部署“架构-方法”对中，仅TSMixer配合hinge方法在所有折接近oracle，弥合74%–118%的参照到oracle差距且无可测测试技能损失。方法排名随架构和滚动窗口变化；审计分离随先前记忆上升，但精确删除后仍可能很小；窗口级损失比较最高仅0.69，把股票级窗口视为独立会使绝对t统计量中位数膨胀1.9倍。

Conclusion: 时间序列机器遗忘应以oracle为验证目标，而非追求删除期低精度；评估需明确删除范围，针对相关架构与滚动窗口进行oracle验证，并采用功效感知的审计，避免因窗口独立性假设导致显著性被高估。

Abstract: When a data license expires, deleting stored records does not remove influence encoded in a trained forecaster. Machine unlearning seeks to remove this influence without retraining. We benchmark temporal unlearning with 3,200 paired references trained on all data and oracles retrained without the requested period. The grid covers five architectures, four rolling folds, five deletable years, and three experimental deletion levels on an S&P 500 volatility panel. The 2020 COVID crisis year produces the largest memorization gap for every architecture. Removing it improves all three deployable models in every fold, with the largest improvement in the 2022 bear market, while the two non-deployable models respond inconsistently. The target for approximate unlearning is the oracle, not low predictive accuracy on the deleted period. In one Transformer cell, an oracle that never trained on 2020 still predicts it at an information coefficient of 0.51, compared with 0.55 for the reference; pushing predictions toward noise reduces test skill. Across twelve deployable architecture-method pairs, only TSMixer with the hinge method remains near the oracle in every fold, closing 74-118% of the reference-to-oracle gap without a measurable loss of test skill. Method rankings vary across architectures and rolling windows. Audit separation rises with prior memorization but can remain small after exact deletion. The window-level loss comparison reaches at most 0.69, and treating stock-level windows as independent inflates the absolute t-statistic by a median factor of 1.9. These results call for an explicit deletion scope, oracle validation for the relevant architecture and window, and power-aware auditing.

</details>


### [365] [Information-Theoretic Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26257)
*Yunfei Zhang,Wen Wen,Tieliang Gong,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPT4CL，将CLIP文本提示解耦为任务共享提示分布与类别特定提示，缓解检索依赖与分类器偏置，并给出信息论泛化界，在标准持续学习基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需在序列数据上增量获取知识且避免灾难性遗忘；提示微调是高效适配预训练模型的方式，但现有提示设计普遍存在检索依赖（对提示选择敏感）和分类器偏置（预测偏向新到达类别）两大问题。

Method: DPT4CL将CLIP文本提示解耦为两部分：1）任务共享提示分布，通过优化信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）目标得到，促进跨任务知识迁移并缓解分类器偏置；2）类别特定提示，在不依赖显式提示检索的前提下增强类间可分性。同时从信息论角度建立统一的超额风险界，为框架的鲁棒泛化与遗忘缓解提供理论支撑。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准上进行大量实验，DPT4CL取得state-of-the-art性能；理论分析给出了支持鲁棒泛化与遗忘缓解的超额风险界；源代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过解耦提示设计并结合信息瓶颈优化与信息论泛化界，DPT4CL有效克服了提示微调在持续学习中的检索依赖与分类器偏置问题，实现了更稳健的持续学习性能。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to incrementally acquire knowledge from sequential data while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. Recently, prompt tuning has attracted increasing attention as an efficient approach for adapting pre-trained models to CL tasks. However, existing prompt design paradigms commonly suffer from retrieval dependence and classifier bias, which make model adaptation sensitive to prompt selection and bias predictions toward newly arrived classes. To address these challenges, we propose Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning (DPT4CL), which decouples the CLIP textual prompt into a task-shared prompt distribution and class-specific prompts. The task-shared prompt distribution is derived by optimizing an Information Bottleneck objective to facilitate cross-task knowledge transfer and alleviate classifier bias, while class-specific prompts enhance inter-class separability without relying on explicit prompt retrieval. Furthermore, we establish a unified excess risk bound from an information-theoretic perspective, providing theoretical support for the robust generalization and forgetting mitigation of the proposed framework. Extensive experiments on standard CL benchmarks demonstrate that DPT4CL achieves state-of-the-art performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/Cloudfly-Z/DPT4CL

</details>


### [366] [Mode Collapse Is Cheap to Detect: A Ground-Truth-Free Pre-Flight Check for Neural Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26272)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"预检"诊断方法，能在训练早期廉价地检测神经采样器是否漏掉了目标分布的部分质量（missing mass），只依赖 $E$、$\nabla E$、$\nabla^2 E$，且不依赖模型自身采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经采样器针对未归一化目标 $\tilde\pi=e^{-E}$ 训练，且没有来自 $\pi$ 的真实样本，因此实践者无法判断一次昂贵的训练是否"悄悄漏掉"了部分目标质量。常用的诊断指标均由模型自身采样计算，因而被限制在模型支撑集内——论文展示了一个采样器自归一化 ESS 高达 0.99，却漏掉了 87% 的目标质量。核心动机是：检测（detecting）漏失质量比采样（sampling）它严格更简单。

Method: 将问题重构为"检测"而非"修正"：检测每个被漏掉的盆地只需一个点加一个局部曲率估计，而修正则需要重新训练采样器。据此设计一个预检（pre-flight check），仅消耗采样器自身训练预算的百分之几，且只使用能量 $E$、梯度 $\nabla E$ 与海森 $\nabla^2 E$，无需真实样本或可解密度。

Result: 在具有可精确计算真值的高斯混合、Many-Well 及旋转各向异性 Many-Well 目标上，该方法以 2.7% 的训练成本将漏失质量估计到 $10^{-3}$ 以内；而调优后的退火 SMC 基线需耗费 70%–280% 训练成本却表现更差。该方法同样适用于无可解密度的 controlled-SDE 采样器（此时 ESS 与 ELBO 均无法计算）。其自诊断（self-diagnostic）在无真值时偏向保守的安全方向：60 组配置中通过 16 组，其中 15 组精度达 $10^{-2}$ 或更好。

Conclusion: 作者明确界定了方法的适用边界：该预检能廉价地产生"存在漏失质量"的证据，有时也能产生"搜索已稳定"的证据，但无法对一次运行做认证（certify），且其阈值是启发式的。最后在真实物理能量景观 LJ-13 上刻画方法边界，报告其失效位置与原因。

Abstract: Neural samplers are trained against an unnormalised target $\tildeπ=e^{-E}$ with no samples from $π$, which leaves the practitioner with no way to tell whether an expensive training run has silently dropped part of the target. The diagnostics in common use are computed from the model's own draws and are therefore confined to the model's support: we exhibit a sampler whose self-normalised effective sample size is $0.99$ while it misses $87\%$ of the target mass. We argue that \emph{detecting} missing mass is a strictly easier problem than sampling it: detection needs one point per missed basin plus a local curvature estimate, whereas correction needs the sampler retrained. We turn this into a pre-flight check that consumes a few percent of the sampler's own training budget and uses only $E$, $\nabla E$ and $\nabla^2 E$. On Gaussian-mixture, Many-Well and rotated anisotropic Many-Well targets with exactly computable ground truth, the check estimates the missing mass to within $10^{-3}$ at $2.7\%$ of training cost, where a tuned annealed SMC reference needs $70$--$280\%$ of training cost to do worse. It also applies unchanged to a controlled-SDE sampler that has no tractable density, where ESS and the ELBO cannot be formed at all. The estimator carries a \emph{self-diagnostic} that, without ground truth, is conservative in the safe direction: across $60$ configurations it clears $16$, of which $15$ are accurate to $10^{-2}$ or better. We are explicit about what this does and does not license: the check cheaply produces evidence of missing mass, and sometimes evidence that the search has stabilised, but it cannot certify a run, and its thresholds are heuristic. We then map the boundary of the method on a real physical landscape, LJ-13, and report where it fails and why.

</details>


### [367] [Disaggregated Quantization: Specializing LLM Prefill and Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26333)
*Andrei Panferov,Maximilian Kleinegger,Sweta Priyadarshi,Tijmen Blankevoort,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“解耦量化”(DQ)，将预填充与解码的量化格式、权重和存储位置分别特化，在Qwen 3与Gemma 3上兼顾精度与推理效率，并通过ODP将预填充权重卸载至SSD，实现27B模型1.78倍TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预填充(prefill)与解码(decode)对量化的需求不同：低精度算术可加速提示处理，而紧凑权重可降低生成阶段的内存流量。传统统一量化难以同时优化两个阶段，因此需要针对阶段特化量化策略。

Method: 提出解耦量化(DQ)：分别特化计算格式、权重与存储放置。具体包括：在解码阶段取消激活量化以提升解码密集任务精度；训练独立的计算原生预填充权重以加速提示处理并匹配/超越仅权重量化精度；发布Qwen3.8-27B GGUF解码器并训练NVFP4预填充器；用离载解耦预填充(ODP)从SSD流式加载预填充权重，按提示长度摊薄加载开销；在vLLM中评估解耦服务精度，并通过PTQ验证共享权重格式解耦，规模至2.8T参数。

Result: 在Qwen 3与Gemma 3上，解码阶段取消激活量化在不增加推理成本下提升解码密集任务精度；训练独立计算原生预填充权重相对仅权重量化加速提示处理，并在2-3-bit解码下于解码密集与预填充密集任务上匹配或超越其精度。基于Qwen3.8-27B GGUF解码器训练NVFP4预填充器，在不修改解码检查点情况下，MMLU-Pro 1-bit精度提升32.5分，MMMU-Pro提升35.3分。ODP在8K提示长度下于llama.cpp中相比仅权重基线实现1.78倍TTFT加速。

Conclusion: 解耦量化可针对预填充与解码分别优化，在保持或提升精度的同时降低推理成本；ODP通过SSD流式加载解决单设备额外检查点问题并显著加速长提示首token生成；方法在vLLM服务与最高2.8T参数模型上得到验证，具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Prefill and decode reward different approaches to quantization: low-precision arithmetic accelerates prompt processing, while compact weights reduce memory traffic during generation. We propose "disaggregated quantization" (DQ), which specializes computation formats, weights and storage placement to both of these phases. On Qwen 3 and Gemma 3, removing activation quantization specifically on decode improves accuracy on decode-heavy tasks without increasing inference cost. Training separate compute-native prefill weights accelerates prompt processing relative to weight-only inference while matching or exceeding its accuracy at 2-3-bit decode on both decode-heavy and prefill-heavy tasks. With released Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoders, training an NVFP4 prefiller improves 1-bit accuracy by 32.5 points on MMLU-Pro and 35.3 on MMMU-Pro without modifying the decode checkpoint. To accommodate the additional checkpoint on a single device, offloaded disaggregated prefill (ODP) streams its weights from SSD, amortizing loading over prompt length. On the same 27B model, ODP delivers a 1.78x time-to-first-token speedup over the weight-only baseline at 8K prompt length in llama.cpp. We evaluate accuracy under disaggregated serving in vLLM and further validate shared-weight format disaggregation through post-training quantization on models up to 2.8T parameters.

</details>


### [368] [Geometry-Aware Hyperbolic Residual Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26342)
*Alessio Colombo,Melika Ayoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种几何感知的双曲残差量化方法，在前向传播中恢复残差量化在庞加莱球上的望远镜式聚合行为，在反向传播中用折扣双曲直通估计器将重建梯度作为单一几何块路由，从而避免残差阶段间不稳定的递归梯度传输，提升了双曲残差码的稳定性与结构组织性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差向量量化（RVQ）能将连续表示转为离散的多层级 token 序列，但多数方法在欧氏空间中进行，忽视了所得码本的粗到细结构以及许多数据域中固有的潜在层次。双曲几何天然适合层次表示，但朴素的双曲扩展会引入几何不一致：非结合的双曲加法导致残差聚合不一致，标准直通梯度估计忽略了潜在空间的几何结构。

Method: 提出几何感知的双曲残差量化，同时处理前向与反向传播。前向：双曲残差聚合（Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation）在庞加莱球上恢复残差量化的望远镜式（telescoping）行为；反向：折扣双曲直通估计器（discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator）将重建梯度作为单一几何块通过量化器路由，避免跨残差阶段的不稳定递归梯度传输。

Result: 在层次预测、推荐、图像分词与神经音频编码任务上评估，相比朴素双曲基线，方法提升了双曲残差码的稳定性与结构组织性。同时观察到明显的结构—压缩权衡：纯压缩场景下欧氏残差量化仍更优，而几何感知双曲量化对层次化组织的离散潜在空间最有用。

Conclusion: 几何感知的双曲残差量化通过在前向与反向传播中引入几何一致性，改善了双曲残差码的稳定性与层次结构组织。但存在结构—压缩权衡：欧氏 RVQ 适合纯压缩，双曲量化适合层次化的离散潜在空间。

Abstract: Residual Vector Quantization turns continuous representations into discrete, multi-level token sequences. Yet most methods operate in Euclidean space, despite the coarse-to-fine structure of the resulting codes and the latent hierarchies present in many data domains. Hyperbolic geometry offers a natural alternative for hierarchical representations, but naive hyperbolic extensions introduce geometric inconsistencies: non-associative hyperbolic addition prevents consistent residual aggregation, while standard straight-through gradient estimation ignores the geometry of the latent space. We propose a geometry-aware hyperbolic residual quantization that addresses these issues in both the forward and backward passes. In the forward pass, Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation restores the telescoping behavior of residual quantization on the Poincare ball. In the backward pass, a discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator routes the reconstruction gradient through the quantizer as a single geometric block, avoiding unstable recursive gradient transport across residual stages. Evaluations on hierarchical prediction, recommendation, image tokenization, and neural audio coding tasks show that our method improves the stability and structural organization of hyperbolic residual codes over naive hyperbolic baselines. At the same time, we observe a clear structure-compression trade-off: Euclidean residual quantization remains preferable for pure compression, while geometry-aware hyperbolic quantization is most useful for hierarchically organized discrete latent spaces.

</details>


### [369] [Double Descent and Malign Overfitting in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26392)
*Raphaël Urfin,Tony Bonnaire,Giulio Biroli,Marc Mézard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了扩散模型在过参数化下“灾难性过拟合”与深度学习中“良性过拟合”之间的悖论：在每样本固定 m 个噪声实现时，插值峰出现在 p∼nm 而非 p∼n，但测试损失早在 p∼n 就开始上升，原因是偏差开始增长并趋向记忆训练集的 empirical score；不过，配合岭惩罚或早停等正则化后，大模型仍可优于未正则化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为深度学习中过参数化是良性的：模型更大泛化更好，即使无正则化，插值模型也能泛化，测试误差呈双下降曲线。扩散模型训练可归结为回归，即最小化二次分数匹配损失，因此人们可能期望同样出现良性过拟合；但实际观察到相反现象：过拟合是灾难性的，会将模型推向记忆化机制。论文旨在解释这一悖论。

Method: 结合两类手段：1）在 CelebA 上训练 U-Net 进行实验；2）构建随机特征模型，并推导闭式学习曲线。设定每个训练样本有固定数量 m 个噪声实现，进行偏差-方差分解以定位机制。

Result: 实验与理论表明：插值峰确实会出现，但位置在 p∼nm，而非标准回归中的 p∼n；然而测试损失的上升更早，在 p∼n 处开始，且与 m 无关。该过拟合是 malign 的：尽管训练的隐式正则化完全起作用，它仍驱使模型逼近经验分数（empirical score），从而记忆训练集，而非逼近真实分数。偏差-方差分解显示：分数估计器的偏差在 p∼n 处开始增长；越过峰值后方差像回归中一样衰减，但偏差持续增长，二者最终饱和于较大值。由于扩散模型通常 m≫1，插值峰被推到非常大的模型规模，因此实际模型位于峰值前的上升分支，此时恶性过拟合已经发生。

Conclusion: 过参数化本身在扩散模型中并非良性，会因偏差增长导致记忆化；但当与正则化结合时仍有益：在随机特征理论与 U-Net 实验中，通过岭惩罚或早停得到的最优正则化大模型，均优于任何未正则化模型。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom in deep learning holds that overparameterization---having more parameters $p$ than training samples $n$---is benign: larger models generalize better and, even without regularization, interpolating models generalize well, the test error following a double-descent curve. One might expect the same benign overfitting for diffusion models, whose training reduces to regression, i.e. to minimizing a quadratic score-matching loss. Yet the opposite is observed: overfitting here is catastrophic, driving the model into a memorization regime.
